# MSCD Platform Build Plan

| | |
|---|---|
| Purpose | The implementation plan that AI coding agents build the MSCD platform from: decisions, layout, schema, API, jobs, security, seed, web integration, deployment, tests and ordered work packages |
| Inputs | `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` (target architecture), `docs/platform/ui-contract.md` (what the current UI reads and changes, cited as "UI 2.3"), `docs/platform/spec-digest.md` (TS-MSCD-01 and PRD-MSCD-01 v2.4, cited as "TS 6.1", "PRD 9.2" and "A-05" for its ambiguity list) |
| Scope of this build | Monorepo, NestJS server with run modes `api`, `worker`, `storage-gateway` and `migrate`, PostgreSQL schema with RLS, local, OIDC and SAML sign-in with MFA for every user, signed packs and licences with trust anchors compiled into the release build, Managed Vault, Microsoft 365 and Google Drive adapters tested against local mocks, read model with exact parity to the current sample UI, commands for every UI action, the P0 registers and workflows of the MVP and v1.0 (risks, treatments, residual acceptance, findings, corrective actions, self-assessment, audit activity planner, management review, context, interested parties, assets, metrics, evidence records), worker jobs, Compose, Helm, offline bundle and tests at every layer |
| Out of scope | Listed in Appendix 1 (deferred items, each with the product owner sign-off it needs). Deviations from `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` and the specification are listed in Appendix 2 |
| Build environment | Linux container, 4 CPUs, 15 GB RAM, Node 22.22 and npm 10, Docker 29 with Compose v5 and Docker Hub pulls, Playwright Chromium build 1194 in `/opt/pw-browsers`. Containers reach the npm registry and GitHub through a TLS-inspecting proxy (CA bundle `/root/.ccr/ca-bundle.crt`, rule R-12); ghcr.io blob downloads are refused. Nested Kubernetes (k3s in Docker) cannot start pods on this host (cgroup v1), so Helm checks here are static (J.1). No Microsoft 365, Google or Entra ID tenant. At most 2 agents work at the same time |

## 0. Rules for implementing agents

These rules apply to every work package (WP) in section K.

| # | Rule |
|---|---|
| R-01 | Work only inside the paths your WP owns. You may read anything. A path owned by a finished WP may be edited only when your WP lists it. If your brief needs a change outside your paths, stop and write the need in your handoff note. |
| R-02 | Two WPs run at the same time at most. WPs in one wave never own the same file. Shared files (root `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `tsconfig.base.json`, `eslint.config.mjs`, `packages/shared/src/index.ts`, `packages/shared/src/api/routes.ts`, `apps/server/src/app/*.module.ts`, `apps/server/vitest.*.config.ts`, every Compose file) have exactly one owner per wave, named in K.1. |
| R-03 | Dependencies are installed by the WP marked **lockfile owner** only. WP-01 and WP-03 are the only lockfile owners in this plan. If you find a missing package, do not run `npm install`: write the need in your handoff note; the orchestrator adds it between waves with `npm install <pkg>@<version> -w <workspace>` while no agent is working. |
| R-04 | Never hand-edit `apps/web/src/views/*.jsx` (except `util.js`, which is not generated) or `apps/web/src/hover.css`. They are generated by `npm run gen:views -w @mscd/web` from `project/MSCD Mockup.dc.html`. Every web WP runs `npm run gen:views:check -w @mscd/web`, which regenerates the views and `hover.css` into a temporary folder and fails on any difference. |
| R-05 | Fixed ports used by automated checks: sample E2E 4173; WP-03 api start check 3000; API E2E web 4174, api 3174, mock providers 4310, gateway 4311; WP-01 image check 18081; WP-18 image smoke 18082; Compose stacks (WP-28, full-stack smoke) 18080, 18443 and Keycloak 18088; bundle install check 18090 and 18453; dev stack 55432, 58333, 56379, 58080, 51025, 58025. Databases, object stores and Keycloak in Vitest suites run in containers on random ports (Testcontainers). No two WPs of one wave use the same fixed port. |
| R-06 | Docker Hub allows about 100 pulls per hour from this host, shared by all agents. Use only the images in A.3. Never use `:latest`. Testcontainers runs with `TESTCONTAINERS_RYUK_DISABLED=true` (no extra image) and tests clean up containers labelled `mscd.test=true` in global teardown. |
| R-07 | Status codes, enum values and table names are exactly as written in this plan. A disagreement with `ui-contract.md` or `spec-digest.md` is resolved by the decision tables in A.4 and A.5 and the deviations in Appendix 2. |
| R-08 | Documentation you write follows the project writing rules: plain English, no em dash and no centre dot characters, no notes about the writing itself. `npm run check:text` fails on the two characters. |
| R-09 | Definition of done: every acceptance command of the WP passes from a clean checkout of the files (`npm ci` already run; generated and git-ignored outputs such as `packs/dist` are rebuilt by the scripts that need them, never assumed), the WP's handoff note is written to `docs/platform/handoff/WP-nn.md` (what was built, deviations, follow-ups), and no file outside the owned paths changed (`git status --porcelain` reviewed). |
| R-10 | Do not commit unless the orchestrator says so. Do not push. |
| R-11 | Secrets in the repository are development keys only and live in the folders named in this plan: `packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/`, `apps/server/test/fixtures/secrets/`, `deploy/compose/config/demo-secrets/`, `deploy/compose/dev/`. Production keys never enter the repository. Every development key id is listed in `packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/dev-key-ids.json` (a JSON array, read by `packages/pack-tools/src/dev-key-ids.ts`, which exports `DEV_KEY_IDS`). `deploy/scripts/check-release-artefacts.sh` hashes every file in the folders above when it runs, so no list of development secrets has to be maintained. |
| R-12 | Every image build passes the build CA secret: `docker buildx build ... --secret id=build_ca,src="${MSCD_BUILD_CA:?}"`. In this sandbox `MSCD_BUILD_CA=/root/.ccr/ca-bundle.crt` (containers reach the npm registry through a TLS-inspecting proxy); elsewhere it points to an empty file (`MSCD_BUILD_CA=/dev/null` is not accepted by BuildKit, so scripts create an empty temporary file when the variable is unset). Every Dockerfile `RUN` step that calls `npm` or `apk` mounts `--mount=type=secret,id=build_ca` and exports `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS=/run/secrets/build_ca` (npm) or appends it to a temporary CA file (`apk`) when the file is not empty, exactly as `deploy/docker/web.Dockerfile` (the former `app/Dockerfile`) does. Scripts that build images (`deploy/docker/smoke.sh`, `tools/smoke`, `deploy/scripts/build-bundle.sh`) accept `--build-ca <file>` and default to `$MSCD_BUILD_CA`. |
| R-13 | Release artefacts (the `mscd-server` release image, the offline bundle) never contain a development key id, a development secret, `profiles/demo.yaml`, `profiles/test.yaml` or the demo seed data. `deploy/scripts/check-release-artefacts.sh` (WP-33) enforces this and runs in CI. |

## A. Final decisions

### A.1 Runtime, language and tooling

| Concern | Decision | Version (pin in `package.json`) | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| Node.js | Node 22 LTS for this build. Every `package.json` has `"engines": {"node": ">=22"}`. `.nvmrc` holds `22.22.0`. Images use `node:22-alpine` (already present locally), pinned by digest at release | 22.22 | Matches the build environment. Dependencies below need 22.12 or later (pg-boss, `require()` of ES modules). The move to Node 24 is a later roadmap item (ARCHITECTURE 4.1) |
| Package manager | npm 10 workspaces: `apps/*`, `packages/*`, `tools/*`. One root `package-lock.json` | npm 10.9 | Already used by the project; no extra tool |
| Language | TypeScript for all new code. The web controller and generated views stay JavaScript in this build | `typescript` 5.9.3 | TypeScript 7 is out but NestJS dependency injection relies on `emitDecoratorMetadata`, which 5.9 supports |
| Server module format | `apps/server` compiles to CommonJS (`"type": "commonjs"`, `module` and `moduleResolution` `nodenext`). ES-module-only packages (for example `openid-client`) load through `require()`, which Node 22.12 or later supports and TypeScript 5.8 or later type-checks under `nodenext` | | NestJS is most stable on CommonJS |
| Shared package format | `packages/shared` is an ES module package with type declarations, consumed by the web (Vite) and the server (`require()` of ES modules). Vite and Vitest alias `@mscd/shared` to `packages/shared/src` so no build step is needed during development | | One source of schemas |
| Server framework | NestJS 11 on Fastify | `@nestjs/core`, `@nestjs/common`, `@nestjs/platform-fastify`, `@nestjs/testing` 11.2.7; `fastify` 5.11.3 (the version `@nestjs/platform-fastify` 11.2.7 pins) | ARCHITECTURE 4 |
| Fastify plugins | Cookies, security headers, rate limiting, multipart | `@fastify/cookie` 11.1.2, `@fastify/helmet` 13.1.1, `@fastify/rate-limit` 10.3.0, `@fastify/multipart` 9.4.0 | Fastify 5 compatible majors |
| Database access | Kysely with the `pg` driver | `kysely` 0.28.17, `pg` 8.23.1, `@types/pg` 8.23.1 | ARCHITECTURE 4 |
| Migrations | Plain SQL files under `apps/server/migrations/{platform,ref,tenant}`, run by the Kysely `Migrator` through a custom `SqlFileMigrationProvider`. Forward only (expand and contract). `allowUnorderedMigrations: true` | | DBAs can read them (ARCHITECTURE 9.3) |
| Contracts | zod schemas in `packages/shared`, reused by server validation, web client types and the OpenAPI document (generated with zod's built-in `z.toJSONSchema`) | `zod` 4.6.5 | One definition (ARCHITECTURE 13) |
| Jobs | `JobQueue` interface with two drivers: pg-boss (default) and BullMQ on Valkey (`jobs.driver: bullmq`) | `pg-boss` 12.35.1, `bullmq` 5.81.5, `ioredis` 5.11.1 | ARCHITECTURE 7 |
| Object storage client | AWS SDK v3 | `@aws-sdk/client-s3` and `@aws-sdk/lib-storage` 3.1144.0 | Any S3-compatible store |
| Identity | Local accounts with argon2id and TOTP; OIDC through `openid-client`; SAML 2.0 through `@node-saml/node-saml` | `@node-rs/argon2` 2.2.1, `openid-client` 6.8.8, `@node-saml/node-saml` 5.1.0 | TOTP is implemented in-house on `node:crypto` (RFC 6238, about 40 lines, tested with the RFC vectors), so no TOTP library |
| Provider HTTP | Microsoft Graph and Google Drive are called with `undici` `fetch` against configurable base URLs (an `undici` `Agent` also carries the mTLS client certificate to the gateway). Token requests use signed JWT client assertions built with `jose` | `undici` 7.30.0, `jose` 6.2.12 | The same code runs against the real APIs and the local mocks |
| Signatures | Ed25519 through `node:crypto` for packs, licences and audit anchors; trusted public keys compiled into the build (D-16) | | No dependency |
| Pack archives | `.pack` file is a gzipped tar | `tar` 7.5.22 | Readable with standard tools |
| Mail | Nodemailer over SMTP | `nodemailer` 7.0.13 | `mail.transport: smtp` |
| Exports and datasets | XLSX through `exceljs` 4.4.0, PDF through `pdf-lib` 1.17.1, CSV through `csv-stringify` 6.9.0 and `csv-parse` 6.2.1, iCalendar through `ical-generator` 8.1.1 | | ARCHITECTURE 4 |
| QR codes | `qrcode` 1.5.4 (MFA enrolment page, rendered as SVG in the browser) | | |
| Logging and metrics | pino through `nestjs-pino`; Prometheus through `prom-client`; OpenTelemetry tracing off by default | `nestjs-pino` 4.6.1, `pino` 9.14.0, `pino-http` 10.5.0, `prom-client` 15.1.3, `@opentelemetry/sdk-node` and `@opentelemetry/exporter-trace-otlp-http` 0.222.0, `@opentelemetry/auto-instrumentations-node` 0.80.0 | ARCHITECTURE 12 |
| CLI | `commander` | 14.0.3 | Run modes and admin commands |
| YAML | `yaml` | 2.9.1 | Deployment profile |
| Unit and integration tests | Vitest with SWC for decorator metadata; Testcontainers for PostgreSQL, SeaweedFS, Valkey and Keycloak | `vitest` and `@vitest/coverage-v8` 3.2.7, `unplugin-swc` 2.0.0, `@swc/core` 1.16.13, `testcontainers` and `@testcontainers/postgresql` 11.14.0 | esbuild drops decorator metadata, SWC keeps it. Vitest 3.2 accepts Vite 5, so the workspace keeps one Vite (the web's 5.4.21); Vitest 4 would need Vite 6 or later |
| Web E2E | Playwright pinned to the installed browser build | `@playwright/test` 1.56.1 (exact) | Chromium 1194 is preinstalled; a newer Playwright would need a download |
| Web stack | Unchanged in this build: React 18.3.1, Vite 5.4.21, `@vitejs/plugin-react` 4.7.0. New web data and auth files are TypeScript | | Keeps the E2E suite green; framework upgrades are a later step |
| Build flavours | `MSCD_BUILD_FLAVOR` `dev` (default) or `release`, read by `apps/server/scripts/gen-build-info.mjs` at build, typecheck and test time (D-16, D-24) | | Trust anchors and demo content never depend on runtime configuration |
| Lint | ESLint flat config with `typescript-eslint` | `eslint` 9.39.5, `typescript-eslint` 8.71.0, `@eslint/js` 9.39.x | |
| Mock provider servers | `tools/mock-providers`: Fastify application emulating the Microsoft identity token endpoint, the Graph drive item API and the Google OAuth token and Drive v3 API subsets MSCD uses | | No real tenant exists; adapters are built against the real API shapes |

### A.2 Product and architecture decisions taken for this build

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| D-01 | Run modes: `mscd-server api`, `worker`, `storage-gateway`, `migrate`, plus admin commands `db bootstrap`, `tenant create`, `tenant verify-domain`, `packs install`, `keys generate`, `keys rewrap`, `admin create-user`, `seed demo`. Images: `cyberlentera/mscd-server` (release flavour), `cyberlentera/mscd-server-demo` (dev flavour, demo and tests only, never in the offline bundle), `cyberlentera/mscd-web` (D-24). |
| D-02 | Tenancy: one PostgreSQL schema per organisation (`org_<slug>`), Row-Level Security on `organization_id` in every tenant table, `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`, application role `mscd_app` that owns nothing. Same layout on-premise (one organisation per database). Organisation-scoped platform tables also carry RLS (D-31). |
| D-03 | Every request runs in one transaction that sets `search_path` to the tenant schema and `ext` (the schema that holds `pg_trgm`; `public` is never on the path and nobody but the migrator may create objects in a schema on the path), and `app.org_id`, `app.user_id`, `app.request_id` with `set_config(..., true)` (transaction local). Platform and reference tables are always schema-qualified in queries (`platform.app_user`, `ref.requirement`). Every function in `platform` is created with `SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform`. |
| D-04 | Owner scoping (TS 7.1: a Domain Owner sees only records they own) is enforced by one application service, `ScopePolicy` (F.7), on every read path: `/ui/*`, the TS 9.2 endpoints, register lists, files (`open`, `content`, `redirect`), evidence packs, chains, exports, notifications and the audit trail. It is not an RLS policy in this build because derived values (chain counts, orphan flags, readiness) need the full data of the system before scoping. This deviates from ARCHITECTURE 9.1 step 4 (Appendix 2, X-01). Tenant isolation stays an RLS policy. |
| D-05 | Parity first: `GET /api/v1/ui/org` and `GET /api/v1/ui/ms/{ms}` return exactly the `Snapshot` shapes of UI 4.2. The TS 9.2 per-screen endpoints are projections of the same derivation functions (WP-32). |
| D-06 | Values that the specification does not derive and the sample fabricates (UI 2.14, 2.15, 5.14, 5.17) are stored columns, written by the seed and editable later. They are listed in G.4. |
| D-07 | `today` is the organisation's local date (time zone column, default `Asia/Jakarta`). The profile key `demo.fixed_today` overrides it for demo and test installs only (`2026-09-29`, the sample's `TODAY_S`). |
| D-08 | Repository credentials are sealed by `storage-gateway` with the tenant data key and stored as ciphertext in `storage_credential.sealed_blob`; the api passes the sealed blob, the wrapped tenant key and the repository descriptor on each call and cannot open the blob. The seal's additional authenticated data is `cred:<orgId>:<repositoryId>:<sha256 of the canonical repository config>`, recomputed by the gateway from the descriptor of every call, so a credential cannot be used with another organisation, repository, drive or root folder (ARCHITECTURE 10.4 "Binding"). TS 4.5 and ARCHITECTURE 10.4 say the gateway fetches credentials itself; storing sealed blobs in the tenant schema is a deviation (Appendix 2, X-02). A Vault KV driver is deferred. |
| D-09 | Two key-encryption keys (KEKs): `platform.key` for api and worker (TOTP secrets, IdP client secrets, calendar and redirect token MACs) and `storage.key` for the gateway only (tenant data keys, which encrypt vault objects and seal credentials). Each container receives only its own key file (I.3, I.4). The `KeyProvider` interface has one driver in this build, `file`. The profile schema rejects `kms.provider` values `cloud_kms`, `vault` and `hsm` at start-up with the message "kms.provider <value> is not available in this build; use file". SaaS in this build uses the `file` driver with the KEKs delivered as Kubernetes Secrets mounted into the api, worker and gateway Deployments separately (deviation from TS 2.4, Appendix 2, X-03). |
| D-10 | Managed Vault objects are immutable keys (`tenants/<org_id>/<object_id>`), encrypted by the gateway with AES-256-GCM in 64 KiB chunks under a per-object content key wrapped by the tenant data key. A new version is a new object. Bucket versioning is optional defence in depth, not required. |
| D-11 | Golden Thread chains are stored instances (`trace_chain_instance`, `trace_chain_node`), seeded from the sample `CHAINS`. Record up and down lists are stored per record (`record_reference`). The generated recursive CTE over `traceability_chain_def` is deferred. |
| D-12 | Chain item status uses the sample rule (UI 2.3): `missing` when the holder is unassigned and no due date is set (or the source is missing), `overdue` when the due date is past, else `pending`. A record is open while its `pending_action` is not null. The TS 6.9 entity-state derivations are deferred. |
| D-13 | Activity `locked` is derived: an activity is shown `locked` when its stored status is `not_started` and the previous phase is not fully verified. This reproduces every sample `PHASE_STATE` and keeps TS 6.6 (locked is never stored). Critical gates (`is_gate_phase`) additionally reject `not_started -> in_progress` for activities in later phases (BR-12). |
| D-14 | The pack set for the demo is generated from the sample: `ISO27001-2022` 1.2.0, `ISO9001-2026` 0.9.0, `ISO9001-2015` 1.4.2, `ISO22301-2019` 1.0.0, `ISO37001-2025` 0.6.0 (pre-release), `ISO37001-2016` 1.0.0 (withdrawn). Packs hold short titles only, never ISO text. |
| D-15 | The licence is canonical JSON (keys sorted recursively, no whitespace, `signature` removed) signed with Ed25519. It names the organisation by display name and binds to the installation (`installation_id`, on-premise) or to the organisation (`organization_id`, SaaS); a licence bound to another installation or organisation is rejected (422 `LIC-BINDING`). Unbound licences are accepted only when the document carries `"trial": true` and `valid_until` is at most 90 days after `valid_from`. Entitlements and state are derived on every evaluation from the verified document and the clock only (cached in memory by document hash); no derived column is stored. `max_users` counts active, non-guest, non-system members of the organisation; every path that creates or enables a non-guest member (admin routes, invitations, JIT provisioning, `admin create-user`, the seed) calls `LicenceGate.assertCanAddUser` in the same transaction (rule `LIC-USERS`). Creating a management system beyond `max_management_systems` is rejected (rule `LIC-MS`). Features are checked with `LicenceGate.assertFeature(name)` (rule `LIC-FEATURE`): `auditor_guest_access` for auditor grants, `integrated_mode` for entity links to a second system, `link_health_monitor` for link health runs and schedules, `edition_transition` for setting a transition target. A clock high-water mark (`platform.clock_mark`) is advanced hourly; when the clock is more than 24 hours behind it, the licence is evaluated at the high-water mark, the state is at least `grace`, an operator warning is logged and `licence.clock_rollback` is audited. During grace, everything works and a warning banner is raised; after grace, read-only mode (BR-37) blocks all user writes, while jobs and exports keep running (A-39, A-40). |
| D-16 | Packs are signed with Ed25519 keys separate from the licence keys (A-17). Trusted public keys are compiled into the server build (`apps/server/src/generated/build-info.ts`, written by `scripts/gen-build-info.mjs`, B.4); there is no profile key or environment variable for them. Release builds (`MSCD_BUILD_FLAVOR=release`) take the keys from the directory named by `MSCD_TRUSTED_KEYS_DIR` (release CI injects only CyberLentera keys); the generator fails when that directory is missing or holds a development key id. Development builds (`dev`, the default for local work and the demo image) trust the keys in `packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/`. `verifyLicence` and `verifyPack` in a release build reject any key id listed in `DEV_KEY_IDS`. |
| D-17 | Pack install runs with the database role `mscd_pack_installer` (insert and update on `ref`), used only by `migrate`, `packs install`, the worker's `pack.install` job and the installation-wide `packs.lifecycle` job. The api role cannot write `ref`. With `tenancy.mode = multi` only the Platform Operator installs packs (`pack.install` at platform scope); Tenant Admins choose which installed edition a system uses (`pack.activate`). With `tenancy.mode = single` the Tenant Admin installs packs (ARCHITECTURE 9.2). |
| D-18 | Display strings that combine data (for example the pack card `v` text) are composed by the server read model in English and Indonesian, using the formats in G.5, so the UI shows the same text as today. |
| D-19 | Sessions are server-side rows in `platform.session` (both job drivers), referenced by an opaque random cookie. No tokens in the browser. |
| D-20 | Demo mode (`demo.enabled: true`) allows: the persona switcher (`POST /auth/demo/persona`), `organization.demo_deploy_label` (per-organisation SaaS or on-premise label, so the seeded "PT Bank Sentosa Digital" behaves as on-premise as in the sample), `demo.fixed_today`, `identity.oidc.allow_insecure_http` and the demo Keycloak provider. Demo mode is refused at start-up unless the profile also sets `demo.acknowledge_insecure: true`, and is refused in every release-flavour build. |
| D-21 | Repository `mode` (TS single value) becomes `modes text[]` with the primary mode first, because one library serves several modes (A-36; sample "Link, Pass-through"). |
| D-22 | Notifications: email and in-app only (TS 10.2). Teams and Slack deferred. |
| D-23 | LDAP sign-in is deferred (not needed for SaaS; on-premise customers use OIDC or SAML through their IdP in this build). |
| D-24 | Build flavours. `release`: trusted keys from `MSCD_TRUSTED_KEYS_DIR`, packs signed with the release pack key (build secret `pack_signing_key`), no `demo.yaml`, `test.yaml` or seed data in the image, demo mode refused. `dev`: development keys, development-signed packs, demo and test profiles and the seed snapshot included; published only as `cyberlentera/mscd-server-demo`. `server.Dockerfile` has the targets `release` (the default, last stage) and `demo`. |
| D-25 | External identities are never linked to an account by email. A pair (identity provider, subject) is linked only by (a) an invitation token sent to the invited email and redeemed through a provider of the inviting organisation, (b) the user while signed in locally with MFA (`POST /me/identities/{slug}/link`), or (c) Tenant Admin pre-registration of the subject. Microsoft Entra ID providers key identities on `tid` and `oid`. JIT provisioning creates new accounts only, only into the provider's organisation, only when the provider's `allowed_email_domains` are all verified for that organisation by the platform operator (`platform.verified_domain`) and the token says `email_verified === true`. Details in F.8. |
| D-26 | A session is pinned to the organisation whose policy authenticated it (`session.auth_org_id`). `PUT /me/org` to an organisation that does not accept that authentication (its own identity providers, or local sign-in with MFA when its `local_login` policy allows it for the user) answers 409 `reauth-required` with the accepted methods, and the client signs in again. |
| D-27 | Guest auditor access: the access link carries its token in the URL fragment (never sent in a query string or logged), is single use and only starts an email one-time code check; the guest then enrols and uses TOTP. Each grant gets its own guest account (`kind = 'guest'`), so an auditor's existing account in another organisation is never reused silently. Revoking a grant ends the guest's sessions and role at once (F.14). |
| D-28 | Files are served by the api only as downloads: `Content-Disposition: attachment`, `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox`, `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, and a content type chosen by the server from the type the gateway detected in the file's first bytes, never from the client. Inline display is allowed only for PDF and raster images (F.13). |
| D-29 | Tasks created by business rules (BR-10, BR-11, BR-15, BR-31) are stored rows (`task`), opened and closed by the jobs that evaluate the rules, and merged with chain items in `GET /tasks` without changing the sample counts (C.9). |
| D-30 | Pack minor updates replace reference content (requirements, catalogue titles, labels) in place, as TS 3.2 step 4 allows. Activity templates are copied into `implementation_activity_progress` when a system is activated (title, clause, output code, expected outcome, pack version), so a minor update never rewrites a tenant's activities; offering template updates is deferred (Appendix 1). An older `pack_version` than the installed one for the same edition is rejected. |
| D-31 | Platform tables get narrow grants (C.4 `secure_platform_schema`), and the organisation-scoped ones (`org_membership`, `identity_provider`, `licence`, `audit_log`, `idempotency_key`, `session`, `invitation`, `verified_domain`) get forced RLS keyed on `app.org_id`, with an explicit platform scope (`app.platform_scope = 'on'`) that only authentication, operator, cross-tenant job and CLI code may set (F.10). `mscd_report` exists only when `tenancy.mode = single`, is created `NOLOGIN`, and never gets the credential, grant, token or audit tables. |
| D-32 | `license.validation: online` (SaaS) in this build means: the platform operator installs a signed entitlement document per organisation (`PUT /admin/license` at platform scope), verified exactly like an offline licence and bound to the organisation id. There is no runtime call to a licence service (deviation from ARCHITECTURE 10.8, Appendix 2, X-04). |
| D-33 | Audit chain anchors are signed by the worker with an Ed25519 key that never enters the database (`audit-anchor.key`) and are written outside the database: a structured log line for the customer's SIEM, an object in the Managed Vault through the gateway, and a downloadable signed anchor file (F.11). |
| D-34 | External auditors get a dedicated read projection limited to the audited system and to evidence inside its evidence packs; files open only as pack copies; `web_url` is never sent to them (F.6, F.7, G.5). Top Management gets no file access (TS 7.3). |

| Use | Image | Licence | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| PostgreSQL (Compose, tests) | `postgres:16.15-alpine` (reference); `postgres:15-alpine` and `postgres:17-alpine` only in the CI version matrix | PostgreSQL | TS 2.1 minimum 15, reference 16 |
| S3-compatible store (Compose, tests, Helm test values) | `chrislusf/seaweedfs:4.48`, started as `weed mini -dir=/data -s3.config=/etc/seaweedfs/s3.json -bucket=mscd-vault` | Apache 2.0 | Pullable from Docker Hub today and verified to run on this host (`weed version` 4.48). MinIO is rejected: the `minio/minio` repository is no longer available on Docker Hub (the registry answers "object not found") and MinIO is AGPLv3. Garage (`dxflrs/garage:v2.4.1`) is pullable but AGPLv3 and lacks bucket versioning; SeaweedFS is permissive, single binary, and its `mini` command creates the bucket and S3 identities at start |

| Helm static checks | `alpine/k8s:1.34.1` (contains `helm` and `kubeconform` 0.7.0), run with `--network host`, `HTTPS_PROXY` passed through and `$MSCD_BUILD_CA` mounted as the CA bundle so kubeconform can download the Kubernetes 1.34.1 schemas (verified on 1 Oct 2026) | Apache 2.0 | WP-29 sandbox acceptance (J.1) |
| Helm install test (nightly CI only) | `rancher/k3s:v1.34.1-k3s1` | Apache 2.0 | `deploy/helm/mscd/ci/k3s-test.sh`; cannot start pods on this host (cgroup v1, nested runc), so it never runs in a WP acceptance |
| Shell lint | `koalaman/shellcheck:stable` | GPL 3.0 (tool only, not shipped) | WP-28 and WP-33 |

### A.4 Resolution of UI contract section 5

| UI ref | Decision |
|---|---|
| 5.1 `complete` | Stored status `complete` added to `applicability_entry.implementation_status` between the working states and `implemented`, with `completed_by_user_id` and `completed_at`. `not_started`, `planned` and `partially_implemented` may go to `complete` in one command (one audit entry recording the from and to states). |
| 5.2 `blocked` | Not stored. `applicability_entry.holds_gate = true` makes the read model return `st: 'blocked'` and `gate: true`. The rules engine sets `holds_gate` for ISO 27001 and ISO 37001 when an applicable entry has no risk-based justification (BR-02); MS Managers can set it for other gate conditions (`PATCH .../applicability/{id}` with `holdsGate`, `gateReason`). |
| 5.3 integrity | The UI code mapping in C.12 is used for display, and "cannot be verified" lists keep the UI set (`changed`, `moved`, `missing`, `revoked`) for parity. Status downgrades (TS 6.1, BR-31) use TS 6.4 validity: valid is `intact`, `moved`, `version_changed`; `verification_pending` for 7 days; `access_denied` for 72 hours. Raised to the product owner (Appendix 2). |
| 5.4 verification checks | Requirement verify: actor holds `applicability.verify` and differs from `coalesce(completed_by_user_id, owner_user_id)`. Activity verify: actor differs from `completed_by_user_id` and from `owner_user_id` (TS 6.6). The "different date" rule applies only to corrective actions (BR-08, A-44). |
| 5.5 auditors verify | No. Only MS Manager and Programme Owner verify. In API mode the controller reads permissions from the snapshot and hides Verify for auditors. Sample-only E2E tests are tagged (J.6). |
| Programme Owner rights | The Programme Owner keeps `applicability.verify`, `activity.verify`, `document.approve` and `record.assign` (F.6), although A-16 proposes the MS Manager read set and TS 6.1 and 6.6 name the MS Manager as the performer. Reason: A-48 needs a second verifier when the MS Manager owns an activity, and the sample's Programme Owners (CISO, Head of Quality) approve documents. Raised to the product owner (Appendix 2, Q-9). |
| Licence and pack cards for other roles | `GET /ui/org` returns `LICENCE: null` without `license.read` and omits `PACKS` entries without `pack.read`. The controller renders the licence card and pack cards with `-` and empty lists when the data is absent (H.5 step 12), so the Framework library screen works for every role that can open it. |
| 5.6 pending per person | Everyone's pending list only for MS Manager and Programme Owner (TS 7.6). |
| 5.7 document upload | A new version is created `in_review`; the document moves to `in_review`; approval is a separate command by someone other than the uploader (BR-16). |
| 5.8 approve | `POST /ms/{ms}/documents/{id}/transitions {to: 'approved'}` added. The UI keeps the "Open review" toast in this build. |
| 5.9 forms | Commands exist; forms are later UI work. |
| 5.10 file input | Controller-owned hidden `<input type="file">` (H.4). |
| 5.11 gate items | Derived from current state on the server, so reassignment shows. |
| 5.12 `ns` to complete | Allowed in one command (two audit entries: `not_started -> in_progress`, `in_progress -> complete`). |
| 5.13 two counts | `criticalEvidence` and `unverifiableEvidence` are separate derivation functions. |
| 5.14 clause readiness | Stored in `clause_readiness` (seeded). The four maturity values use the sample formula over the stored value and live open counts until the product owner defines dimensions. |
| 5.15 findings and drift | Timeline findings list the system's certification audit findings (`finding.source = 'certification_audit'`), as the sample does. Drift rows come from `drift_result` for row rules and from live predicates for `unverif`, `nohash`, `metric`, `exercise`. |
| 5.16 role per system | `GET /me` returns roles and a UI role per system; the controller uses the role of the selected system. |
| 5.17 fabricated dues | Stored on the record (`action_due_at`, activity `due_at`, `verified_at`) and counted per stage from real rows. |
| 5.18 override scope | Not applicable on the API. |
| 5.19 greeting clock | The greeting keeps the browser clock (cosmetic). |

### A.5 Resolution of specification ambiguities needed now

| Id | Decision |
|---|---|
| A-01, A-07 | BR-01 to BR-20 are implemented as listed in the digest table 6.2. Effects chosen where the digest marks them unknown: BR-13 and BR-14 reject closing a management review whose mandatory inputs are empty for any linked system or that has no recorded decisions; BR-15 opens a task and notifies the owner when a finding passes `due_at`; BR-17 rejects a risk without an owner. BR-05 (risk appetite escalation, needs the A-33 risk profile format) and the BR-18 regression predicates for management review inputs and customer satisfaction are deferred (Appendix 1). BR-24 is a CHECK constraint; BR-25 to BR-28 wait for the framework workbenches (Appendix 1). |
| A-02, 5.10 | The v1.0 machines of digest 5.10 are implemented as explicit transition tables in C.13 (risk treatment, finding, corrective action, management review, document). |
| A-03 | `audit_event.outcome` values: `recommended`, `recommended_with_conditions`, `not_recommended`. |
| A-05 | `integrity_status`: `ok` for `intact` and `moved`; `degraded` for `version_changed`, `verification_pending` within 7 days, `access_denied` within 72 hours, and `hash_source = 'none'`; `broken` otherwise. |
| A-06 | Link health precedence: unreachable, not found, forbidden, hash mismatch (provider hash, same version), version changed or pinned version lost, moved, intact. `moved` updates the stored folder and is recorded only when no other state applies. |
| A-08 | Computed hashes (vault) are re-verified weekly by `integrity.rehash`. |
| A-09 | Evidence packs include evidence soft-deleted after the pack period start, flagged as deleted. |
| A-10 | Pack manifest exceptions: everything except `intact` and `moved`, with the reason (TS 5.6). |
| A-18, A-32 | Evidence without a storage object ("system link") has `external_ref`; its integrity is `ok` and freshness alone applies. |
| A-21 | Wire records carry business ids for display and UUID `ref` objects for commands. |
| A-22 | All date comparisons use the organisation's local date (D-07). |
| A-23 | `audit_prep_task` and `cycle_requirement_status`: `blocked -> in_progress` and `overdue -> in_progress` or `done` by the owner. |
| A-24 | Owner columns are nullable where BR-45 applies (activity progress, prep tasks, cycle items, chain records); null renders as Unassigned. |
| A-26 | BR-43 is a trigger on prep task insert and update. Moving an audit date earlier flags violating tasks (`violates_br43`) and notifies their owners instead of failing (BR-42). |
| A-27 | Gate items are derived (C.12, UI 2.4): applicability entries with `holds_gate`, plus chain records with `is_gate_item`. `blocker_gate_item_ref` holds the business id of one of them. |
| A-28 | `UNIQUE ... NULLS NOT DISTINCT` (PostgreSQL 15 or later). |
| A-29 | Partial unique index on the primary link plus a deferred constraint trigger requiring one primary. |
| A-37 | Routing precedence: most specific rule first (system, then classification, then object kind), then lowest `priority`. |
| A-41 | Every organisation gets a `managed_vault` repository at creation, used as the default until another is chosen. |
| A-42 | Thresholds: tenant table `freshness_threshold` overrides the defaults (fresh up to period + 1 day, aging up to 1.25 times period + 1 day, expired up to 2 times period, critical after). |
| A-48 | When the MS Manager owns the activity, another MS Manager or a Programme Owner verifies. |
| A-50 | BR-38 routing changes stay `pending_approval` until a second Tenant Admin approves; with a single Tenant Admin the change cannot be activated and the response says so. |

### B.2 Moving `app/` to `apps/web`

Done by WP-01 in wave 1, after the orchestrator confirms that no other agent is editing `app/`, `deploy/` or `docs/INSTALL-ONPREM.md`. Steps, in order:

1. Record the current versions: `cd app && npm ls react react-dom vite @vitejs/plugin-react @playwright/test htmlparser2 @fontsource/ibm-plex-sans @fontsource/ibm-plex-mono > /tmp/web-versions.txt`.
2. Move: `git mv app apps/web` when tracked, otherwise `mkdir -p apps && mv app apps/web`. Delete `apps/web/node_modules`, `apps/web/dist`, `apps/web/test-results`, `apps/web/e2e-report` and `apps/web/package-lock.json`.
3. `apps/web/package.json`: name `@mscd/web`, version unchanged, add `"engines": {"node": ">=22"}`, keep every dependency range, change `@playwright/test` to the exact `1.56.1`.
4. Root `package.json` with `workspaces: ["apps/*", "packages/*", "tools/*"]` and the root scripts of B.3 (those whose workspaces do not exist yet use `--if-present`). Run `npm install` at the root. Compare `npm ls` output with step 1; versions must match.
5. `apps/web/scripts/convert-template.mjs`: the template path becomes `path.resolve(root, '../../project/MSCD Mockup.dc.html')`. Add an `--out <dir>` option (default `src`) that redirects both outputs: the views go to `<out>/views/` and the hover rules to `<out>/hover.css` (today `hover.css` is written to a fixed path). `npm run gen:views -w @mscd/web` must leave `src/views` and `src/hover.css` unchanged.
6. `gen:views:check` (script `scripts/check-views.mjs`): runs the converter with `--out <tmpdir>`, then compares every `<tmpdir>/views/*.jsx` with `src/views/*.jsx` (the hand-written `src/views/util.js` is excluded and must still exist) and `<tmpdir>/hover.css` with `src/hover.css`; any missing, extra or different file exits 1 with the file names.
7. `playwright.config.js` and `vite.config.js` stay as they are (paths are relative to `apps/web`).
8. Move `app/Dockerfile` to `deploy/docker/web.Dockerfile` with the repository root as build context: a `manifests` stage copies every `package.json` (excluding `node_modules`) so `npm ci` runs against the root lockfile, then `npm run build -w @mscd/web`, then the unchanged NGINX runtime stage. Keep the `build_ca` secret mounts of the current file (R-12). Update every `app/` reference in `deploy/scripts/build-bundle.sh` (find them with `grep -n 'app/' deploy/scripts/build-bundle.sh`; on 1 Oct 2026 there were five: the `--version` help text, the `-f .../app/Dockerfile` build argument and its build context, the base image advice message, the version read from `app/package.json`, and the uncommitted-files warning) and in `deploy/README.md` (the line naming `app/Dockerfile`).
9. Root `.gitignore`: `node_modules/`, `apps/*/dist/`, `packages/*/dist/`, `tools/*/dist/`, `apps/web/test-results/`, `apps/web/e2e-report/`, `packs/dist/`, `deploy/dist/`, `*.tsbuildinfo`, `apps/server/src/generated/`, `deploy/helm/mscd/ci/.schema-cache/`.
10. Run the full E2E suite from the new place with `CI=1`, so Playwright never reuses a server that is already listening on 4173.


Each module folder holds `<name>.module.ts`, its controllers, services and a `README.md` of a few lines. Modules never import another module's internals; they use the exported service classes listed in the module file, or the injection tokens of `src/common/tokens.ts` (WP-08) whose interfaces are implemented later: `SESSION_RESOLVER` (identity, WP-11), `PERMISSION_EVALUATOR`, `SCOPE_POLICY`, `RECORD_RESOLVER`, `MEMBERSHIP_SERVICE`, `ME_ASSEMBLER` (authz, WP-39), `LICENCE_GATE` (license, WP-12), `GATEWAY_CLIENT` (storage, WP-13), `NOTIFICATION_WRITER`, `AUDIT_WRITER`, `JOB_QUEUE` (WP-08). WP-08 registers a default provider for each token in the module that will own it (deny-all permission evaluator, empty scope, permissive licence gate, minimal `Me`); the owning WP replaces the default inside its own folder.

`scripts/gen-build-info.mjs` (WP-03) reads `MSCD_BUILD_FLAVOR` (`dev` default, or `release`), `MSCD_VERSION`, `MSCD_REVISION` and `MSCD_TRUSTED_KEYS_DIR`, lists `migrations/platform/*.sql` for `MIGRATIONS_EXPECTED`, and writes `TRUSTED_KEYS: {kind: 'licence' | 'pack' | 'anchor', keyId, publicKeyPem}[]` from `<dir>/licence-*.pub`, `<dir>/pack-*.pub` and `<dir>/anchor-*.pub` (dev flavour default dir `packages/pack-tools/dev-keys`; a missing dev directory gives an empty list). In release flavour it exits 1 when the directory is missing, holds no licence or no pack key, or holds a key whose id appears in `packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/dev-key-ids.json`. Until WP-09 creates that file, the dev list is empty.

### B.5 Generated views and the controller

- `apps/web/src/views/*.jsx` stay generated. `scripts/convert-template.mjs` remains the only writer. Rule R-04 guards them.
- `apps/web/src/MscdApp.jsx` stays the controller. WP-07 moves derivations out of it into `src/data/sample-derive.js` and routes every action through `DataSource` commands without changing behaviour; WP-38 adds the boot router and auth pages around it; WP-25 adds API mode (H.5). `renderVals()` and the view-model shape do not change (UI parity rule).
- Splitting the controller into per-screen hooks, React Router and TypeScript views remain the later steps 2, 3 and 8 of ARCHITECTURE 14.2.

## C. Database

### C.1 Conventions

- PostgreSQL 16 reference, 15 minimum, 17 tested in CI (A.3). Extensions: `pg_trgm` only, installed in the dedicated schema `ext` (owned by `mscd_migrator`); operator classes are referenced as `ext.gin_trgm_ops`.
- Four kinds of schema: `platform` (installation-wide: organisations, users, sessions, licences, pack registry, platform audit log), `ref` (framework content from packs, read-only for the api), `org_<slug>` (one per organisation, all business data and its audit trail), `ext` (extensions). pg-boss keeps its own `pgboss` schema. Nobody but `mscd_migrator` may create objects in any of them, and `public` is never on a search path (D-03).
- Primary keys `uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()`. Business ids (`doc_code`, `evidence_code`, `code`) are unique among non-deleted rows of the schema (partial unique indexes).
- Standard columns on every tenant business table, written out in each `CREATE TABLE`: `id`, `organization_id` (default from `app.org_id`), `created_at`, `updated_at`, `created_by`, `updated_by`, `deleted_at`. The system user `00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001` (`handle = 'system'`) is the actor for jobs.
- Soft delete only, through the generic audited command of D.3 (`record.delete`, BR-19). `mscd_app` has no `DELETE` and no `TRUNCATE` on business tables. Technical tables that are cleaned up (`platform.rate_limit_bucket`, `platform.idempotency_key`, `platform.auth_transaction`, `platform.saml_assertion_seen`, `platform.job_outbox`) are the only exceptions.
- No `file_uri` column anywhere; files are reached through `storage_object_id`.
- Generic enum values are `varchar` with `CHECK` constraints, short `snake_case` (TS 1.1). Framework-specific enums (`doc_type`, `treatment_option`, `exercise_type`, `bribery_risk_level`, `due_diligence_subject`, `lookup` domains in general) are plain `varchar` columns validated by commands against `ref.lookup_value` of the system's active framework version (TS 1.1, ARCHITECTURE 8.3).
- Dates without time (`date`) are local dates of the organisation; instants are `timestamptz`.
- Every tenant migration file ends with `SELECT platform.secure_tenant_schema(current_schema());`, which grants privileges, enables and forces RLS, creates the `tenant_isolation` policy, the `updated_at` trigger and the `assert_member` trigger for every table, and raises an exception when a table has no `organization_id` column or ends without forced RLS.
- Every column that names a person (owner, holder, approver, recipient, assignee, verifier) is checked twice: by `MembershipService.assertMember` in the command (422 `NOT-A-MEMBER`) and by the `assert_member` trigger as a database backstop.

### C.2 Roles and bootstrap

| Role | Login | Rights |
|---|---|---|
| `mscd_migrator` | yes | Owns `platform`, `ref`, `ext`, `pgboss` and every `org_*` schema; runs migrations, seed and tenant creation |
| `mscd_app` | yes | Per-table grants on `platform` (C.4 `secure_platform_schema`); `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE` on tenant tables; `SELECT` on `ref`; `SELECT, INSERT` only on `platform.audit_log`, `platform.audit_anchor` and every `audit_trail`; used by `api` and `worker`. Default `search_path` is empty |
| `mscd_pack_installer` | yes | `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE` on `ref`, `platform.pack_install` and `platform.pack_catalog`; `SELECT, INSERT` on `platform.audit_log`; used only by pack installation (D-17) |
| `mscd_report` | `NOLOGIN` (the customer enables it) | Created only by `db bootstrap --with-report-role`, which refuses to run when the profile has `tenancy.mode: multi`. `SELECT` on tenant tables except `storage_credential`, `auditor_access_grant`, `calendar_feed_token`, `upload_intent` and `audit_trail`; sessions must `SET app.org_id`. Never granted anything in `platform` |

`apps/server/migrations/bootstrap/roles.sql` (the `db bootstrap` command replaces `{{...}}` with escaped literals and runs it with an administrator URL; `--with-report-role` also runs `bootstrap/report-role.sql`):

```sql
DO $bootstrap$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_migrator') THEN
    CREATE ROLE mscd_migrator LOGIN PASSWORD '{{migrator_password}}';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_app') THEN
    CREATE ROLE mscd_app LOGIN PASSWORD '{{app_password}}';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_pack_installer') THEN
    CREATE ROLE mscd_pack_installer LOGIN PASSWORD '{{pack_installer_password}}';
  END IF;
END
$bootstrap$;

GRANT CONNECT, CREATE ON DATABASE "{{database}}" TO mscd_migrator;
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE "{{database}}" TO mscd_app, mscd_pack_installer;
-- CVE-2018-1058: databases created before PostgreSQL 15 still let PUBLIC create objects in public.
REVOKE CREATE ON SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC;
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS ext AUTHORIZATION mscd_migrator;
REVOKE ALL ON SCHEMA ext FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA ext TO mscd_app, mscd_pack_installer;
DO $ext$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_extension e JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = e.extnamespace
             WHERE e.extname = 'pg_trgm' AND n.nspname <> 'ext') THEN
    ALTER EXTENSION pg_trgm SET SCHEMA ext;
  END IF;
END
$ext$;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm SCHEMA ext;
ALTER ROLE mscd_app SET statement_timeout = '30s';
ALTER ROLE mscd_app SET idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = '60s';
ALTER ROLE mscd_app SET search_path = '';
ALTER ROLE mscd_pack_installer SET search_path = '';
```

`apps/server/migrations/bootstrap/report-role.sql`:

```sql
DO $report$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_report') THEN
    CREATE ROLE mscd_report NOLOGIN;
  END IF;
END
$report$;
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE "{{database}}" TO mscd_report;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA ext TO mscd_report;
ALTER ROLE mscd_report SET search_path = '';
```

### C.3 Migration runner

- `SqlFileMigrationProvider(dir)` returns one Kysely migration per `NNNN_name.sql` file, sorted by name; `up` runs the whole file with `sql.raw(text).execute(db)` (simple query protocol, so several statements per file are allowed). No `down`.
- `mscd-server migrate` (role `mscd_migrator`): (1) `platform` files with `migrationTableSchema: 'platform'`; (2) `ref` files with `migrationTableSchema: 'ref'`; (3) pg-boss schema creation through `PgBoss` with `migrate: true` when `jobs.driver = pgboss`, then `GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA pgboss TO mscd_app; GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA pgboss TO mscd_app; GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA pgboss TO mscd_app; GRANT EXECUTE ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA pgboss TO mscd_app`; (4) for every row of `platform.organization` (not deleted), `tenant` files into `schema_name` with `migrationTableSchema` set to that schema, on a dedicated client (never the pool) whose session `search_path` is `"<schema>", ext` and which is closed after the schema is done; (5) installs the packs found in `packs.dir` (default `/app/packs` in the image) that are not yet installed (WP-12), using the pack installer role. Every step runs with `app.platform_scope = 'on'` set for the session of its dedicated client.
- Readiness: `api` and `worker` report not ready while (a) `platform.kysely_migration` lacks the newest platform migration name compiled into the image (`MIGRATIONS_EXPECTED` in `src/generated/build-info.ts`), or (b) a role other than `mscd_migrator`, superusers, predefined `pg_*` roles and the database owner holds `CREATE` on `public`, `ext`, `platform`, `ref` or an `org_*` schema, or `PUBLIC` holds `CREATE` on any schema (checked with `has_schema_privilege` and `aclexplode(coalesce(nspacl, acldefault('n', nspowner)))`; the readiness detail names the role and schema).
- Reserved migration numbers: WP-06 uses 0001 to 0099. A later WP that needs DDL adds files numbered from its own block: WP number times 100 (WP-36 uses 3600 to 3699). Every such file ends with the secure call (tenant) or the platform grants call (platform).

### C.4 Platform migrations

`apps/server/migrations/platform/0001_foundation.sql`

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS platform;
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS ref;

DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_app')
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_pack_installer') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Roles mscd_app and mscd_pack_installer must exist. Run "mscd-server db bootstrap" first.';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_extension e JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = e.extnamespace
                 WHERE e.extname = 'pg_trgm' AND n.nspname = 'ext') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Extension pg_trgm must exist in schema ext. Run "mscd-server db bootstrap" first.';
  END IF;
END $$;

REVOKE ALL ON SCHEMA platform FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON SCHEMA ref FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA platform TO mscd_app, mscd_pack_installer;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA ref TO mscd_app, mscd_pack_installer;

CREATE FUNCTION platform.set_updated_at() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at := now();
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION platform.forbid_change() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
BEGIN
  RAISE EXCEPTION '% on %.% is not allowed: the table is append-only', TG_OP, TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME
    USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
END $$;

-- Canonical text of one audit row: a JSON array, so no two different rows share one text.
-- Timestamps are rendered in UTC so the hash does not depend on the session time zone.
CREATE FUNCTION platform.audit_canonical_text(
  p_seq bigint, p_id uuid, p_occurred_at timestamptz, p_organization_id uuid, p_management_system_id uuid,
  p_actor_user_id uuid, p_actor_kind text, p_action text, p_entity_type text, p_entity_id uuid,
  p_entity_code text, p_before jsonb, p_after jsonb, p_edition text, p_request_id text, p_source_ip inet,
  p_user_agent text)
RETURNS text LANGUAGE sql STABLE SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
  SELECT jsonb_build_array(
    p_seq, p_id, to_char(p_occurred_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC', 'YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS.US"Z"'),
    p_organization_id, p_management_system_id, p_actor_user_id, p_actor_kind, p_action, p_entity_type,
    p_entity_id, p_entity_code, p_before, p_after, p_edition, p_request_id, host(p_source_ip), p_user_agent)::text
$$;

-- Hash chain for platform.audit_log and every org_*.audit_trail (same columns). Serialised per table.
CREATE FUNCTION platform.chain_hash() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
DECLARE
  last_seq bigint;
  last_hash bytea;
BEGIN
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(TG_TABLE_SCHEMA || '.' || TG_TABLE_NAME, 42));
  EXECUTE format('SELECT seq, row_hash FROM %I.%I ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME)
    INTO last_seq, last_hash;
  NEW.seq := coalesce(last_seq, 0) + 1;
  NEW.prev_hash := coalesce(last_hash, '\x'::bytea);
  NEW.row_hash := sha256(NEW.prev_hash || convert_to(platform.audit_canonical_text(
    NEW.seq, NEW.id, NEW.occurred_at, NEW.organization_id, NEW.management_system_id, NEW.actor_user_id,
    NEW.actor_kind, NEW.action, NEW.entity_type, NEW.entity_id, NEW.entity_code, NEW.before_data,
    NEW.after_data, NEW.edition, NEW.request_id, NEW.source_ip, NEW.user_agent), 'UTF8'));
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

-- A-29: every shared entity keeps exactly one primary management system.
CREATE FUNCTION platform.entity_link_has_primary() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
DECLARE
  found_primary boolean;
BEGIN
  EXECUTE format('SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM %I.entity_ms_link WHERE entity_type = $1 AND entity_id = $2 AND is_primary AND deleted_at IS NULL)', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA)
    INTO found_primary USING NEW.entity_type, NEW.entity_id;
  IF NOT found_primary THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Entity % % has no primary management system', NEW.entity_type, NEW.entity_id
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $$;

-- BR-43: preparation tasks finish before the audit starts.
CREATE FUNCTION platform.prep_task_before_audit() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
DECLARE
  event_date date;
BEGIN
  EXECUTE format('SELECT scheduled_at FROM %I.audit_event WHERE id = $1', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA)
    INTO event_date USING NEW.audit_event_id;
  IF event_date IS NOT NULL AND NEW.due_at >= event_date THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'BR-43: preparation task due % is not before the audit on %', NEW.due_at, event_date
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

-- Person columns must name a member of the row's organisation (backstop for MembershipService, C.1).
-- Trigger arguments are the column names to check.
CREATE FUNCTION platform.assert_member() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
DECLARE
  col text;
  uid uuid;
BEGIN
  FOREACH col IN ARRAY TG_ARGV LOOP
    EXECUTE format('SELECT ($1).%I', col) INTO uid USING NEW;
    IF uid IS NOT NULL AND uid <> '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'::uuid AND NOT EXISTS (
         SELECT 1 FROM platform.org_membership m
         WHERE m.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND m.user_id = uid AND m.deleted_at IS NULL) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'User % in column % is not a member of organisation %', uid, col, NEW.organization_id
        USING ERRCODE = 'foreign_key_violation';
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

-- Grants, RLS, policies and triggers for one tenant schema. Idempotent. Raises on a table it cannot secure.
CREATE FUNCTION platform.secure_tenant_schema(target_schema text) RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
DECLARE
  t record;
  tbl text;
  schema_org uuid;
  member_cols text[];
  report_excluded text[] := ARRAY['storage_credential', 'auditor_access_grant', 'calendar_feed_token',
                                  'upload_intent', 'audit_trail'];
  person_cols text[] := ARRAY['user_id', 'owner_user_id', 'holder_user_id', 'pending_holder_user_id',
                              'approver_user_id', 'justification_holder_user_id', 'relationship_owner_user_id',
                              'recipient_user_id', 'accepted_by_user_id', 'auditee_user_id',
                              'implemented_by_user_id', 'verified_by_user_id', 'completed_by_user_id'];
BEGIN
  -- The policy binds the schema to its own organisation, so a session of organisation A can neither read nor
  -- write rows in the schema of organisation B, even with a schema-qualified statement.
  SELECT o.id INTO schema_org FROM platform.organization o WHERE o.schema_name = target_schema;
  IF schema_org IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Schema % has no row in platform.organization', target_schema;
  END IF;
  EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON SCHEMA %I FROM PUBLIC', target_schema);
  EXECUTE format('GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA %I TO mscd_app', target_schema);
  FOR t IN
    SELECT c.relname FROM pg_class c JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
    WHERE n.nspname = target_schema AND c.relkind IN ('r', 'p') AND c.relname NOT LIKE 'kysely\_%'
  LOOP
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
                   WHERE table_schema = target_schema AND table_name = t.relname AND column_name = 'organization_id') THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Tenant table %.% has no organization_id column', target_schema, t.relname;
    END IF;
    EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON %I.%I TO mscd_app', target_schema, t.relname);
    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I.%I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', target_schema, t.relname);
    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I.%I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', target_schema, t.relname);
    EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS tenant_isolation ON %I.%I', target_schema, t.relname);
    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation ON %I.%I '
      'USING (organization_id = %L::uuid AND organization_id = nullif(current_setting(''app.org_id'', true), '''')::uuid) '
      'WITH CHECK (organization_id = %L::uuid AND organization_id = nullif(current_setting(''app.org_id'', true), '''')::uuid)',
      target_schema, t.relname, schema_org, schema_org);
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
               WHERE table_schema = target_schema AND table_name = t.relname AND column_name = 'updated_at')
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger tg
                       JOIN pg_class c2 ON c2.oid = tg.tgrelid JOIN pg_namespace n2 ON n2.oid = c2.relnamespace
                       WHERE n2.nspname = target_schema AND c2.relname = t.relname AND tg.tgname = 'set_updated_at') THEN
      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON %I.%I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.set_updated_at()',
        target_schema, t.relname);
    END IF;
    member_cols := ARRAY(SELECT column_name::text FROM information_schema.columns
                         WHERE table_schema = target_schema AND table_name = t.relname
                           AND column_name = ANY (person_cols) ORDER BY column_name);
    EXECUTE format('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS assert_member ON %I.%I', target_schema, t.relname);
    IF cardinality(member_cols) > 0 THEN
      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER assert_member BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF %s ON %I.%I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.assert_member(%s)',
        (SELECT string_agg(quote_ident(c), ', ') FROM unnest(member_cols) AS c), target_schema, t.relname,
        (SELECT string_agg(quote_literal(c), ', ') FROM unnest(member_cols) AS c));
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  FOREACH tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY['audit_trail', 'link_health_result', 'reminder'] LOOP
    IF to_regclass(format('%I.%I', target_schema, tbl)) IS NOT NULL THEN
      EXECUTE format('REVOKE UPDATE ON %I.%I FROM mscd_app', target_schema, tbl);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  EXECUTE format('GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA %I TO mscd_app', target_schema);
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_report') THEN
    EXECUTE format('GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA %I TO mscd_report', target_schema);
    FOR t IN
      SELECT c.relname FROM pg_class c JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
      WHERE n.nspname = target_schema AND c.relkind IN ('r', 'p') AND c.relname NOT LIKE 'kysely\_%'
        AND c.relname <> ALL (report_excluded)
    LOOP
      EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT ON %I.%I TO mscd_report', target_schema, t.relname);
    END LOOP;
  END IF;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_class c JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
             WHERE n.nspname = target_schema AND c.relkind IN ('r', 'p') AND c.relname NOT LIKE 'kysely\_%'
               AND NOT (c.relrowsecurity AND c.relforcerowsecurity)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Schema % has a table without forced row-level security', target_schema;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- Narrow grants and RLS on platform tables (D-31). Called at the end of every platform migration. Idempotent.
CREATE FUNCTION platform.secure_platform_schema() RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql
  SET search_path = pg_catalog, platform AS $$
DECLARE
  g record;
  org_expr text := 'nullif(current_setting(''app.org_id'', true), '''')::uuid';
  user_expr text := 'nullif(current_setting(''app.user_id'', true), '''')::uuid';
  scope_expr text := 'coalesce(current_setting(''app.platform_scope'', true), '''') = ''on''';
BEGIN
  EXECUTE 'REVOKE ALL ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA platform FROM mscd_app, mscd_pack_installer';
  FOR g IN SELECT * FROM (VALUES
      ('kysely_migration', 'SELECT'), ('organization', 'SELECT'), ('installation', 'SELECT'),
      ('app_user', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'), ('org_membership', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'),
      ('platform_role', 'SELECT'), ('verified_domain', 'SELECT'),
      ('identity_provider', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'), ('user_identity', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'),
      ('session', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'), ('mfa_factor', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'),
      ('mfa_recovery_code', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'), ('auth_transaction', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE'),
      ('saml_assertion_seen', 'SELECT, INSERT, DELETE'), ('password_reset_token', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'),
      ('invitation', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'), ('user_preference', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'),
      ('licence', 'SELECT, INSERT'), ('clock_mark', 'SELECT, UPDATE'),
      ('pack_install', 'SELECT'), ('pack_catalog', 'SELECT'), ('tenant_key', 'SELECT, INSERT'),
      ('audit_log', 'SELECT, INSERT'), ('audit_anchor', 'SELECT, INSERT'),
      ('rate_limit_bucket', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE'), ('idempotency_key', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE'),
      ('job_outbox', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE'), ('system_setting', 'SELECT')
    ) AS v(tbl, privs)
  LOOP
    IF to_regclass('platform.' || g.tbl) IS NOT NULL THEN
      EXECUTE format('GRANT %s ON platform.%I TO mscd_app', g.privs, g.tbl);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF to_regclass('platform.licence') IS NOT NULL THEN
    GRANT UPDATE (superseded_at) ON platform.licence TO mscd_app;
  END IF;
  IF to_regclass('platform.organization') IS NOT NULL THEN
    GRANT UPDATE (name, time_zone, site_count, local_login) ON platform.organization TO mscd_app;
  END IF;
  FOR g IN SELECT * FROM (VALUES
      ('pack_install', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'), ('pack_catalog', 'SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE'),
      ('audit_log', 'SELECT, INSERT'), ('installation', 'SELECT')
    ) AS v(tbl, privs)
  LOOP
    IF to_regclass('platform.' || g.tbl) IS NOT NULL THEN
      EXECUTE format('GRANT %s ON platform.%I TO mscd_pack_installer', g.privs, g.tbl);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  EXECUTE 'GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA platform TO mscd_app';
  -- Organisation-scoped platform tables: forced RLS with an explicit platform scope (F.10).
  FOR g IN SELECT * FROM (VALUES
      ('org_membership', format('organization_id = %s OR user_id = %s OR %s', org_expr, user_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('session', format('user_id = %s OR %s', user_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('identity_provider', format('organization_id = %s OR %s', org_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('verified_domain', format('organization_id = %s OR %s', org_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('invitation', format('organization_id = %s OR %s', org_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('licence', format('organization_id = %s OR %s', org_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('tenant_key', format('organization_id = %s OR %s', org_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('audit_log', format('organization_id = %s OR %s', org_expr, scope_expr)),
      ('idempotency_key', format('organization_id = %s', org_expr))
    ) AS v(tbl, expr)
  LOOP
    IF to_regclass('platform.' || g.tbl) IS NOT NULL THEN
      EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE platform.%I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', g.tbl);
      EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE platform.%I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', g.tbl);
      EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS platform_isolation ON platform.%I', g.tbl);
      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY platform_isolation ON platform.%I USING (%s) WITH CHECK (%s)', g.tbl, g.expr, g.expr);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;
```

`apps/server/migrations/platform/0002_identity.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE platform.organization (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  slug varchar(40) NOT NULL CHECK (slug ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,39}$'),
  name varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  schema_name varchar(63) NOT NULL CHECK (schema_name ~ '^org_[a-z0-9_]{1,59}$'),
  time_zone varchar(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Asia/Jakarta',
  site_count smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (site_count >= 0),
  status varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active', 'suspended', 'offboarding', 'archived')),
  local_login varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'allowed' CHECK (local_login IN ('allowed', 'admins_only', 'disabled')),
  region varchar(40),
  demo_deploy_label varchar(10) CHECK (demo_deploy_label IN ('saas', 'onprem')),
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX organization_slug_uq ON platform.organization (slug);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX organization_schema_uq ON platform.organization (schema_name);

-- Email domains the platform operator verified for an organisation (CLI `tenant verify-domain`). JIT needs them (D-25).
CREATE TABLE platform.verified_domain (
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  domain varchar(253) NOT NULL CHECK (domain = lower(domain) AND domain ~ '^[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}$'),
  verified_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  verified_by varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (organization_id, domain)
);

CREATE TABLE platform.app_user (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  handle varchar(40) NOT NULL CHECK (handle ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9._-]{1,39}$'),
  email varchar(254) NOT NULL,
  display_name varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  job_title varchar(150),
  kind varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'person' CHECK (kind IN ('person', 'system', 'guest')),
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active', 'invited', 'disabled')),
  password_hash text,
  password_changed_at timestamptz,
  failed_login_count smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  locked_until timestamptz,
  mfa_required boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  mfa_failed_count smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  mfa_lock_level smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  mfa_locked_until timestamptz,
  is_demo boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  last_login_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX app_user_handle_uq ON platform.app_user (handle);
-- Guest accounts are per grant (D-27) and never collide with a person's account.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX app_user_email_uq ON platform.app_user (lower(email)) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND kind <> 'guest';
CREATE INDEX app_user_name_trgm ON platform.app_user USING gin (display_name ext.gin_trgm_ops);
INSERT INTO platform.app_user (id, handle, email, display_name, kind, status, mfa_required)
VALUES ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001', 'system', 'system@mscd.invalid', 'System', 'system', 'active', false);

CREATE TABLE platform.org_membership (
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  admin_roles text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}' CHECK (admin_roles <@ ARRAY['tenant_admin', 'storage_admin']::text[]),
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active', 'invited', 'disabled')),
  is_default boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  invited_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  accepted_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  PRIMARY KEY (organization_id, user_id)
);
CREATE INDEX org_membership_user_idx ON platform.org_membership (user_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE platform.platform_role (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  role varchar(30) NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('platform_operator')),
  granted_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  granted_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  revoked_at timestamptz,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, role)
);

CREATE TABLE platform.identity_provider (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  slug varchar(40) NOT NULL CHECK (slug ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,39}$'),
  kind varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('oidc', 'saml')),
  display_name varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  icon varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'generic' CHECK (icon IN ('microsoft', 'google', 'generic')),
  enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  oidc_issuer text,
  oidc_client_id varchar(200),
  oidc_client_secret_sealed text,
  oidc_scopes text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'openid email profile',
  subject_mode varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'sub' CHECK (subject_mode IN ('sub', 'entra')),
  entra_tenant_id uuid,
  saml_entry_point text,
  saml_idp_issuer text,
  saml_idp_certs text[],
  saml_sp_entity_id text,
  saml_mfa_authn_contexts text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT ARRAY['https://refeds.org/profile/mfa',
    'http://schemas.microsoft.com/claims/multipleauthn']::text[],
  require_mfa_claim boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  accepted_amr text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT ARRAY['pwd', 'otp', 'hwk', 'swk', 'fpt', 'face', 'pin', 'sms', 'mca']::text[],
  mfa_fallback varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'local_totp' CHECK (mfa_fallback IN ('local_totp', 'reject')),
  jit_provisioning boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  allowed_email_domains text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  CHECK (kind <> 'oidc' OR (oidc_issuer IS NOT NULL AND oidc_client_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK (kind <> 'saml' OR (saml_entry_point IS NOT NULL AND saml_idp_certs IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK (subject_mode <> 'entra' OR (kind = 'oidc' AND entra_tenant_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK (NOT jit_provisioning OR cardinality(allowed_email_domains) > 0)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX identity_provider_slug_uq ON platform.identity_provider (slug) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX identity_provider_org_idx ON platform.identity_provider (organization_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- subject is the OIDC `sub`, '<tid>:<oid>' for subject_mode entra, or the SAML persistent NameID.
CREATE TABLE platform.user_identity (
  identity_provider_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.identity_provider(id),
  subject varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  email_at_link varchar(254),
  link_method varchar(12) NOT NULL CHECK (link_method IN ('invitation', 'session', 'admin', 'jit')),
  linked_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  linked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_login_at timestamptz,
  PRIMARY KEY (identity_provider_id, subject)
);
CREATE INDEX user_identity_user_idx ON platform.user_identity (user_id);

CREATE TABLE platform.session (
  id_hash bytea PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  organization_id uuid REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  auth_org_id uuid REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  state varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('mfa_pending', 'mfa_enrolment', 'active')),
  auth_method varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (auth_method IN ('local', 'oidc', 'saml', 'demo', 'guest', 'test')),
  identity_provider_id uuid REFERENCES platform.identity_provider(id),
  auth_grant_id uuid,
  amr text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  password_at timestamptz,
  mfa_at timestamptz,
  csrf_token_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  persistent boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_seen_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  idle_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  absolute_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  revoked_at timestamptz,
  revoke_reason varchar(40),
  ip inet,
  user_agent text
);
CREATE INDEX session_user_idx ON platform.session (user_id) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX session_grant_idx ON platform.session (auth_grant_id) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL AND auth_grant_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX session_expiry_idx ON platform.session (absolute_expires_at);

CREATE TABLE platform.mfa_factor (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  kind varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'totp' CHECK (kind IN ('totp')),
  secret_sealed text NOT NULL,
  label varchar(60),
  confirmed_at timestamptz,
  last_used_step bigint,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX mfa_factor_confirmed_uq ON platform.mfa_factor (user_id, kind)
  WHERE confirmed_at IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE platform.mfa_recovery_code (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  code_hash text NOT NULL,
  used_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX mfa_recovery_code_user_idx ON platform.mfa_recovery_code (user_id) WHERE used_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;

-- One row per browser flow: OIDC and SAML sign-in, identity linking, repository OAuth consent, guest one-time code.
CREATE TABLE platform.auth_transaction (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  kind varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('oidc_login', 'oidc_link', 'saml_login', 'saml_link', 'repo_oauth', 'guest_otp')),
  identity_provider_id uuid REFERENCES platform.identity_provider(id),
  organization_id uuid REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  target_id uuid,
  state_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  browser_binding_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  nonce varchar(100),
  code_verifier_sealed text,
  saml_request_id varchar(100),
  otp_hash text,
  attempts smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  return_to text,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  CHECK (kind NOT IN ('oidc_login', 'oidc_link', 'saml_login', 'saml_link') OR identity_provider_id IS NOT NULL),
  CHECK (kind <> 'repo_oauth' OR (organization_id IS NOT NULL AND user_id IS NOT NULL AND target_id IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX auth_transaction_state_uq ON platform.auth_transaction (state_hash);

-- SAML assertion ids seen within their validity window (replay protection, F.9).
CREATE TABLE platform.saml_assertion_seen (
  identity_provider_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.identity_provider(id),
  assertion_id_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (identity_provider_id, assertion_id_hash)
);

CREATE TABLE platform.password_reset_token (
  token_hash bytea PRIMARY KEY,
  kind varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'reset' CHECK (kind IN ('reset', 'set_password')),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  used_at timestamptz
);

-- Invitation to an organisation; redeemed by setting a password or by signing in through one of its providers (D-25).
CREATE TABLE platform.invitation (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  token_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  email varchar(254) NOT NULL,
  created_by uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  used_at timestamptz,
  revoked_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX invitation_token_uq ON platform.invitation (token_hash);

CREATE TABLE platform.user_preference (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  key varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  value jsonb NOT NULL,
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, key)
);

CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON platform.organization FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.set_updated_at();
CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON platform.app_user FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.set_updated_at();
CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON platform.org_membership FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.set_updated_at();
CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON platform.identity_provider FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.set_updated_at();
CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON platform.mfa_factor FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.set_updated_at();
SELECT platform.secure_platform_schema();
```

`apps/server/migrations/platform/0003_licensing_packs_keys.sql`

```sql
-- One row per installation, created on the first migrate. Licences may bind to it (D-15).
CREATE TABLE platform.installation (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  singleton boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true UNIQUE CHECK (singleton),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
INSERT INTO platform.installation DEFAULT VALUES;

-- Highest wall-clock time the api or worker has seen (licence clock rollback check, D-15).
CREATE TABLE platform.clock_mark (
  singleton boolean PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT true CHECK (singleton),
  high_water timestamptz NOT NULL
);
INSERT INTO platform.clock_mark (high_water) VALUES (now());

-- Signed licence documents. Validity, state and entitlements are derived from `document` on every evaluation.
CREATE TABLE platform.licence (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  license_id varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  document jsonb NOT NULL,
  document_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,
  key_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  installed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  installed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  superseded_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX licence_current_uq ON platform.licence (organization_id) WHERE superseded_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE platform.pack_install (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  pack_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  pack_version varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  framework_code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  edition varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  framework_version_id uuid,
```
