```sql
  status varchar(12) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('validating', 'installed', 'failed', 'superseded')),
  pre_release boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  manifest jsonb NOT NULL,
  archive_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,
  signature_key_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  error text,
  installed_at timestamptz,
  installed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX pack_install_installed_uq ON platform.pack_install (pack_id, pack_version) WHERE status = 'installed';
CREATE INDEX pack_install_framework_idx ON platform.pack_install (framework_code, edition);

CREATE TABLE platform.pack_catalog (
  pack_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  pack_version varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  channel varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (channel IN ('managed', 'online', 'offline')),
  note_en text NOT NULL,
  note_id text NOT NULL,
  published_at date,
  retrieved_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (pack_id, pack_version)
);

-- Tenant data key, wrapped by the storage-gateway KEK. The api never sees it unwrapped.
CREATE TABLE platform.tenant_key (
  organization_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES platform.organization(id),
  wrapped_dek text NOT NULL,
  kek_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  rotated_at timestamptz
);
SELECT platform.secure_platform_schema();
```

`apps/server/migrations/platform/0004_operations.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE platform.audit_log (
  seq bigint NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  occurred_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  organization_id uuid,
  management_system_id uuid,
  actor_user_id uuid,
  actor_kind varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (actor_kind IN ('user', 'system', 'guest', 'operator')),
  action varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  entity_type varchar(40),
  entity_id uuid,
  entity_code varchar(80),
  before_data jsonb,
  after_data jsonb,
  edition varchar(40),
  request_id varchar(64),
  source_ip inet,
  user_agent text,
  prev_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  row_hash bytea NOT NULL
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX audit_log_id_uq ON platform.audit_log (id);
CREATE INDEX audit_log_org_time_idx ON platform.audit_log (organization_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX audit_log_entity_idx ON platform.audit_log (entity_type, entity_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE TRIGGER audit_log_chain BEFORE INSERT ON platform.audit_log FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.chain_hash();
CREATE TRIGGER audit_log_append_only BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON platform.audit_log FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.forbid_change();
CREATE TRIGGER audit_log_no_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON platform.audit_log FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.forbid_change();

-- Daily anchors of every chain head, signed by the worker's anchor key (D-33, F.11). The signed copy also leaves the database.
CREATE TABLE platform.audit_anchor (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  chain varchar(80) NOT NULL,
  seq bigint NOT NULL,
  row_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  anchored_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  payload jsonb NOT NULL,
  signature text NOT NULL,
  key_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  exported_to text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
);
CREATE INDEX audit_anchor_chain_idx ON platform.audit_anchor (chain, anchored_at DESC);
CREATE TRIGGER audit_anchor_append_only BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON platform.audit_anchor FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.forbid_change();

CREATE TABLE platform.rate_limit_bucket (
  key varchar(200) PRIMARY KEY,
  window_start timestamptz NOT NULL,
  hits integer NOT NULL,
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX rate_limit_bucket_expiry_idx ON platform.rate_limit_bucket (expires_at);

CREATE TABLE platform.idempotency_key (
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  key varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  route varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  request_hash char(64) NOT NULL,
  status smallint,
  response jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (organization_id, user_id, key)
);

CREATE TABLE platform.job_outbox (
  id bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
  queue varchar(80) NOT NULL,
  payload jsonb NOT NULL,
  singleton_key varchar(200),
  run_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  dispatched_at timestamptz,
  attempts smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_error text
);
CREATE INDEX job_outbox_pending_idx ON platform.job_outbox (run_at) WHERE dispatched_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE platform.system_setting (
  key varchar(80) PRIMARY KEY,
  value jsonb NOT NULL,
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
SELECT platform.secure_platform_schema();
```

### C.5 Reference schema migration

`apps/server/migrations/ref/0001_framework_content.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE ref.framework (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code varchar(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE ref.framework_version (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework(id),
  edition varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  pack_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  pack_version varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  status varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('current', 'transition', 'withdrawn')),
  pre_release boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  transition_ends_at date,
  supersedes_version_id uuid REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  applicability_target varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (applicability_target IN ('catalogue_item', 'requirement')),
  pack_checksum char(64) NOT NULL,
  manifest jsonb NOT NULL,
  installed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (framework_id, edition)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.requirement (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  requirement_code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  parent_id uuid REFERENCES ref.requirement(id),
  title_short varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  clause_group varchar(3) NOT NULL CHECK (clause_group IN ('4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9', '10')),
  is_hs_common boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  documented_info_mandatory boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  applicability_assessable boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  default_frequency_code varchar(20),
  evidence_example text,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, requirement_code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.catalogue_item (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  item_code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  theme_code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  title_short varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  requirement_id uuid REFERENCES ref.requirement(id),
  flags jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  default_frequency_code varchar(20),
  evidence_example text,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, item_code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.requirement_mapping (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  source_requirement_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.requirement(id),
  target_requirement_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.requirement(id),
  mapping_type varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (mapping_type IN ('equivalent', 'partial', 'related', 'split', 'merged', 'changed', 'new', 'removed')),
  evidence_reusable boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  note text
);
CREATE INDEX requirement_mapping_source_idx ON ref.requirement_mapping (source_requirement_id);
CREATE INDEX requirement_mapping_target_idx ON ref.requirement_mapping (target_requirement_id);

CREATE TABLE ref.traceability_chain_def (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  stage_key varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  position smallint NOT NULL,
  label_en varchar(80) NOT NULL,
  label_id varchar(80) NOT NULL,
  auditor_question_en text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  auditor_question_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  source varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('entity', 'applicability', 'justification', 'document', 'evidence')),
  entity_type varchar(40),
  risk_profile_code varchar(30),
  is_mandatory boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  clause_at_risk varchar(20),
  overview_clause smallint NOT NULL CHECK (overview_clause BETWEEN 4 AND 10),
  gate_type_label_en varchar(40),
  gate_type_label_id varchar(40),
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, stage_key),
  CHECK (source <> 'entity' OR entity_type IS NOT NULL)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.activity_template (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  phase_no smallint NOT NULL,
  phase_name_en varchar(120) NOT NULL,
  phase_name_id varchar(120) NOT NULL,
  is_gate_phase boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  activity_no smallint NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  clause_ref varchar(20),
  output_document_code varchar(30),
  expected_outcome text,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, phase_no, activity_no)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.evidence_frequency_rule (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  label_en varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  label_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  period_days integer NOT NULL CHECK (period_days >= 0),
  sort_order smallint NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.label (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  key varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  lang varchar(2) NOT NULL CHECK (lang IN ('en', 'id')),
  value text NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, key, lang)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.cycle_requirement_def (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  requirement_code varchar(20),
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  derivation jsonb,
  sort_order smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.drift_rule_def (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  label_en varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  label_id varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  severity varchar(5) NOT NULL CHECK (severity IN ('crit', 'risk', 'warn')),
  source_stage varchar(20),
  source_kind varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (source_kind IN ('manual', 'unverif', 'nohash', 'metric', 'exercise', 'stale_risk', 'dd_expired')),
  predicate jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  message text NOT NULL,
  sort_order smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.prep_task_template (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  days_before_audit smallint NOT NULL CHECK (days_before_audit > 0),
  skip_on_first_audit boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  sort_order smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.review_input_def (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  mandatory boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  sort_order smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.finding_condition (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  classification varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (classification IN ('major', 'minor', 'ofi', 'signal', 'not_a_finding')),
  requirement_code varchar(20),
  text text NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.document_template (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  doc_type varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

-- Audit Activity Planner templates (PRD 8.1, pack file audit_activities.csv).
CREATE TABLE ref.audit_activity_template (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  event_type varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (event_type IN ('stage1', 'stage2', 'surveillance', 'recertification', 'transition', 'special')),
  clause_ref varchar(40),
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  auditee_role varchar(40),
  key_questions text,
  evidence_to_request text,
  sort_order smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

-- Framework-specific enumerations (TS 1.1): domains doc_type, treatment_option, exercise_type,
-- bribery_risk_level, due_diligence_subject, risk_profile, plus any domain a pack adds.
CREATE TABLE ref.lookup_value (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  domain varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  code varchar(40) NOT NULL CHECK (code ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,39}$'),
  label_en varchar(120) NOT NULL,
  label_id varchar(120) NOT NULL,
  sort_order smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, domain, code)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.rule_activation (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  rule_id varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (rule_id ~ '^BR-[0-9]{2}$'),
  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  params jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, rule_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ref.risk_profile (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  definition jsonb NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (framework_version_id, code)
);

GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA ref TO mscd_app;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA ref TO mscd_pack_installer;
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'mscd_report') THEN
    GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA ref TO mscd_report;
    GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA ref TO mscd_report;
  END IF;
END $$;
```

### C.6 Tenant schema migrations

Tenant files run with `search_path` set to the organisation schema and `ext`, so table names are unqualified and trigram operator classes are written `ext.gin_trgm_ops`. Every table with business data carries the standard columns; tables bound to one system carry `management_system_id`; shareable entities (TS 4.2: context issue, interested party, asset, document, evidence, risk, audit event, management review, metric) reach their systems through `entity_ms_link`.

Chain-stage records (context issues, risks, treatments, metrics, objectives, prioritised activities, BIAs, exercises, business associates and the generic `chain_record`) share one block of columns: `code`, `title`, `owner_user_id` (the holder; null is Unassigned, BR-45), `pending_action` (null when the record has no open action), `action_due_at`, `action_completed_at`, `action_completed_by`, `is_gate_item`, `position`.

`apps/server/migrations/tenant/0001_core.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE management_system (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  slug varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (slug ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,19}$'),
  code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  name varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  transition_target_version_id uuid REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  scope_statement text NOT NULL,
  status varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('setup', 'implementing', 'certified', 'transitioning', 'archived')),
  default_storage_repository_id uuid NOT NULL,
  certification_body varchar(150),
  color varchar(7) NOT NULL DEFAULT '#e8731a' CHECK (color ~ '^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$'),
  display jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX management_system_slug_uq ON management_system (slug) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX management_system_code_uq ON management_system (code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE management_system_version (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  framework_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  role varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('active', 'transition_target', 'previous')),
  activated_at date NOT NULL,
  ended_at date
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX management_system_version_role_uq ON management_system_version (management_system_id, role)
  WHERE ended_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL AND role <> 'previous';

CREATE TABLE user_ms_role (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  role varchar(30) NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('ms_manager', 'programme_owner', 'domain_owner', 'risk_owner',
    'internal_auditor', 'top_management', 'external_auditor', 'compliance_investigator')),
  is_primary boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  valid_until timestamptz,
  -- Grants to a Tenant or Storage Admin, and every self-grant, wait for a second Tenant Admin (F.6, TS 7.2).
  approval_status varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (approval_status IN ('active', 'pending_approval', 'rejected')),
  requested_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  approved_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  CHECK (role <> 'external_auditor' OR valid_until IS NOT NULL),
  CHECK (approved_by IS NULL OR approved_by IS DISTINCT FROM requested_by),
  CHECK (approved_by IS NULL OR approved_by IS DISTINCT FROM user_id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX user_ms_role_uq ON user_ms_role (user_id, management_system_id, role) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX user_ms_role_primary_uq ON user_ms_role (management_system_id, role) WHERE is_primary AND deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX user_ms_role_ms_idx ON user_ms_role (management_system_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE entity_ms_link (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  entity_type varchar(30) NOT NULL CHECK (entity_type IN ('context_issue', 'interested_party', 'asset', 'document',
    'evidence', 'risk', 'audit_event', 'management_review', 'metric')),
  entity_id uuid NOT NULL,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  is_primary boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  relevance_note text
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX entity_ms_link_uq ON entity_ms_link (entity_type, entity_id, management_system_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX entity_ms_link_primary_uq ON entity_ms_link (entity_type, entity_id) WHERE is_primary AND deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX entity_ms_link_ms_idx ON entity_ms_link (management_system_id, entity_type) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX entity_ms_link_entity_idx ON entity_ms_link (entity_type, entity_id);
CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER entity_ms_link_has_primary AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON entity_ms_link
  DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.entity_link_has_primary();

CREATE TABLE certification_cycle (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  cycle_start date NOT NULL,
  cycle_end date NOT NULL,
  status varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('planned', 'active', 'certified', 'suspended', 'withdrawn', 'expired', 'closed')),
  certified_at date,
  certificate_no varchar(60),
  CHECK (cycle_end > cycle_start)
);
CREATE INDEX certification_cycle_ms_idx ON certification_cycle (management_system_id, cycle_start DESC) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE ms_alert (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  kind varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('connector', 'transition', 'beta', 'withdrawn_edition', 'licence', 'other')),
  tone varchar(5) NOT NULL CHECK (tone IN ('info', 'warn', 'bad')),
  text_en text NOT NULL,
  text_id text NOT NULL,
  source_ref jsonb,
  raised_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  resolved_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX ms_alert_open_idx ON ms_alert (management_system_id) WHERE resolved_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE clause_readiness (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  clause_group smallint NOT NULL CHECK (clause_group BETWEEN 4 AND 10),
  readiness_pct smallint NOT NULL CHECK (readiness_pct BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  source varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'seeded' CHECK (source IN ('seeded', 'manual', 'computed'))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX clause_readiness_uq ON clause_readiness (management_system_id, clause_group) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE audit_trail (
  seq bigint NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  occurred_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  management_system_id uuid,
  actor_user_id uuid,
  actor_kind varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (actor_kind IN ('user', 'system', 'guest', 'operator')),
  action varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  entity_type varchar(40),
  entity_id uuid,
  entity_code varchar(80),
  before_data jsonb,
  after_data jsonb,
  edition varchar(40),
  request_id varchar(64),
  source_ip inet,
  user_agent text,
  prev_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  row_hash bytea NOT NULL
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX audit_trail_id_uq ON audit_trail (id);
CREATE INDEX audit_trail_entity_idx ON audit_trail (entity_type, entity_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX audit_trail_ms_time_idx ON audit_trail (management_system_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE TRIGGER audit_trail_chain BEFORE INSERT ON audit_trail FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.chain_hash();
CREATE TRIGGER audit_trail_append_only BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON audit_trail FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.forbid_change();
CREATE TRIGGER audit_trail_no_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON audit_trail FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.forbid_change();

SELECT platform.secure_tenant_schema(current_schema());
```

`apps/server/migrations/tenant/0002_storage.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE storage_repository (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  slug varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (slug ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,19}$'),
  name varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  adapter varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (adapter IN ('managed_vault', 'm365', 'google_drive', 'smb', 's3', 'webdav')),
  modes text[] NOT NULL CHECK (cardinality(modes) >= 1
    AND modes <@ ARRAY['stored', 'pass_through', 'link', 'zero_transit']::text[]),
  config jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  credential_ref varchar(200),
  ownership_type varchar(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'organizational' CHECK (ownership_type IN ('organizational', 'personal')),
  retention_confirmed boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  connection_status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ok' CHECK (connection_status IN ('ok', 'degraded', 'failed', 'revoked')),
  connection_message_en text,
  connection_message_id text,
  connection_status_since timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_checked_at timestamptz,
  metadata_minimization boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  read_only boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  display jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  CHECK (adapter = 'managed_vault' OR NOT ('stored' = ANY (modes))),
  CHECK (adapter <> 'managed_vault' OR modes = ARRAY['stored']::text[])
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX storage_repository_slug_uq ON storage_repository (slug) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
ALTER TABLE management_system ADD CONSTRAINT management_system_default_repository_fk
  FOREIGN KEY (default_storage_repository_id) REFERENCES storage_repository(id) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED;

CREATE TABLE storage_credential (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  storage_repository_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_repository(id),
  credential_ref varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  kind varchar(30) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('m365_certificate', 'google_service_account', 'oauth_refresh_token',
    's3_access_key', 'smb_account', 'webdav_account')),
  sealed_blob text NOT NULL,
  key_id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  -- SHA-256 of the canonical repository config the blob is bound to (D-08); a config change needs a new seal.
  config_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,
  external_account varchar(254),
  expires_at timestamptz,
  revoked_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX storage_credential_ref_uq ON storage_credential (credential_ref);

CREATE TABLE storage_routing_rule (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid REFERENCES management_system(id),
  classification varchar(15) CHECK (classification IN ('public', 'internal', 'confidential', 'restricted')),
  object_kind varchar(10) CHECK (object_kind IN ('document', 'evidence')),
  storage_repository_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_repository(id),
  mode varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('stored', 'pass_through', 'link', 'zero_transit')),
  priority smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
  note text,
  approval_status varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (approval_status IN ('active', 'pending_approval', 'rejected', 'superseded')),
  requested_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  approved_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  CHECK (approved_by IS NULL OR approved_by IS DISTINCT FROM requested_by)
);
CREATE INDEX storage_routing_rule_ms_idx ON storage_routing_rule (management_system_id, priority)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND approval_status = 'active';

CREATE TABLE storage_object (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  storage_repository_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_repository(id),
  external_item_id varchar(300) NOT NULL,
  external_version_id varchar(200),
  version_label varchar(30),
  display_name varchar(255),
  -- Type detected by the gateway from the first bytes (vault, pass-through) or reported by the provider (link);
  -- never the type a client declared (F.13).
  mime_type varchar(100),
  size_bytes bigint CHECK (size_bytes IS NULL OR size_bytes >= 0),
  hash_algorithm varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none' CHECK (hash_algorithm IN ('sha256', 'sha1', 'md5', 'quickxor', 'none')),
  hash_value varchar(128),
  hash_source varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (hash_source IN ('computed', 'provider', 'none')),
  provider_created_at timestamptz,
  provider_modified_at timestamptz,
  provider_modified_by varchar(150),
  folder_path text,
  folder_id varchar(300),
  link_status varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'intact' CHECK (link_status IN ('intact', 'moved', 'version_changed',
    'verification_pending', 'hash_mismatch', 'pinned_version_lost', 'missing', 'access_denied')),
  link_status_since timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  -- 'credential_invalid' shortens the validity of verification_pending from 7 days to 72 hours (E.3, TS 5.7).
  link_status_reason varchar(20) CHECK (link_status_reason IN ('unreachable', 'credential_invalid')),
  last_verified_at timestamptz,
  last_good_check_at timestamptz,
  next_check_at timestamptz,
  web_url text,
  encryption jsonb,
  CHECK ((hash_algorithm = 'none') = (hash_value IS NULL)),
  CHECK (hash_source <> 'none' OR hash_algorithm = 'none')
);
CREATE INDEX storage_object_health_idx ON storage_object (link_status, last_verified_at);
CREATE INDEX storage_object_item_idx ON storage_object (storage_repository_id, external_item_id);
CREATE INDEX storage_object_next_check_idx ON storage_object (next_check_at) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE link_health_result (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  storage_object_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  checked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  outcome varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('found', 'not_found', 'forbidden', 'unreachable', 'rate_limited', 'error')),
  status_before varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  status_after varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  observed_version_id varchar(200),
  observed_hash varchar(128),
  observed_folder_path text,
  detail jsonb,
  job_id varchar(80)
);
CREATE INDEX link_health_result_object_idx ON link_health_result (storage_object_id, checked_at DESC);

CREATE TABLE upload_intent (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  object_kind varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (object_kind IN ('evidence', 'document')),
  target_id uuid NOT NULL,
  storage_repository_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_repository(id),
  mode varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('stored', 'pass_through', 'zero_transit')),
  folder_path text NOT NULL,
  -- Name written to the repository. With metadata_minimization it is the evidence or document code based name
  -- and original_name is null (TS 4.5).
  stored_name varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  original_name varchar(255),
  max_bytes bigint NOT NULL CHECK (max_bytes > 0),
  correlation_code varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open', 'uploaded', 'completed', 'expired', 'failed')),
  storage_object_id uuid REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX upload_intent_correlation_uq ON upload_intent (correlation_code);

CREATE TABLE repository_move_request (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  storage_repository_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_repository(id),
  target_repository_id uuid REFERENCES storage_repository(id),
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open', 'done', 'rejected')),
  note text
);

SELECT platform.secure_tenant_schema(current_schema());
```

`apps/server/migrations/tenant/0003_registers.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE context_issue (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  issue_type varchar(10) CHECK (issue_type IN ('internal', 'external')),
  description text,
  last_reviewed_at date
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX context_issue_code_uq ON context_issue (code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE interested_party (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  requirements text,
  last_reviewed_at date,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX interested_party_code_uq ON interested_party (code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE asset (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  asset_type varchar(40),
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  classification varchar(15) CHECK (classification IN ('public', 'internal', 'confidential', 'restricted')),
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX asset_code_uq ON asset (code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE risk (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  risk_profile_code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  is_opportunity boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  description text,
  likelihood smallint,
  consequence smallint,
  impact_dimensions jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  residual_likelihood smallint,
  residual_consequence smallint,
  treatment_option varchar(20),
  assessed_at date,
  next_review_at date,
  residual_accepted_at date
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX risk_code_uq ON risk (code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX risk_title_trgm ON risk USING gin (title ext.gin_trgm_ops);

CREATE TABLE risk_treatment (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  risk_id uuid REFERENCES risk(id),
  target_type varchar(15) CHECK (target_type IN ('catalogue_item', 'requirement')),
  catalogue_item_id uuid REFERENCES ref.catalogue_item(id),
  requirement_id uuid REFERENCES ref.requirement(id),
  status varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'proposed' CHECK (status IN ('proposed', 'approved', 'in_progress', 'delayed',
    'implemented', 'residual_accepted', 'closed')),
  target_date date,
  implemented_at date,
  implemented_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  closed_at date,
  CHECK (status NOT IN ('implemented', 'residual_accepted', 'closed') OR implemented_by_user_id IS NOT NULL),
  CHECK (target_type IS NULL
    OR (target_type = 'catalogue_item' AND catalogue_item_id IS NOT NULL AND requirement_id IS NULL)
    OR (target_type = 'requirement' AND requirement_id IS NOT NULL AND catalogue_item_id IS NULL)),
  CHECK (status = 'proposed' OR target_type IS NOT NULL)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX risk_treatment_code_uq ON risk_treatment (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE residual_acceptance (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  risk_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES risk(id),
  risk_treatment_id uuid REFERENCES risk_treatment(id),
  accepted_by_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  -- Snapshot of the treatment implementer at acceptance (BR-04: the implementer never accepts).
  implemented_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  accepted_at date NOT NULL,
  residual_score smallint,
  note text,
  CHECK (implemented_by_user_id IS NULL OR accepted_by_user_id <> implemented_by_user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE metric (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  unit varchar(20),
  target_value numeric(18,4),
  frequency_code varchar(20),
  last_measured_at date
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX metric_code_uq ON metric (code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE metric_value (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  metric_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES metric(id),
  period_start date NOT NULL,
  period_end date NOT NULL,
  value numeric(18,4) NOT NULL,
  note text
);

CREATE TABLE quality_objective (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  statement text,
  metric_id uuid REFERENCES metric(id),
  target_text varchar(150),
  due_at date
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX quality_objective_code_uq ON quality_objective (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE process (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  inputs text,
  outputs text,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX process_code_uq ON process (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE prioritized_activity (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  product_service varchar(150),
  mtpd_hours integer CHECK (mtpd_hours IS NULL OR mtpd_hours > 0),
  rto_hours integer CHECK (rto_hours IS NULL OR rto_hours > 0),
  rpo_hours integer CHECK (rpo_hours IS NULL OR rpo_hours >= 0),
  mbco_note text,
  CHECK (rto_hours IS NULL OR mtpd_hours IS NULL OR rto_hours <= mtpd_hours)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX prioritized_activity_code_uq ON prioritized_activity (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE business_impact_analysis (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  prioritized_activity_id uuid REFERENCES prioritized_activity(id),
  impact_over_time jsonb,
  dependencies jsonb,
  resources_required jsonb,
  mtpd_hours integer,
  rto_hours integer,
  rpo_hours integer,
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'approved')),
  analysed_at date,
  next_review_at date,
  CHECK (rto_hours IS NULL OR mtpd_hours IS NULL OR rto_hours <= mtpd_hours)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX business_impact_analysis_code_uq ON business_impact_analysis (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE bc_plan (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),

  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,

);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bc_plan_code_uq ON bc_plan (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE exercise (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  bc_plan_id uuid REFERENCES bc_plan(id),

  planned_at date,
  performed_at date,

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX exercise_code_uq ON exercise (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE business_associate (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  category varchar(40),
  jurisdiction varchar(60),
  bribery_risk_level varchar(20),
  relationship_owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX business_associate_code_uq ON business_associate (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE due_diligence (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  subject_type varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  subject_id uuid,
  performed_at date,
  valid_until date,
  outcome varchar(30),
  conditions text,
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'completed', 'expired', 'superseded'))
);

-- Explicit chain records of stages without a typed table (for example the ISO 27001 SoA approval, SOA-01).
CREATE TABLE chain_record (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  stage_key varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_action text,
  action_due_at date,
  action_completed_at timestamptz,
  action_completed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  is_gate_item boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  related_entity_type varchar(40),
  related_entity_id uuid
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX chain_record_code_uq ON chain_record (management_system_id, stage_key, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- Up and down references as authored on each chain record (D-11). target_code is a business id in the same organisation.
CREATE TABLE record_reference (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  entity_type varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  entity_id uuid NOT NULL,
  direction varchar(4) NOT NULL CHECK (direction IN ('up', 'down')),
  target_code varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE INDEX record_reference_entity_idx ON record_reference (entity_type, entity_id, direction, position) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX record_reference_target_idx ON record_reference (target_code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
```

`apps/server/migrations/tenant/0004_documents_applicability_evidence.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE document (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  doc_code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  doc_type varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'in_review', 'approved', 'expired')),
  classification varchar(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'internal' CHECK (classification IN ('public', 'internal', 'confidential', 'restricted')),
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  approver_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  current_version_id uuid,
  approved_at date,
  next_review_at date,
  is_org_wide boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  pending_holder_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  pending_due_at date,
  review_started_at date,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX document_code_uq ON document (doc_code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX document_title_trgm ON document USING gin (title ext.gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX document_review_idx ON document (next_review_at) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE document_version (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  document_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES document(id),
  version_label varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  storage_object_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  status varchar(12) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'in_review', 'approved', 'rejected', 'superseded')),
  submitted_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  submitted_at timestamptz,
  approved_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  approved_at date,
  rejection_note text,
  CHECK (approved_by IS NULL OR submitted_by IS NULL OR approved_by <> submitted_by)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX document_version_label_uq ON document_version (document_id, version_label) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
ALTER TABLE document ADD CONSTRAINT document_current_version_fk
  FOREIGN KEY (current_version_id) REFERENCES document_version(id) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED;
ALTER TABLE bc_plan ADD CONSTRAINT bc_plan_document_fk FOREIGN KEY (document_id) REFERENCES document(id);

CREATE TABLE applicability_entry (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  target_type varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (target_type IN ('catalogue_item', 'requirement')),
  catalogue_item_id uuid REFERENCES ref.catalogue_item(id),
  requirement_id uuid REFERENCES ref.requirement(id),
  is_applicable boolean,
  inclusion_justification text,
  exclusion_justification text,
  implementation_status varchar(25) CHECK (implementation_status IN ('not_started', 'planned', 'partially_implemented',
    'complete', 'implemented', 'not_applicable')),
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  status_note text,
  holds_gate boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  gate_reason text,
  action_due_at date,
  justification_holder_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  justification_due_at date,
  completed_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  completed_at date,
  verified_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  verified_at date,
  last_evidence_at date,
  freshness_status varchar(10) CHECK (freshness_status IN ('fresh', 'aging', 'expired', 'critical')),
  integrity_status varchar(10) CHECK (integrity_status IN ('ok', 'degraded', 'broken')),
  derived_at timestamptz,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  CHECK ((target_type = 'catalogue_item') = (catalogue_item_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((target_type = 'requirement') = (requirement_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK (implementation_status IS DISTINCT FROM 'not_applicable' OR exclusion_justification IS NOT NULL),
  CHECK (verified_by_user_id IS NULL OR verified_by_user_id IS DISTINCT FROM completed_by_user_id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX applicability_entry_target_uq
  ON applicability_entry (management_system_id, target_type, catalogue_item_id, requirement_id) NULLS NOT DISTINCT
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX applicability_entry_status_idx ON applicability_entry (management_system_id, implementation_status) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX applicability_entry_owner_idx ON applicability_entry (owner_user_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE applicability_entry_risk (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  applicability_entry_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES applicability_entry(id),
  risk_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES risk(id),
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX applicability_entry_risk_uq ON applicability_entry_risk (applicability_entry_id, risk_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE applicability_entry_document (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  applicability_entry_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES applicability_entry(id),
  document_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES document(id),
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX applicability_entry_document_uq ON applicability_entry_document (applicability_entry_id, document_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX applicability_entry_document_doc_idx ON applicability_entry_document (document_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE operating_criteria (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  applicability_entry_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES applicability_entry(id),
  frequency_code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  description text
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX operating_criteria_entry_uq ON operating_criteria (applicability_entry_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE evidence (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  evidence_code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  produced_at date,
  storage_object_id uuid REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  external_ref text,
  frequency_code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  classification varchar(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'internal' CHECK (classification IN ('public', 'internal', 'confidential', 'restricted')),
  next_due_override date,
  file_sequence integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  freshness_status varchar(10) CHECK (freshness_status IN ('fresh', 'aging', 'expired', 'critical')),
  integrity_status varchar(10) CHECK (integrity_status IN ('ok', 'degraded', 'broken')),
  derived_at timestamptz,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  CHECK (produced_at IS NULL OR storage_object_id IS NOT NULL OR external_ref IS NOT NULL)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX evidence_code_uq ON evidence (evidence_code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX evidence_produced_idx ON evidence (organization_id, produced_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX evidence_owner_idx ON evidence (owner_user_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX evidence_title_trgm ON evidence USING gin (title ext.gin_trgm_ops);

CREATE TABLE evidence_target (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  evidence_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES evidence(id),
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  applicability_entry_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES applicability_entry(id),
  target_type varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (target_type IN ('catalogue_item', 'requirement')),
  catalogue_item_id uuid REFERENCES ref.catalogue_item(id),
  requirement_id uuid REFERENCES ref.requirement(id),
  mapping_note text,
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  CHECK ((target_type = 'catalogue_item') = (catalogue_item_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((target_type = 'requirement') = (requirement_id IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX evidence_target_uq ON evidence_target (evidence_id, applicability_entry_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX evidence_target_target_idx ON evidence_target (target_type, catalogue_item_id, requirement_id);
CREATE INDEX evidence_target_evidence_idx ON evidence_target (evidence_id);
CREATE INDEX evidence_target_entry_idx ON evidence_target (applicability_entry_id);

CREATE TABLE freshness_threshold (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid REFERENCES management_system(id),
  frequency_code varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  fresh_grace_days numeric(6,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  aging_factor numeric(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.25,
  expired_factor numeric(6,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
  CHECK (aging_factor >= 1 AND expired_factor >= aging_factor)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX freshness_threshold_uq ON freshness_threshold (management_system_id, frequency_code) NULLS NOT DISTINCT
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

SELECT platform.secure_tenant_schema(current_schema());
```

`apps/server/migrations/tenant/0005_audit_cycle_phases.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_event (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  certification_cycle_id uuid REFERENCES certification_cycle(id),
  event_type varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (event_type IN ('stage1', 'stage2', 'surveillance', 'recertification', 'transition', 'special')),
  name_en varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  name_id varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  short_label varchar(80) NOT NULL,
  scheduled_at date NOT NULL,
  audit_days smallint CHECK (audit_days IS NULL OR audit_days > 0),
  lead_auditor_name varchar(150),
  expected_focus text,
  prep_window_days smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 60 CHECK (prep_window_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
  outcome varchar(30) CHECK (outcome IN ('recommended', 'recommended_with_conditions', 'not_recommended')),
  outcome_summary text
);
CREATE INDEX audit_event_next_idx ON audit_event (management_system_id, scheduled_at) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE audit_prep_task (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  audit_event_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES audit_event(id),
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  template_code varchar(30),
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  due_at date NOT NULL,
  status varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'not_started' CHECK (status IN ('not_started', 'in_progress', 'done', 'blocked', 'overdue')),
  note text,
  violates_br43 boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  CHECK (status <> 'blocked' OR note IS NOT NULL)
);
CREATE INDEX audit_prep_task_event_idx ON audit_prep_task (audit_event_id, position) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE TRIGGER audit_prep_task_br43 BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF due_at, audit_event_id ON audit_prep_task
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform.prep_task_before_audit();

CREATE TABLE finding (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  framework_version_id uuid REFERENCES ref.framework_version(id),
  audit_event_id uuid REFERENCES audit_event(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  classification varchar(15) NOT NULL CHECK (classification IN ('major', 'minor', 'ofi', 'signal', 'not_a_finding')),
  clause_ref varchar(40),
  source varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('certification_audit', 'internal_audit', 'self_assessment', 'other')),
  status varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'closed')),
  status_note text,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  raised_at date,
  due_at date,
  rca_text text,
  systemic_check_text text,
  closed_at date,
  closed_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  close_note text,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  CHECK (status <> 'closed' OR closed_at IS NOT NULL),
  CHECK (status <> 'closed' OR classification <> 'major' OR (rca_text IS NOT NULL AND systemic_check_text IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX finding_code_uq ON finding (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX finding_open_idx ON finding (management_system_id, status, classification) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE corrective_action (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  status varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'correction' CHECK (status IN ('correction', 'rca', 'systemic_check', 'action',
    'effectiveness_verification', 'closed', 'cancelled')),
  correction_text text,
  rca_text text,
  systemic_check_text text,
  action_text text,
  due_at date,
  implemented_at date,
  implemented_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  verified_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  verified_at date,
  effective boolean,
  verification_note text,
  -- BR-09: neither the implementer nor the owner verifies. BR-08: verification on a different day.
  CHECK (verified_by_user_id IS NULL OR verified_by_user_id IS DISTINCT FROM implemented_by_user_id),
  CHECK (verified_by_user_id IS NULL OR verified_by_user_id IS DISTINCT FROM owner_user_id),
  CHECK (verified_at IS NULL OR implemented_at IS NULL OR verified_at <> implemented_at),
  CHECK (status <> 'closed' OR (verified_by_user_id IS NOT NULL AND verified_at IS NOT NULL AND effective IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX corrective_action_code_uq ON corrective_action (management_system_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE management_review (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  planned_at date,
  held_at date,
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'planned' CHECK (status IN ('planned', 'held', 'closed')),
  decisions_recorded boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  decisions_text text,
  closed_at date,
  closed_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  CHECK (status <> 'closed' OR (decisions_recorded AND closed_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX management_review_code_uq ON management_review (code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE management_review_input (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_review_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_review(id),
  review_input_def_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.review_input_def(id),
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  content text,
  source_refs jsonb
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX management_review_input_uq ON management_review_input (management_review_id, review_input_def_id, management_system_id)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE cycle_requirement_status (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  certification_cycle_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES certification_cycle(id),
  cycle_requirement_def_id uuid REFERENCES ref.cycle_requirement_def(id),
  requirement_code varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT '-',
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  status varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'not_started' CHECK (status IN ('not_started', 'in_progress', 'done', 'blocked', 'overdue')),
  due_at date NOT NULL,
  condition_note text,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE INDEX cycle_requirement_status_ms_idx ON cycle_requirement_status (management_system_id, position) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX cycle_requirement_status_def_uq ON cycle_requirement_status (certification_cycle_id, cycle_requirement_def_id)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND cycle_requirement_def_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TABLE drift_result (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  drift_rule_def_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.drift_rule_def(id),
  rule_code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  entity_type varchar(40),
  entity_id uuid,
  entity_code varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  title text NOT NULL,
  holder_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  due_at date,
  detected_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  resolved_at timestamptz,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE INDEX drift_result_open_idx ON drift_result (management_system_id, rule_code, position) WHERE resolved_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE implementation_activity_progress (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  activity_template_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.activity_template(id),
  phase_no smallint NOT NULL,
  activity_no smallint NOT NULL,
  -- Copied from the template at activation (D-30); a pack minor update never rewrites them.
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  clause_ref varchar(20),
  output_document_code varchar(30),
  expected_outcome text,
  is_gate_phase boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  template_pack_version varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  status varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'not_started' CHECK (status IN ('not_started', 'in_progress', 'complete', 'verified', 'blocked')),
  output_document_id uuid REFERENCES document(id),
  blocker_gate_item_ref varchar(60),
  blocker_reason text,
  due_at date,
  completed_at date,
  completed_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  verified_at date,
  verified_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  CHECK (status <> 'blocked' OR (blocker_gate_item_ref IS NOT NULL AND blocker_reason IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK (verified_by_user_id IS NULL OR verified_by_user_id IS DISTINCT FROM owner_user_id),
  CHECK (verified_by_user_id IS NULL OR verified_by_user_id IS DISTINCT FROM completed_by_user_id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX implementation_activity_progress_uq ON implementation_activity_progress (management_system_id, phase_no, activity_no)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE trace_chain_instance (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  anchor_code varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  position smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX trace_chain_instance_uq ON trace_chain_instance (management_system_id, anchor_code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE trace_chain_node (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  trace_chain_instance_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES trace_chain_instance(id),
  position smallint NOT NULL,
  stage_key varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  record_code varchar(40)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX trace_chain_node_uq ON trace_chain_node (trace_chain_instance_id, position) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
```
