`apps/server/migrations/tenant/0006_workflow.sql`

```sql
CREATE TABLE reminder (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  management_system_id uuid REFERENCES management_system(id),
  record_key varchar(120) NOT NULL,
  entity_type varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  entity_id uuid NOT NULL,
  recipient_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  sent_by_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  sent_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  channels text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT ARRAY['email', 'in_app'],
  bulk_id uuid,
  notification_id uuid
);
CREATE INDEX reminder_throttle_idx ON reminder (recipient_user_id, record_key, sent_at DESC);

CREATE TABLE notification (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  recipient_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  management_system_id uuid REFERENCES management_system(id),
  kind varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  title_en text NOT NULL,
  title_id text NOT NULL,
  body_en text NOT NULL,
  body_id text NOT NULL,
  entity_ref jsonb,
  read_at timestamptz,
  email_status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (email_status IN ('pending', 'sent', 'failed', 'skipped')),
  email_sent_at timestamptz,
  email_attempts smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_error text,
  digest boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false
);
CREATE INDEX notification_recipient_idx ON notification (recipient_user_id, created_at DESC) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX notification_email_pending_idx ON notification (created_at) WHERE email_status = 'pending';

CREATE TABLE background_task (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  kind varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('evidence_pack', 'export', 'link_health_run', 'pack_install', 'import')),
  management_system_id uuid REFERENCES management_system(id),
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued' CHECK (status IN ('queued', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed')),
  progress smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (progress BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  params jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  result jsonb,
  error text,
  job_id varchar(80),
  started_at timestamptz,
  finished_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX background_task_creator_idx ON background_task (created_by, created_at DESC);

CREATE TABLE evidence_pack (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  audit_event_id uuid REFERENCES audit_event(id),
  background_task_id uuid REFERENCES background_task(id),
  scope jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued' CHECK (status IN ('queued', 'building', 'ready', 'failed')),
  pack_folder text,
  manifest_pdf_object_id uuid REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  manifest_csv_object_id uuid REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  archive_object_id uuid REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  item_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  exception_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  built_at timestamptz
);

CREATE TABLE evidence_pack_item (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  evidence_pack_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES evidence_pack(id),
  evidence_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES evidence(id),
  evidence_code varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  requirement_codes text[] NOT NULL,
  produced_at date,
  external_version_id varchar(200),
  hash_algorithm varchar(10),
  hash_value varchar(128),
  link_status varchar(20),
  copy_location text,
  -- The copy made by copy-within (or the vault archive member); external auditors open only these (D-34).
  copy_storage_object_id uuid REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  is_deleted_record boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  is_exception boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  exception_reason text
);
CREATE INDEX evidence_pack_item_pack_idx ON evidence_pack_item (evidence_pack_id);

CREATE TABLE export_file (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  kind varchar(20) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('portfolio_board', 'readiness', 'soa', 'document_register', 'audit_trail')),
  format varchar(5) NOT NULL CHECK (format IN ('pdf', 'xlsx', 'csv')),
  management_system_id uuid REFERENCES management_system(id),
  background_task_id uuid REFERENCES background_task(id),
  storage_object_id uuid REFERENCES storage_object(id),
  file_name varchar(200),
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE auditor_access_grant (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  email varchar(254) NOT NULL,
  -- The guest account created for this grant (kind guest, D-27); never an existing person's account.
  user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  user_ms_role_id uuid REFERENCES user_ms_role(id),
  token_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  link_used_at timestamptz,
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  revoked_at timestamptz,
  revoked_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  last_used_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX auditor_access_grant_token_uq ON auditor_access_grant (token_hash);

SELECT platform.secure_tenant_schema(current_schema());
```

`apps/server/migrations/tenant/0007_registers_workflow.sql`

```sql
-- One corrective action may close several findings of a joint audit (PRD 8.2).
CREATE TABLE corrective_action_finding (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  corrective_action_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES corrective_action(id),
  finding_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES finding(id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX corrective_action_finding_uq ON corrective_action_finding (corrective_action_id, finding_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX corrective_action_finding_finding_idx ON corrective_action_finding (finding_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- Self-Assessment Workbench (PRD 8.1): one row per finding condition of the system's active framework version.
CREATE TABLE self_assessment (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  finding_condition_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ref.finding_condition(id),
  status varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'not_assessed' CHECK (status IN ('not_assessed', 'conforming', 'gap', 'not_applicable')),
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  target_fix_at date,
  note text,
  assessed_at date,
  finding_id uuid REFERENCES finding(id),
  CHECK (status <> 'gap' OR owner_user_id IS NOT NULL)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX self_assessment_uq ON self_assessment (management_system_id, finding_condition_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE self_assessment_evidence (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  self_assessment_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES self_assessment(id),
  evidence_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES evidence(id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX self_assessment_evidence_uq ON self_assessment_evidence (self_assessment_id, evidence_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- Audit Activity Planner (PRD 8.1): activities of one audit event, generated from ref.audit_activity_template.
CREATE TABLE audit_activity (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  audit_event_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES audit_event(id),
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  template_code varchar(30),
  title varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  clause_ref varchar(40),
  key_questions text,
  evidence_to_request text,
  auditee_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  scheduled_at date,
  status varchar(12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'planned' CHECK (status IN ('planned', 'done', 'cancelled')),
  note text,
  position integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX audit_activity_template_uq ON audit_activity (audit_event_id, template_code)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND template_code IS NOT NULL;

-- Tasks opened by business rules and closed when the condition clears (D-29). One open task per rule and entity.
CREATE TABLE task (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  management_system_id uuid REFERENCES management_system(id),
  source_rule varchar(12) NOT NULL CHECK (source_rule IN ('BR-10', 'BR-11', 'BR-15', 'BR-31', 'manual')),
  entity_type varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  entity_id uuid NOT NULL,
  entity_code varchar(80),
  title_en text NOT NULL,
  title_id text NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  due_at date,
  status varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open', 'done', 'cancelled')),
  closed_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  closed_at timestamptz,
  CHECK ((status = 'open') = (closed_at IS NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX task_open_uq ON task (source_rule, entity_type, entity_id) WHERE status = 'open' AND deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX task_owner_idx ON task (owner_user_id, status) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- Review calendar feed tokens (D.3), stored hashed, revocable, checked against ms.read on every fetch.
CREATE TABLE calendar_feed_token (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  organization_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  updated_by uuid REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.app_user(id),
  management_system_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES management_system(id),
  token_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  revoked_at timestamptz,
  last_used_at timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX calendar_feed_token_hash_uq ON calendar_feed_token (token_hash);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX calendar_feed_token_active_uq ON calendar_feed_token (user_id, management_system_id)
  WHERE revoked_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;

SELECT platform.secure_tenant_schema(current_schema());
```

### C.6a Tenant DDL notes

- Copy the files in this order: 0001 to 0007. Later DDL blocks of WPs (C.3 numbering) append files; they never edit these.
- `implementation_activity_progress.verified_by_user_id` and the corrective action checks name persons, so the `assert_member` trigger covers them too (C.4 `person_cols`).
- `management_review` is shareable: it has no `management_system_id` and reaches its systems through `entity_ms_link` (A-31); its inputs carry their own system.

### C.7 Kysely types

- `apps/server/src/db/types/` holds hand-written row interfaces, one file per migration group (`platform.ts`, `ref.ts`, `tenant-core.ts`, `tenant-storage.ts`, `tenant-registers.ts`, `tenant-documents.ts`, `tenant-audit.ts`, `tenant-workflow.ts`, `tenant-registers-workflow.ts`). Columns with defaults use `Generated<T>`; `timestamptz` maps to `Date`, `date` to `string` (`YYYY-MM-DD`, parsed with a custom `pg` type parser for OID 1082 so no time zone shift happens), `bigint` to `string`, `numeric` to `string`, `jsonb` to typed `JSONColumnType`.
- `apps/server/src/db/types/index.ts` declares `export interface DB {}` and every group file extends it through declaration merging (`declare module './index' { interface DB { ... } }`). Platform tables are keyed `'platform.<table>'`, reference tables `'ref.<table>'`, tenant tables by bare name. A later WP adds its tables in its own file without editing `index.ts`.
- A schema drift test (`test/int/db/schema-types.test.ts`) migrates a fresh database, reads `information_schema.columns` for `platform`, `ref` and one tenant schema, and compares table and column names with a list exported from the type files (`TABLE_COLUMNS` constant maintained next to the interfaces). It fails on any difference.

### C.8 Tenant provisioning

`TenantProvisioner.create({id?, slug, name, timeZone, siteCount, demoDeployLabel?})` (WP-06), used by `mscd-server tenant create` and by the seed (the organisation row must exist before step 3, because `secure_tenant_schema` binds the schema's policies to its id):

1. Validate the slug; with `app.platform_scope = 'on'`, insert `platform.organization` with `schema_name = 'org_' || slug`.
2. `CREATE SCHEMA "org_<slug>" AUTHORIZATION mscd_migrator`.
3. Run the tenant migrations into that schema.
4. Call the storage-gateway `POST /v1/keys/data-key` to get a wrapped tenant data key; insert `platform.tenant_key` (platform scope). When the gateway is not reachable the command fails (no tenant without a key). Tests use the gateway's in-process key service.
5. With `app.org_id` set, insert the default `managed_vault` repository (`slug 'vault'`, `name 'Managed Vault'`, `modes ['stored']`, `config {"prefix": "tenants/<org_id>/"}`) (A-41).
6. Write `tenant.created` to `platform.audit_log`.

### C.9 UI contract entities and their tables

| UI entity (UI section) | Tables | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Organisation (1.1) | `platform.organization`, `platform.org_membership` | `id` on the wire is the slug; `ms` lists the systems the session user may see; `deploy` from the profile (I.1) or `demo_deploy_label`; `sites` from `site_count` |
| Management system (1.2) | `management_system`, `management_system_version`, `ref.framework`, `ref.framework_version`, `ref.label`, `user_ms_role`, `audit_event`, `finding`, `clause_readiness`, `ms_alert`, `certification_cycle`, `storage_repository` | Field rules in G.5 |
| Person (1.3) | `platform.app_user` | PersonId is `handle`; `none` is synthetic |
| Role and current user (1.4) | `user_ms_role`, `platform.org_membership.admin_roles`, `platform.session` | UI role per system from F.7 |
| Requirement or control (1.5) | `applicability_entry`, `ref.catalogue_item` or `ref.requirement`, `applicability_entry_risk`, `applicability_entry_document`, `operating_criteria` | `id`, `key` and `theme` rules in G.5 |
| Document (1.6) | `document`, `document_version`, `entity_ms_link` | `shared` is `is_org_wide` or linked to two or more systems |
| Evidence (1.7) | `evidence`, `evidence_target`, `storage_object`, `storage_repository`, `entity_ms_link` | `integ` from C.12 |
| Chain stages (1.8, `STAGES`) | `ref.traceability_chain_def` | `rec` counted per stage (G.5) |
| Chain records (1.8, `X`) | `context_issue`, `risk`, `risk_treatment`, `metric`, `quality_objective`, `prioritized_activity`, `business_impact_analysis`, `exercise`, `business_associate`, `chain_record`, `record_reference` | Open when `pending_action` is set; stage mapping in C.10 |
| Chain instances (1.8, `CHAINS`, `TITLES`, `CLAUSE_AT_RISK`) | `trace_chain_instance`, `trace_chain_node`; titles from all chain record tables; `ref.traceability_chain_def.clause_at_risk` | D-11 |
| Phases and activities (1.9) | `ref.activity_template`, `implementation_activity_progress` | `locked` per D-13 |
| Audit events, preparation, findings, cycle, drift (1.10) | `audit_event`, `audit_prep_task`, `finding`, `cycle_requirement_status`, `drift_result`, `ref.drift_rule_def` | Tuple formats in G.5 |
| Review calendar (1.11) | `document` | Client grouping |
| Tasks (1.12) | Chain items held by the user, plus open `task` rows owned by the user whose entity is not already one of those chain items (D-29); `EXAMPLES` from `ref.catalogue_item.evidence_example` and `ref.requirement.evidence_example` | `GET /tasks`; a `task` row appears with kind `task`. On the demo seed every open `task` row matches a chain item of its owner, so the My Tasks counts of UI 4.7 do not change |
| Registers without UI screens (risks, treatments, findings, corrective actions, self-assessment, audit activities, management reviews, context, interested parties, assets, metrics) | C.6 tables | Served by the register endpoints of D.3 (WP-36, WP-37); forms are later UI work |
| Packs and licence (1.13) | `platform.pack_install`, `platform.pack_catalog`, `ref.framework_version`, `platform.licence` | Text formats in G.5 |
| Storage (1.14) | `storage_repository`, `storage_routing_rule`, `storage_object` | `LOC` colours and icons stay in the web bundle, keyed by repository kind |
| Picker files (1.15) | storage-gateway `list` on the repository | `GET /storage/repositories/{slug}/browse` |
| Frequencies (1.16) | `ref.evidence_frequency_rule`, `freshness_threshold` | |
| Tera, translations, date helpers (1.17 to 1.19) | Web bundle | `today` from the server |

### C.10 Chain stage to table mapping

| `traceability_chain_def.source` | Stage examples | Records | `rec` (denominator) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `entity` with `entity_type` | `context`, `risk`, `treatment`, `metric`, `action`, `objective`, `product`, `bia`, `drisk`, `exercise`, `brisk`, `associate` | Rows of `entity_type` in the system (shareable types through `entity_ms_link`; `risk` also filtered by `risk_profile_code`) | Count of those rows |
| `applicability` | `control`, `process`, `strategy` | `applicability_entry` | Count of entries |
| `justification` | `soa` | `applicability_entry` rows with `holds_gate` (kind `soa`), then open `chain_record` rows of the stage (kind `x`), for example `SOA-01` "Approve revised SoA" | Count of applicability entries |
| `document` | `document` | `document` rows of the system | Count of documents |
| `evidence` | `evidence` | `evidence` rows of the system | Count of evidence |

`chain_record` (in `tenant/0003_registers.sql`) holds explicit chain records of stages that have no typed table in this build. Its `stage_key` names the stage; `related_entity_type` and `related_entity_id` point to the record it concerns (the `SOA-01` chain record points to the `SOA-01` document, which is also listed at the `document` stage under its own key `isms:doc:SOA-01`).

### C.11 Status codes between database and UI

| Concept | Database value | UI code (wire of `/ui/*`) |
|---|---|---|
| Requirement status | `not_started`, `planned`, `partially_implemented`, `complete`, `implemented`, `not_applicable`; `holds_gate` | `ns`, `planned`, `partial`, `complete`, `implemented`, `na`; `blocked` when `holds_gate` and the status is not `implemented` or `not_applicable` |
| Document status | `draft`, `in_review`, `approved`, `expired` | `draft`, `review`, `approved`, `expired` (also `expired` when `approved` and `next_review_at < today`, BR-10) |
| Activity status | `not_started`, `in_progress`, `complete`, `verified`, `blocked` | `ns`, `progress`, `complete`, `verified`, `blocked`, `locked` (D-13) |
| Prep task and cycle item | `done`, `in_progress`, `not_started`, `blocked`, `overdue` | `done`, `progress`, `open` (UI 1.10) |
| Finding | classification `major`, `minor`, `ofi`; status `closed` or other | `Major`, `Minor`, `OFI`; `closed` or `open` |
| Evidence freshness | `fresh`, `aging`, `expired`, `critical` | same |
| Chain item | derived | `pending`, `overdue`, `missing` |
| Repository status | `connection_status` and `ownership_type` | `ok` (ok and organisational), `warn` (personal), `bad` (degraded, failed, revoked) |

### C.12 Integrity codes

| `storage_object` state | UI `integ` | Unverifiable in UI lists (A.4, 5.3) | Valid for status logic (TS 6.4) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `intact`, hash present | `ok` | no | yes |
| `intact`, `hash_algorithm = 'none'` | `nohash` | no | yes |
| `verification_pending` | `pending` | no | yes for 7 days from `link_status_since`; for 72 hours when `link_status_reason = 'credential_invalid'` (TS 5.7) |
| `version_changed`, `hash_mismatch` | `changed` | yes | `version_changed` yes, `hash_mismatch` no |
| `moved` | `moved` | yes | yes |
| `missing`, `pinned_version_lost` | `missing` | yes | no |
| `access_denied` | `revoked` | yes | yes for 72 hours from `link_status_since` |
| No storage object (`external_ref`) | `ok` | no | yes |

UI `hashAlg` text: `quickxor` and `provider` gives `quickXorHash`; `sha256` and `computed` gives `SHA-256`; `sha256` and `provider` gives `SHA-256 (provider)`; `md5` gives `MD5`; `sha1` gives `SHA-1`; `none` gives `none`.

The DDL of C.2 to C.6 is checked by WP-06 on `postgres:16.15-alpine`, `15-alpine` and `17-alpine`: bootstrap, platform, reference and all seven tenant files into two schemas; an `audit_trail` insert chains correctly and the F.11 verification query finds no broken row; `UPDATE` on `audit_trail` and `DELETE` on `reminder` are denied to `mscd_app`; a second `app.org_id` sees zero rows; an insert without `app.org_id` is rejected by RLS; a person column naming a non-member is rejected by `assert_member`; `mscd_app` cannot write `platform.platform_role`, `platform.licence` (except `superseded_at`), `platform.pack_install` or `ref`.

### C.13 State machines as transition tables

`packages/shared/src/machines.ts` (WP-05) exports each machine as data: `{entity, from, to, permission, own?: boolean, rules: RuleId[], sets: string[]}[]`. Commands look up the row for `(from, to)`; a pair that is not listed returns 409 `transition-not-allowed`. The RBAC suite (J.5) and the contract tests read the same tables.

| Entity (owner WP) | Transitions (from to to: permission; rules) |
|---|---|
| `applicability_entry.implementation_status` (WP-20) | null to `not_started`: `applicability.manage`. `not_started` to `planned`: `applicability.manage`. `not_started`, `planned`, `partially_implemented` to `complete`: `applicability.complete` (own); sets `completed_by_user_id`, `completed_at`. `planned` to `partially_implemented`: `applicability.complete` (own). `complete` to `implemented`: `applicability.verify`; BR-01, `VERIFY-SELF`; sets `verified_by_user_id`, `verified_at`. `complete` to `partially_implemented` (rejection): `applicability.verify`. `partially_implemented` to `implemented`: `applicability.verify`; BR-01. Any to `not_applicable`: `applicability.manage`; exclusion justification required. `implemented` to `partially_implemented`: system only (E.3) |
| `implementation_activity_progress.status` (WP-20) | `not_started` to `in_progress`: `activity.transition` (own); BR-12. `not_started` or `in_progress` to `complete`: `activity.transition` (own); BR-40 (A.4 5.12: from `not_started` writes two audit entries). `complete` to `verified`: `activity.verify`; `VERIFY-SELF`, A-48. `complete` to `in_progress`: `activity.verify`; reason required. Any except `verified` to `blocked`: `activity.transition` (own) or `activity.verify`; BR-39. `blocked` to `in_progress`: `activity.transition` (own) |
| `document.status` (WP-35) | `draft` to `in_review`: `document.review` (own). `expired` to `in_review`: `document.review` (own). `in_review` to `approved`: `document.approve`; BR-16. `in_review` to `draft` (rejection): `document.approve`; note required. `approved` to `expired`: system only (BR-10) |
| `risk_treatment.status` (WP-36) | `proposed` to `approved`: `risk.manage`. `approved` to `in_progress`: `risk.treatment.update` (own). `in_progress` to `delayed`: system (target date passed) or `risk.treatment.update` (own). `delayed` to `in_progress`: `risk.treatment.update` (own). `in_progress` or `delayed` to `implemented`: `risk.treatment.update` (own); sets `implemented_by_user_id`, `implemented_at`. `implemented` to `residual_accepted`: only through `POST .../residual-acceptance` with `risk.residual.accept` (the risk's owner, never the implementer; BR-04). `residual_accepted` to `closed`: `risk.manage`. Any open state to `proposed` (rework): `risk.manage` |
| `finding.status` (WP-37) | `open` to `in_progress`: `finding.manage`. `open` or `in_progress` to `closed`: `finding.manage`; BR-06 and BR-07 (major: `rca_text` and `systemic_check_text` filled), BR-08 (major and minor: at least one linked corrective action `closed` with `effective = true`); `ofi`, `signal` and `not_a_finding` close with a `close_note`. `closed` to `in_progress` (reopen): `finding.manage`; note required |
| `corrective_action.status` (WP-37) | `correction` to `rca` to `systemic_check` to `action` to `effectiveness_verification`, one step at a time: `corrective_action.update` (own) or `corrective_action.manage`; each step requires its text (`correction_text`, `rca_text`, `systemic_check_text`, `action_text`); entering `effectiveness_verification` sets `implemented_by_user_id` (actor) and `implemented_at`. `effectiveness_verification` to `closed`: `corrective_action.verify`; BR-09 (verifier differs from implementer and owner), BR-08 (`verified_at` differs from `implemented_at`); `effective = false` returns it to `action` instead. Any open state to `cancelled`: `corrective_action.manage`; note required |
| `management_review.status` (WP-37) | `planned` to `held`: `management_review.manage`; sets `held_at`. `held` to `closed`: `management_review.manage`; BR-13 (every mandatory `review_input_def` of every linked system has non-empty content), BR-14 (`decisions_recorded` and `decisions_text`) |
| `self_assessment.status` (WP-37) | Any to `not_assessed`, `conforming`, `gap`, `not_applicable`: `self_assessment.manage`; `gap` requires an owner and a target date |
| `audit_prep_task.status`, `cycle_requirement_status.status` (WP-35) | TS 6.7 and A-23: `not_started` to `in_progress`; `in_progress` to `done`; any to `blocked` (note required); `blocked` to `in_progress`; `overdue` to `in_progress` or `done`: `prep_task.update` and `cycle_item.update` (own). `not_started` or `in_progress` to `overdue`: system only |
| `user_ms_role.approval_status` (WP-39) | `pending_approval` to `active` or `rejected`: `user.manage`, actor differs from `requested_by` and from the grantee |
| `management_system.status` (WP-12) | TS 6.2: (new) to `setup`, `setup` to `implementing`, any to `archived`: `ms.admin`. `certified` to `transitioning` and back: `ms.transition` (MS Manager; `edition_transition` feature). `implementing` to `certified`: system, when the cycle is certified |

## D. API contract

### D.1 Conventions

| Topic | Rule |
|---|---|
| Base | `/api/v1`, JSON, same origin as the SPA (NGINX proxies `/api/` to the api service). All `/api/v1` routes are served by run mode `api` only |
| Path parameters | `{ms}` is the management system slug (`isms`); `{id}` is a UUID unless the table says otherwise; `{entityType}` is one value of `RECORD_ENTITY_TYPES` (`packages/shared/src/api/records.ts`: the chain and register tables `applicability_entry`, `document`, `evidence`, `implementation_activity_progress`, `context_issue`, `interested_party`, `asset`, `risk`, `risk_treatment`, `metric`, `quality_objective`, `process`, `prioritized_activity`, `business_impact_analysis`, `bc_plan`, `exercise`, `business_associate`, `chain_record`, `audit_prep_task`, `cycle_requirement_status`, `drift_result`, `finding`, `corrective_action`, `management_review`, `self_assessment`, `audit_activity`); anything else is 404 before a query runs. When a route has a `{ms}` path parameter, the system comes only from the path, never from the body or query |
| Records | Every command that names a record resolves it through `RecordResolver` (F.7): entity type from the allow-list, row by id, and the row must belong to the system of the path (its `management_system_id`, or an `entity_ms_link` row to that system for shareable types); otherwise 404 |
| Authentication | Session cookie (F.1). Unauthenticated requests get 401. Public routes are marked "public" |
| CSRF | Every non-GET request needs header `X-CSRF-Token` equal to the session's token (F.2). Public POST routes check `Origin` instead |
| Validation | Every route validates params, query and body with its zod schema from `packages/shared` before the handler runs; failures return 400 `validation` with `errors[]` |
| Errors | RFC 9457 `application/problem+json`. `type` is `https://mscd.cyberlentera.com/problems/<slug>`. Extension members: `rule` (BR id or `LIC-USERS`, `LIC-MS`, `LIC-FEATURE`, `LIC-BINDING`, `VERIFY-SELF`, `NOT-A-MEMBER`, `SOD-SELF-GRANT`), `messageKey`, `messages: {en, id}`, `ref`, `errors[]`, `requestId`, `retryAfter`, `affected` (BR-22, BR-38 confirmation lists), `methods` (`reauth-required`) |
| Problem slugs | `validation` 400, `unauthenticated` 401, `csrf` 403, `forbidden` 403, `read-only-mode` 403 (rule BR-37), `organisation-inactive` 403, `not-found` 404, `transition-not-allowed` 409, `confirmation-required` 409 (BR-22, BR-38), `reauth-required` 409, `conflict` 409, `precondition-failed` 412, `payload-too-large` 413, `business-rule` 422, `rate-limited` 429, `dependency-unavailable` 503, `internal` 500 |
| Warnings | Warn-and-allow rules (BR-03, BR-17 on assignment, BR-23, BR-30, BR-32, BR-36, BR-41, BR-47) return 200 with `warnings: [{rule, messages: {en, id}}]` |
| Command result | Mutations return `CommandResult` (D.2) with HTTP 200, or 201 for creations, or 202 when a job was queued |
| Idempotency | `Idempotency-Key` header accepted on every POST that creates something; the first response is stored for 24 hours in `platform.idempotency_key`, keyed by organisation, user and key, and replayed; `request_hash` covers the organisation id, method, path and body; a different request with the same key gives 409 `conflict` |
| Concurrency | Records carry an ETag in the snapshot (`refs[key].etag`, `W/"<updated_at epoch ms>"`). `If-Match` is optional on transitions and assignment; when present and stale, 412 |
| Lists | `page` (1-based) and `pageSize` (default and maximum 200); response `Page<T>`. Filter names everywhere: `status`, `q`, `owner`, `stage`, `frequency` |
| Language | `/ui/*` returns `[en, id]` pairs. TS 9.2 endpoints return one string in the language of `Accept-Language` (`id` or `en`) |
| Dates | `YYYY-MM-DD` for dates in the organisation time zone, ISO 8601 UTC for instants |
| Read-only mode | When the organisation licence is past grace (BR-37), every non-GET route except auth, logout, preferences and `PUT /admin/license` returns 403 `read-only-mode` |
| Organisation status | Business routes answer 403 `organisation-inactive` unless the session's organisation is `active`; `offboarding` allows `GET` routes and exports only. `TenantResolver` drops its cache entry on every status change (F.7) |
| Request id | `X-Request-Id` is accepted only when it matches `^[A-Za-z0-9-]{8,64}$`; otherwise the api generates a UUID. The value is echoed in the response header and stored in audit entries |
| Files | Every route that returns file bytes uses `FileResponder` (F.13) |
| Rate limits | F.3; headers `RateLimit-Limit`, `RateLimit-Remaining`, `RateLimit-Reset`, `Retry-After` |
| Contract source | `packages/shared/src/api/<area>.ts` exports `routes: RouteDef[]`; the server's `@ApiRoute(def)` decorator (WP-08) mounts the path, attaches the permission and the validation pipe. `npm run openapi` writes `packages/shared/dist/openapi.json` from the same table. WP-05 writes every route of D.3 into the area files; later WPs change only the area files they own |

```ts
// packages/shared/src/api/common.ts
export interface RouteDef {
  id: string;                         // 'applicability.transition'
  owner: `WP-${string}`;              // the WP that implements the route (contract and RBAC tests read it)
  method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH';
  path: string;                       // '/api/v1/ms/:ms/applicability/:id/transitions'
  auth: 'public' | 'session' | 'mfa_pending' | 'enrolment' | 'guest_otp';
  permission?: Permission;            // F.6; omitted for auth-only routes
  scope?: 'ms' | 'org' | 'platform';  // where the permission is checked
  params?: ZodType; query?: ZodType; body?: ZodType; response: ZodType;
  errors: number[];                   // status codes besides 400, 401, 403
  idempotent?: boolean;               // accepts Idempotency-Key
  readOnlyAllowed?: boolean;          // allowed in BR-37 read-only mode
  testOnly?: boolean;                 // mounted only when the profile enables test endpoints (D.3 Test support)
  file?: boolean;                     // returns bytes through FileResponder (F.13)
}
```

### D.2 Shared schemas

Every name below is a zod schema exported from `packages/shared`. Field lists are complete for request bodies; response schemas list their fields.

| Schema | Fields |
|---|---|
| `Pair` | `[string, string]` (English, Indonesian) |
| `IsoDate` | string `YYYY-MM-DD` |
| `RecordRef` | `module` (`implementation`, `audit`, `surveillance`, `storage`, `admin`), `entityType`, `id` (uuid), `code`, `etag?` |
| `Problem` | `type`, `title`, `status`, `detail?`, `rule?`, `messageKey?`, `messages?: {en, id}`, `ref?`, `errors?: {path, message}[]`, `requestId`, `retryAfter?`, `affected?: string[]` |
| `CommandResult` | `ok: true`, `changed: MsId[]`, `count?`, `message?: Pair`, `warnings?: {rule, messages}[]`, `redirectUrl?`, `downloadUrl?`, `taskId?`, `ref?: RecordRef`, `data?: unknown` |
| `Page<T>` | `items: T[]`, `page`, `pageSize`, `total` |
| `SystemProfile` | `version`, `revision`, `deploy` (`saas` or `onprem`, derived in I.1), `capabilities`: {`licenseValidation`, `updatesChannel`, `packsChannel`, `telemetry`, `identityProviders[]`, `storageAdapters[]`, `mailTransport`, `jobsDriver`}, `demo`: {`enabled`, `personas`, `fixedToday?`}, `login`: {`regionLabel?`, `mfaEnforced`} |
| `AuthProviders` | `local: boolean`, `providers: {slug, kind: 'oidc' or 'saml', label, icon: 'microsoft' or 'google' or 'generic'}[]`. With `tenancy.mode = multi` the providers of one organisation only, chosen by `?org=<slug>` or by the domain of `?email=` (domains in `platform.verified_domain`); without either, an empty list |
| `LoginRequest` | `email` (max 254), `password` (max 256), `remember: boolean` |
| `LoginResponse` | `status`: `ok`, `mfa_required` or `mfa_enrolment_required`; `me?: Me` when `ok` (local sign-in refused by the organisation policy answers 401 `unauthenticated` with `messageKey: 'auth.sso_required'` and `methods`, only after a correct password) |
| `MfaVerifyRequest` | exactly one of `code` (6 digits) or `recoveryCode` (`xxxx-xxxx-xx`) |
| `MfaEnrolStartRequest` | empty in state `mfa_enrolment`; from an active session (re-enrolment) exactly one of `currentCode` (6 digits) or `password`, unless the session verified a password or TOTP in the last 5 minutes (F.5) |
| `MfaEnrolStartResponse` | `secret` (base32), `otpauthUrl`, `issuer`, `account` |
| `MfaEnrolConfirmRequest` | `code` (6 digits) |
| `MfaEnrolConfirmResponse` | `recoveryCodes: string[10]`, `me: Me` |
| `PasswordResetRequest` | `email` |
| `PasswordResetConfirm` | `token`, `password` |
| `PasswordChangeRequest` | `currentPassword`, `newPassword` |
| `DemoPersonaRequest` | `role`: `manager`, `owner`, `auditor` or `top` |
| `Me` | `user: {id, handle, email, displayName, jobTitle}`, `org: {id, slug, name}`, `orgs: Organisation[]` (UI 1.1 shape), `rolesByMs: Record<MsId, BusinessRole[]>`, `uiRoleByMs: Record<MsId, UiRole>`, `adminRoles: ('tenant_admin' or 'storage_admin')[]`, `platformRoles: string[]`, `permissions: {org: Permission[], ms: Record<MsId, Permission[]>}`, `csrfToken`, `session: {authMethod, idleExpiresAt, absoluteExpiresAt}`, `personas?: Record<UiRole, {who: PersonId, label: Pair}>` (demo only), `preferences: Record<string, unknown>` |
| `SwitchOrgRequest` | `org` (slug). 409 `reauth-required` carries `methods: {local: boolean, providers: {slug, kind, label}[]}` (D-26) |
| `IdentityLinkStart` | response `{redirectUrl}` for `POST /me/identities/{slug}/link` (local session with MFA within 5 minutes) |
| `OrgPolicyPatch` | `localLogin`: `allowed`, `admins_only` or `disabled` |
| `GuestStartRequest` | `token` (from the URL fragment of the access link) |
| `GuestLoginRequest` | `email` (returning guest; a new code is sent when an active grant exists, the response is the same either way) |
| `GuestVerifyRequest` | `code` (6 digits, emailed, 10 minutes, 5 attempts) |
| `GuestStartResponse` | `status: 'code_sent'`, `emailHint` (first letter and domain) |
| `UiOrgSnapshot` | `today`, `generatedAt`, `orgs`, `MS: ManagementSystem[]`, `PEOPLE`, `ROLES`, `LOC: Record<slug, [name, kind, short]>`, `REPOS`, `ROUTING`, `PACKS`, `LICENCE`, `STAGES`, `CHAINS`, `TITLES`, `CLAUSE_AT_RISK`, `EXAMPLES`, `FREQ`, `FREQ_ORDER`, `permissions` (as in `Me`). Record shapes are the UI 4.2 interfaces; `LOC` colours and icons are added by the web from the repository kind |
| `UiMsSnapshot` | `ms: MsId`, `bundle: {reqs, docs, evs, chain: {items, recs}, gates, phases}` (UI 4.2 `MsBundle`, with `ChainItem.refKey` instead of `ref` and `Activity.docId` instead of `doc`), `X`, `EVENTS`, `FOCUS`, `PREP`, `PAST`, `CYCLE_ITEMS`, `DRIFT` for that system, `refs: Record<RecordKey, RecordRef>` |
| `TaskList` | `items: {key, ms, kind, id, title, act, due, st, after, stage}[]` (`kind` adds `task` for stored tasks, key `<ms>:task:<uuid>`), `counts: {total, overdue, week, later}` |
| `ApplicabilityTransitionRequest` | `to`: `not_started`, `planned`, `partially_implemented`, `complete`, `implemented`, `not_applicable`; `exclusionJustification?` (required for `not_applicable`), `reason?` |
| `ApplicabilityPatch` | any of `isApplicable`, `inclusionJustification`, `exclusionJustification`, `holdsGate`, `gateReason`, `statusNote`, `frequencyCode`, `ownerHandle`, `riskCodes[]`, `documentCodes[]` |
| `ApplicabilityCreateRequest` | `targetType`, `code` (catalogue item or requirement code of the active edition), `isApplicable`, `inclusionJustification?`, `exclusionJustification?`, `ownerHandle`, `frequencyCode` |
| `ActivityTransitionRequest` | `to`: `in_progress`, `complete`, `verified`, `blocked`; `blockerGateItemRef?` and `blockerReason?` (required for `blocked`), `outputDocumentCode?`, `rejectReason?` (verified to in_progress) |
| `AssignmentRequest` | `kind`: `req`, `soa`, `doc`, `ev`, `act`, `x`, `prep`, `cycle` or `drift` (the RecordKey kind; selects the fields of the WP-35 assignment table, so a SoA gate item never changes the control owner), `holder` (PersonId, `none` allowed), `dueAt: IsoDate or null` |
| `CompleteActionRequest` | `note?` |
| `DocumentTransitionRequest` | `to`: `in_review`, `approved`, `draft`; `note?` |
| `DocumentCreateRequest` | `docCode`, `title`, `docType`, `ownerHandle`, `approverHandle?`, `nextReviewAt?`, `classification`, `orgWide: boolean`, `linkedMs: MsId[]` |
| `DocumentPatch` | any of `title`, `ownerHandle`, `approverHandle`, `nextReviewAt`, `classification`; `confirm?: boolean` (BR-22) |
| `DocumentVersionRequest` | `source`: `link` or `upload`; `repository` (slug) and `externalItemId` for `link`; `uploadIntentId` for `upload` |
| `EvidenceFileRequest` | `source`: `link`, `upload` or `client_upload`; `repository` and `externalItemId` for `link`; `uploadIntentId` for the other two; `producedAt: IsoDate`; `mode`: `submit` or `relink` |
| `UploadIntentRequest` | `ms`, `kind`: `evidence` or `document`, `recordId` (uuid), `fileName`, `size`. No content type: the gateway detects it (F.13) |
| `UploadIntent` | `intentId`, `mode` (`stored`, `pass_through`, `zero_transit`), `repository: {slug, name, adapter}`, `contentUrl` (`/api/v1/storage/upload-intents/{id}/content`, not for zero-transit), `maxBytes`, `expiresAt`, `correlationCode`, `folderPath` |
| `UploadResult` | `storageObjectId`, `hashAlgorithm`, `hashValue`, `sizeBytes`, `versionLabel` |
| `PickerListing` | `path`, `files: [name, dateLabel, version, hashAlgorithm, modifiedBy][]`, `refs: {repositoryId, externalItemId, externalVersionId}[]` (UI 4.2 names; `repositoryId` is the repository slug) |
| `ReminderRequest` | `ms` (required; the record must belong to it), `ref: RecordRef` or `recordKey`, `recipient` (PersonId) |
| `BulkReminderRequest` | `ms`, `scope`: `open_chain_items` |
| `AuditEventCreateRequest` | `eventType`, `nameEn`, `nameId`, `shortLabel`, `scheduledAt`, `auditDays?`, `leadAuditorName?`, `expectedFocus?`, `prepWindowDays?` |
| `AuditEventPatch` | any of the create fields; `outcome?`, `outcomeSummary?` |
| `PrepTaskCreate` and `PrepTaskPatch` | `title`, `ownerHandle`, `dueAt`, `status`, `note` |
| `CycleItemPatch` | `status`, `note`, `ownerHandle`, `dueAt` |
| `RepositoryView` | UI 1.14 `REPOS` row fields plus `ref` (uuid), `adapter`, `modes`, `ownershipType`, `connectionStatus`, `retentionConfirmed`, `credentialSet: boolean`, `credentialExpiresAt?`, `config` (no secrets), `actions: ('test' or 'reauthorise' or 'moveToLibrary')[]` |
| `MoveRequest` | `ms` (system on whose behalf the move is asked; `storage.move.request` is checked there), `note?` |
| `RepositoryCreate` | `slug`, `name`, `adapter`, `modes[]`, `config` (per adapter; m365: `siteId`, `driveId` matching `^[A-Za-z0-9!_-]+$`, `rootFolderId`, `roots: {<ms slug>: {folderId, path}}`; google_drive: `driveId` matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$`, `rootFolderId`, `roots`, `oauthClientId?`; managed_vault: `prefix`), `ownershipType`, `metadataMinimization`, `retentionConfirmed`, `display?` |
| `RepositoryCredential` | `kind`, `secret` (object; per kind: `m365_certificate` {tenantId, clientId, certificatePem, privateKeyPem}; `google_service_account` {clientEmail, privateKeyPem}; `oauth_refresh_token` {clientId, clientSecret}, the refresh token itself only ever arrives through the OAuth consent flow) |
| `RepositoryTestResult` | `status`, `permissions[]`, `references`, `nextCheckAt`, `message: Pair` |
| `RoutingRuleRequest` | `ms?`, `classification?`, `objectKind?`, `repository`, `mode`, `priority`, `note?` |
| `PackSummary` | `packId`, `version`, `frameworkCode`, `edition`, `status`, `preRelease`, `installedAt`, `checksum`, `signatureKeyId` |
| `LicenceView` | `licenseId`, `organization`, `binding: {installationId?, organizationId?, trial}`, `validFrom`, `validUntil`, `graceDays`, `state` (derived), `clockRollback: boolean`, `frameworks[]`, `maxManagementSystems`, `maxUsers`, `activeUsers`, `managementSystems`, `storageAdapters[]`, `features[]`, `signature: {alg, keyId, verified, offline}` |
| `ManagementSystemCreate` | `slug`, `code`, `name`, `packId`, `edition` (an installed edition; activation copies the activity templates, D-30), `scopeStatement`, `defaultRepository`, `certificationBody?`, `color` |
| `MsTransitionRequest` | `to`: `transitioning` (with `targetEdition`) or `certified` (transition audit recommended) |
| `UserCreate` | `email`, `displayName`, `jobTitle?`, `adminRoles[]`. Response 202 `{status: 'invited'}` for a new account and for an existing account alike (no enumeration, F.6) |
| `UserPatch` | Membership fields `membershipStatus` (`active`, `disabled`) and `adminRoles[]`; global fields `displayName`, `jobTitle`, `accountStatus` only when the user has no other active membership (F.6) |
| `UserIdentityRegistration` | `provider` (slug), `subject` (Tenant Admin pre-registration, D-25) |
| `RoleGrant` | `user` (handle), `role`, `isPrimary?`, `validUntil?`. Response `CommandResult` with `data.approvalStatus` (`active` or `pending_approval`) |
| `IdentityProviderRequest` | `slug`, `kind`, `displayName`, `icon`, `oidc?: {issuer, clientId, clientSecret, scopes, subjectMode: 'sub' or 'entra', entraTenantId?}`, `saml?: {entryPoint, idpIssuer, idpCerts[], spEntityId, mfaAuthnContexts[]}`, `requireMfaClaim` (default true), `acceptedAmr[]`, `mfaFallback` (`local_totp` default, or `reject`), `jitProvisioning`, `allowedEmailDomains[]` (must be verified domains when `jitProvisioning`) |

| `AuditEntryMeta` | `AuditEntry` without `before`, `after` and `entityCode` (Tenant Admin view, `audit_trail.metadata.read`) |

| `AuditChainVerifyResult` | `chain`, `checked`, `firstBrokenSeq?`, `anchorOk: boolean` |

| `BackgroundTask` | `id`, `kind`, `status`, `progress`, `result?`, `error?`, `downloadUrl?` |
| `EvidencePackRequest` | `auditEventId?`, `scope?: {clauses?: string[], codes?: string[]}` |
| `EvidencePackView` | `id`, `status`, `packFolder`, `itemCount`, `exceptionCount`, `manifestUrls: {pdf, csv}`, `archiveUrl?`, `items[]` |
| `AuditorAccessRequest` | `email`, `expiresInDays` (1 to 30, default 14). Response `CommandResult` with `data.expiresAt`; the link is emailed to the auditor and never returned to the requester's browser |
| `CalendarFeed` | response `CommandResult` with `downloadUrl` (`/api/v1/ms/{ms}/surveillance/reviews.ics?token=...`, token shown once) |
| `Notification` | `id`, `kind`, `title: Pair`, `body: Pair`, `ref?`, `createdAt`, `readAt?` |
| `RegisterKind` | `context-issues`, `interested-parties`, `assets`, `risks`, `metrics` (path value of `{register}`) |
| `RegisterItem` | `ref`, `code`, `title`, `owner` (handle or `none`), `status?`, `updatedAt`, `shared: boolean`, `ms: MsId[]`, kind fields: context issue `issueType`, `description`, `lastReviewedAt`; interested party `requirements`, `lastReviewedAt`; asset `assetType`, `classification`; risk `riskProfileCode`, `isOpportunity`, `likelihood`, `consequence`, `impactDimensions`, `residualLikelihood`, `residualConsequence`, `treatmentOption`, `assessedAt`, `nextReviewAt`, `residualAcceptedAt`; metric `unit`, `targetValue`, `frequencyCode`, `lastMeasuredAt` |
| `RegisterCreate` and `RegisterPatch` | `code`, `title`, `ownerHandle` (required for risks, BR-17), the kind fields above, `linkedMs?: MsId[]` (shareable kinds; first is primary), `confirm?: boolean` (BR-22 on patch) |
| `TreatmentCreate` | `riskCode`, `code`, `title`, `ownerHandle`, `targetType`, `targetCode`, `targetDate` |
| `TreatmentPatch` | any of `title`, `ownerHandle`, `targetType`, `targetCode`, `targetDate` |
| `TreatmentTransition` | `to` (C.13), `note?` |
| `ResidualAcceptanceRequest` | `treatmentId?`, `residualLikelihood`, `residualConsequence`, `note` |
| `MetricValueRequest` | `periodStart`, `periodEnd`, `value`, `note?` |
| `OperatingCriteriaRequest` | `frequencyCode` (from `ref.evidence_frequency_rule`), `description?` |
| `EvidenceCreate` | `code`, `title`, `ownerHandle`, `frequencyCode`, `classification`, `targets: {targetType, code}[]` (at least one, in the path system) |
| `EvidencePatch` | any of `title`, `ownerHandle`, `frequencyCode`, `classification`, `nextDueOverride` |
| `EvidenceTargetRequest` | `ms` (target system, may differ from the path system when `integrated_mode` is licensed), `targetType`, `code`, `mappingNote?` |
| `EntityLinkRequest` | `entityType` (shareable types of C.6), `entityCode`, `isPrimary?`, `relevanceNote?` |
| `SoftDeleteRequest` | `reason` (required, 10 to 500 characters), `confirm?: boolean` (BR-22) |
| `FindingCreate` | `code`, `title`, `classification`, `clauseRef?`, `source` (`internal_audit`, `self_assessment`, `certification_audit`, `other`), `auditEventId?`, `ownerHandle`, `dueAt?` |
| `FindingPatch` | any of `title`, `classification`, `clauseRef`, `ownerHandle`, `dueAt`, `rcaText`, `systemicCheckText`, `statusNote` |
| `FindingTransition` | `to`: `in_progress` or `closed`; `note?` (`closeNote` for findings without corrective actions) |
| `CorrectiveActionCreate` | `findingIds[]` (one or more, each linked to the path system), `code`, `title`, `ownerHandle`, `dueAt?` |
| `CorrectiveActionTransition` | `to` (C.13), `text?` (the step text), `effective?` and `note?` (verification) |
| `SelfAssessmentUpdate` | `status`, `ownerHandle?`, `targetFixAt?`, `note?`, `evidenceCodes[]` |
| `AuditActivityPatch` | any of `auditeeHandle`, `scheduledAt`, `status`, `note` |
| `ManagementReviewCreate` | `code`, `title`, `plannedAt`, `ownerHandle`, `linkedMs: MsId[]` (the path system first, as primary) |
| `ManagementReviewInputRequest` | `content`, `sourceRefs?: RecordRef[]` |
| `ManagementReviewTransition` | `to`: `held` or `closed`; `heldAt?`, `decisionsText?` |
| `TestResetResponse` | `ok: true`, `durationMs` |
| `TestSessionRequest` | `handle`, `org?` (slug, default the user's default organisation). Response: sets the session cookie; body `Me` |
| `OpsHealth` | `components: {name, status, detail}[]`, `migrations: {schema, latest, expected}[]`, `queues`, `licences: {org, state, validUntil}[]` |

### D.3 Endpoints

Permissions are the codes of F.6; "(own)" means a role holding only an own-scoped grant may act only on records whose owner or holder is the session user (for create routes: the new record's owner must be the session user). The owning WP of every route is in its `RouteDef.owner`.

**System, session and identity** (WP-11 unless stated)

| Method and path | Auth or permission | Request | Response | Errors |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /system/profile` | public | | `SystemProfile` | |
| `GET /auth/providers?org=&email=` | public | | `AuthProviders` (D.2 rules for multi-tenant mode) (WP-16) | |
| `POST /auth/login` | public, Origin check | `LoginRequest` | `LoginResponse`, sets cookie | 401 `unauthenticated` (one generic message for unknown user, wrong password, disabled user, locked account; `auth.sso_required` only after a correct password), 429 |
| `POST /auth/mfa/verify` | session in `mfa_pending` | `MfaVerifyRequest` | `LoginResponse` | 401 wrong code, 429 (per user second-factor lock, F.3) |
| `POST /auth/mfa/enrol/start` | session in `mfa_enrolment`, or active session (re-enrolment) | `MfaEnrolStartRequest` | `MfaEnrolStartResponse` | 401 proof missing or wrong (F.5) |
| `POST /auth/mfa/enrol/confirm` | same | `MfaEnrolConfirmRequest` | `MfaEnrolConfirmResponse` (re-enrolment revokes the user's other sessions) | 401 wrong code |
| `POST /auth/logout` | any session | | 204, cookie cleared | |
| `POST /auth/password/reset-request` | public, Origin check | `PasswordResetRequest` | 202 (always; no email for users whose memberships all forbid local sign-in) | 429 |
| `POST /auth/password/reset` | public, Origin check | `PasswordResetConfirm` | 204 (revokes every session of the user) | 400 weak password or invalid token |
| `POST /auth/password/change` | session | `PasswordChangeRequest` | 204 (revokes the user's other sessions) | 401 wrong current password |
| `POST /auth/invitations/accept` | public, Origin check | `{token, password?}` (set a password) | `LoginResponse` | 400 invalid or expired invitation |
| `GET /auth/oidc/{slug}/start?returnTo=&invitation=` | public | | 302 to the IdP (WP-16) | 404 unknown or disabled provider |
| `GET /auth/oidc/{slug}/callback` | public | IdP query | 302 to `returnTo` or `/login?error=<code>` (WP-16) | |
| `GET /auth/saml/{slug}/start?returnTo=&invitation=` | public | | 302, HTTP-Redirect binding (WP-19) | 404 |
| `POST /auth/saml/{slug}/acs` | public (IdP form post, no CSRF header; binding cookie required, F.9) | `SAMLResponse`, `RelayState` | 302 (WP-19) | |
| `GET /auth/saml/{slug}/metadata` | public | | SP metadata XML (WP-19) | |
| `POST /auth/demo/persona` | session of a demo user, demo mode | `DemoPersonaRequest` | `Me` (new session cookie) (WP-39) | 404 when demo mode is off |
| `POST /auth/guest/start` | public, Origin check | `GuestStartRequest` | `GuestStartResponse`, sets the guest binding cookie (WP-26) | 401 used, expired or revoked link, 429 |
| `POST /auth/guest/login` | public, Origin check | `GuestLoginRequest` | `GuestStartResponse` (WP-26) | 429 |
| `POST /auth/guest/verify` | guest binding cookie (`auth: 'guest_otp'`) | `GuestVerifyRequest` | `LoginResponse` (`mfa_enrolment_required` on first use, else `mfa_required`) (WP-26) | 401, 429 |
| `GET /me` | session (active) | | `Me` (WP-39) | |
| `PUT /me/org` | session | `SwitchOrgRequest` | `Me` (WP-39) | 404 not a member, 409 `reauth-required` (D-26) |
| `POST /me/memberships/{org}/accept` | session | | `Me` (accepts an invited membership) (WP-39) | 404 |
| `POST /me/identities/{slug}/link` | session with password or TOTP verified in the last 5 minutes | | `IdentityLinkStart` (WP-16; SAML providers WP-19) | 401 recent proof missing, 404 |
| `PUT /me/preferences` | session | `Record<string, unknown>` (max 16 KiB) | 204 | |
| `GET /notifications?unread=` | session | | `Page<Notification>` (WP-24) | |
| `POST /notifications/{id}/read` | session (recipient) | | 204 (WP-24) | 404 |

**UI parity reads** (WP-17)

| Method and path | Permission | Response | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /ui/org` | session with any role in the organisation | `UiOrgSnapshot` | `REPOS` and `ROUTING` only with `storage.repository.read`; `PACKS` entries only with `pack.read`; `LICENCE` only with `license.read`, else `null`; `TITLES`, `CHAINS`, `CLAUSE_AT_RISK` and `PEOPLE` limited by `ScopePolicy` (G.5) |
| `GET /ui/ms/{ms}` | `ms.read` | `UiMsSnapshot` | `ScopePolicy` applied: owner scoping (D-04), classification rules, the external auditor projection (D-34) |
| `GET /tasks` | session | `TaskList` | All systems of the organisation where the user holds a role (PRD 5.4); merges stored tasks (D-29) |

**Read model per screen, TS 9.2** (WP-32). All `GET`, permission `ms.read`, every result filtered by `ScopePolicy`, paginated where a list is returned.

| Path | Query | Response |
|---|---|---|
| `/ms/{ms}/implementation/summary` | | `ImplementationSummary`: five cards (implemented, partial, gate items, documents approved, documents expired), phases with derived text, maturity per clause |
| `/ms/{ms}/applicability` | `status`, `group`, `q`, `owner`, `page` | `Page<ApplicabilityItem>` (requirement fields of UI 1.5 plus `ref`, `lastEvidence`, `integrity`) |
| `/ms/{ms}/documents` | `status`, `type`, `q`, `owner`, `due_within`, `page` | `Page<DocumentItem>` |
| `/ms/{ms}/gates` | | `GateItem[]` with `affected` refs |
| `/ms/{ms}/phases/{n}/activities` | | `ActivityItem[]` |
| `/ms/{ms}/audit/event/next` | | `NextAuditEvent` (event plus the four readiness numbers) or 204 |
| `/ms/{ms}/audit/stages` | | `StageReadiness[]` (rec, open, pending, overdue, after, ready, pct, next, question) |
| `/ms/{ms}/audit/stages/{key}/records` | `status`, `q`, `owner`, `page` | `Page<PendingRecord>` (TS 9.3 shape, with `ref.code`) |
| `/ms/{ms}/audit/chain` | `anchor_id` | `ChainResponse` (nodes with status, holder, due, clause at risk); nodes outside the user's scope are returned as `{stage, hidden: true}` |
| `/ms/{ms}/audit/pending` | `owner`, `stage`, `overdue_only`, `aggregate` | `PendingPerson[]` or `Page<PendingRecord>` when `owner` is given; everyone's list needs `audit.pending.all`; `aggregate=unit` needs `audit.pending.aggregate` and returns counts per stage and business role without names (units arrive with `org_unit`, Appendix 1) |
| `/ms/{ms}/surveillance/events/{id}` | | `SurveillanceEvent` |
| `/ms/{ms}/surveillance/cycle` | `cycle_id` | `CycleItem[]` |
| `/ms/{ms}/surveillance/drift` | `rule` | `DriftPattern[]` |
| `/ms/{ms}/evidence` | `freshness`, `frequency`, `q`, `owner`, `page` | `Page<EvidenceItem>` |

**Commands from the UI** (WP-20: applicability, activities, phases; WP-35: the rest; WP-21: files). Every command resolves records through `RecordResolver`, checks person fields with `MembershipService`, writes the audit trail (F.11) in the same transaction and returns `changed` with the systems whose bundles must be reloaded.

| Method and path | Permission | Request | Response | Errors and rules |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /ms/{ms}/applicability/{id}/transitions` | C.13 (`applicability.complete` (own), `applicability.verify`, `applicability.manage`) | `ApplicabilityTransitionRequest` | `CommandResult` | 409 not in C.13; 412; 422 BR-01 (`entryEvidenceState`, E.3), BR-02, `VERIFY-SELF` |
| `PATCH /ms/{ms}/applicability/{id}` | `applicability.manage` | `ApplicabilityPatch` | `CommandResult` | 422 BR-02, `NOT-A-MEMBER`; warning BR-03 |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/applicability` | `applicability.manage` | `ApplicabilityCreateRequest` | 201 `CommandResult` | 409 duplicate target |
| `PUT /ms/{ms}/applicability/{id}/operating-criteria` | `applicability.manage` | `OperatingCriteriaRequest` | `CommandResult` (enqueues `freshness.recompute`) (WP-36) | 422 unknown frequency |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/activities/{id}/transitions` | C.13 (`activity.transition` (own), `activity.verify`) | `ActivityTransitionRequest` | `CommandResult` | 409; 422 BR-12, BR-39, BR-40, `VERIFY-SELF`; warning BR-47 |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/phases/{n}/verify` | `phase.verify` | | `CommandResult` (`message` "Phase n is already verified" when nothing to do) | 422 BR-12 (not all activities verified) |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/records/{entityType}/{id}/assignment` | `record.assign` | `AssignmentRequest` | `CommandResult` | 404; 412; 422 BR-43 (prep tasks), `NOT-A-MEMBER`; warnings BR-41, BR-17 (risk holder set to none) |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/records/{entityType}/{id}/complete-action` | `record.complete_action` (own) | `CompleteActionRequest` | `CommandResult` | 409 no open action |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/records/{entityType}/{id}/delete` | `record.delete` | `SoftDeleteRequest` | `CommandResult` (audit `record.deleted` with the reason) | 409 `confirmation-required` (BR-22); 422 the record still has open dependants (for example a risk with open treatments) |
| `POST /tasks/{id}/complete` | session; the task's owner | `CompleteActionRequest` | `CommandResult` | 404; 409 not open |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/documents/{id}/transitions` | C.13 (`document.review` (own), `document.approve`) | `DocumentTransitionRequest` | `CommandResult` | 409; 422 BR-16 |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/documents` | `document.manage` | `DocumentCreateRequest` | 201 `CommandResult` | 409 duplicate code; 422 unknown `doc_type` lookup, `NOT-A-MEMBER` |
| `PATCH /ms/{ms}/documents/{id}` | `document.manage` | `DocumentPatch` | `CommandResult` | 409 `confirmation-required` (BR-22, `affected` lists systems); 422 `NOT-A-MEMBER` |
| `POST /reminders` | `reminder.send` in `body.ms` (the record must belong to it) | `ReminderRequest` | `CommandResult` (`count: 1`) | 404; 422 BR-50 (`retryAfter`, last sent time in `messages`), `NOT-A-MEMBER` |
| `POST /reminders/bulk` | `reminder.send` in `body.ms` | `BulkReminderRequest` | `CommandResult` (`count` sent, `data.skipped` throttled) | |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/audit-events` | `audit_event.manage` | `AuditEventCreateRequest` | 201 `CommandResult` | |
| `PATCH /ms/{ms}/audit-events/{id}` | `audit_event.manage` | `AuditEventPatch` | `CommandResult` (`count` holders notified, BR-42) | |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/audit-events/{id}/prep-tasks` | `audit_event.manage` | `PrepTaskCreate` | 201 `CommandResult` | 422 BR-43 |
| `PATCH /ms/{ms}/prep-tasks/{id}` | `prep_task.update` (own) | `PrepTaskPatch` | `CommandResult` | 409 (C.13); 422 BR-43 |
| `PATCH /ms/{ms}/cycle-items/{id}` | `cycle_item.update` (own) | `CycleItemPatch` | `CommandResult` | 409 |
| `GET /storage/repositories/{slug}/browse?ms=&kind=&recordId=&path=` | `evidence.file.submit` or `document.version.create` (own) in `ms`, checked against the record `recordId` | | `PickerListing` (only below the system's root folder of the repository, D.5) | 404 repository without a root for `ms`; 422 BR-29 |
| `POST /storage/upload-intents` | `evidence.file.submit` or `document.version.create` (own) | `UploadIntentRequest` | 201 `UploadIntent` | 413; 422 BR-29 |
| `PUT /storage/upload-intents/{id}/content` | creator of the intent | raw body (`application/octet-stream`), streamed | `UploadResult` | 409 intent not open or expired; 413; 422 BR-29 |
| `POST /storage/upload-intents/{id}/complete` | creator of the intent | `{externalItemId}` | `UploadResult` | 409; 422 item outside the system root, older than the intent, created by another account, or without the correlation code (D.5) |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/evidence/{id}/files` | `evidence.file.submit` (own) | `EvidenceFileRequest` | `CommandResult` | 409 intent mismatch; 422 BR-29, item outside the system root; warnings BR-23, BR-30, BR-36 |
| `POST /ms/{ms}/documents/{id}/versions` | `document.version.create` (own) | `DocumentVersionRequest` | `CommandResult` (document now `in_review`) | 409 a version is already in review; 422 BR-29 |
| `GET /storage/objects/{id}/open?ms=` | `storage.object.read` in `ms`, and `ScopePolicy.canReadObject` (F.7) | | `{url, expiresAt}`: vault and pass-through give the `/content` URL; link and zero-transit give a short-lived api redirect (`/redirect`); external auditors get pack copies only | 404; 422 BR-35 |
| `GET /storage/objects/{id}/redirect?t=` | session; `t` is an HMAC (`platform.key`) over user, object and expiry, valid 5 minutes; permission re-checked at use | | 302 to the provider URL from the gateway (`open-url`); never sent to external auditors | 403 expired or foreign token |
| `GET /storage/objects/{id}/content?ms=&inline=` | same as `open` | | file stream through `FileResponder` (F.13) (Managed Vault and pass-through only) | 404; 422 BR-35 |

**Test support** (WP-27). `RouteDef.testOnly: true`. Mounted only when the profile has `demo.enabled: true` and `test.endpoints_enabled: true` (test profile only; the release flavour rejects both keys at start-up). Every call is logged at `warn`.

| Method and path | Auth | Request | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /test/reset` | public, Origin check | | `TestResetResponse`. Drains the api's request queue, closes the app and pack pools, drops and recreates the database from the template database named by `test.template_database` (`DROP DATABASE ... WITH (FORCE)`, then `CREATE DATABASE ... TEMPLATE ...` through `test.admin_database_url_file`), reopens the pools, resets the mock providers (`POST /_admin/reset` with the demo fixtures) and clears in-memory caches (tenant resolver, licence, discovery) |
| `POST /test/session` | public, Origin check | `TestSessionRequest` | `Me` with a new active session cookie (`auth_method = 'test'`, no MFA) |

**Health and metrics** (every run mode, outside `/api`)

| Path | Port | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /healthz` | service port | Process is alive |
| `GET /readyz` | service port | api and worker: database reachable, migrations at the expected version, schema privileges safe (C.3), queue driver ready. storage-gateway: key provider loaded, vault bucket reachable when enabled |
| `GET /metrics` | 9464 (separate listener, not exposed by the ingress) | Prometheus text format |

### D.4 Run modes

| Mode | Listens on | Serves | Uses (secret files mounted, I.3) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `api` | 3000 (HTTP), 9464 (metrics) | All `/api/v1` routes | PostgreSQL as `mscd_app`, job queue (producer), storage-gateway client. Secrets: `db-app.url`, `platform.key`, `gateway.token`, `ca.crt`, `api.crt`, `api.key` |
| `worker` | 9464 (health and metrics only) | Job handlers of E, schedules, outbox relay (BullMQ driver) | PostgreSQL as `mscd_app` and, for `pack.install` and `packs.lifecycle`, as `mscd_pack_installer`; storage-gateway client; SMTP. Secrets: the api set plus `db-pack.url`, `audit-anchor.key`, `smtp.password` when used (worker certificate `CN=mscd-worker`) |
| `storage-gateway` | 8443 (HTTPS with mTLS; plain HTTP only when `gateway.tls.mode: off`, which start-up refuses unless `demo.enabled`), 9464 | `/v1/*` of D.5 | `storage.key`, S3 endpoint, provider endpoints. Secrets: `storage.key`, `s3.access`, `s3.secret`, `gateway.token` (and `gateway.token.previous` during rotation), `ca.crt`, `gateway.crt`, `gateway.key`. No database credentials |
| `migrate` | none | Runs C.3 and exits | Secrets: `db-migrator.url`, `db-pack.url` only |

### D.5 Storage gateway protocol

- Base URL `https://storage-gateway:8443/v1`. Requests carry `Authorization: Bearer <gateway token>` and, with `gateway.tls.mode = mtls`, a client certificate signed by the installation CA (`CN=mscd-api` or `CN=mscd-worker`). The gateway accepts the token in `gateway.token_files[0]` and, during rotation, the one in `gateway.token_files[1]`; `mscd-server keys rotate-gateway-token` writes a new current file and keeps the old one as previous until the next rotation.
- Every request body includes `tenant: {orgId, wrappedDek}` and, where a repository is involved, `repository: {id, slug, adapter, modes, config, credential?: {ref, sealedBlob, kind}}`. The gateway keeps no state except an in-memory cache of unwrapped data keys (five minutes), and unwraps the data key with AAD `dek:<orgId>`.
- Credential binding (D-08): a credential is sealed with AAD `cred:<orgId>:<repositoryId>:<sha256 of canonicalJson(config without display fields)>`. On every call the gateway recomputes the AAD from `tenant.orgId`, `repository.id` and `repository.config`; a mismatch is 403 `forbidden` (`credential-binding`). Changing a repository's drive, site or root therefore needs a new credential.
- Root enforcement: every item the gateway reads, links, lists, copies or verifies must lie under `config.rootFolderId`; calls that act for one management system also pass `scopeRoot` (the system's folder from `config.roots[<ms>]`, chosen by the api) and the item must lie under it (`outside-root` otherwise). Item and drive ids are validated before use (Microsoft Graph `^[A-Za-z0-9!_-]+$`, Google Drive `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$`) and every path segment is passed through `encodeURIComponent`; query strings are built with `URLSearchParams`, never by concatenation.
- File types: on `PUT /v1/objects` and `register-client-upload` the gateway detects the content type from the first 4 KiB (magic numbers for PDF, PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, ZIP-based Office formats, plain text; anything else `application/octet-stream`) and returns it as `detectedMimeType`; the api stores only that value (F.13).
- Audit of use: every call is logged by the gateway as one JSON line `{event: 'gateway.call', orgId, repositoryId, adapter, operation, callerCn, outcome, latencyMs, requestId}` (ARCHITECTURE 10.4 "one place to look").
- Responses are JSON; errors use problem details with slugs `bad-request`, `not-found`, `forbidden`, `outside-root`, `unreachable`, `rate-limited` (with `Retry-After`), `unsupported`, `too-large`, `internal`.

| Endpoint | Request | Response | Adapters |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /v1/keys/data-key` | `{orgId}` | `{wrappedDek, kekId}` | none |
| `POST /v1/credentials/seal` | `{tenant, repository, kind, secret}` | `{sealedBlob, keyId, configSha256}` | none |
| `POST /v1/credentials/oauth-exchange` | `{tenant, repository, credential, code, redirectUri, codeVerifier}` | `{sealedBlob, keyId, configSha256, externalAccount}`; the gateway exchanges the code with the client secret from the sealed credential, checks that the new token can read `config.driveId` and `config.rootFolderId`, then seals the refresh token; the api never sees it | google_drive |
| `POST /v1/test-connection` | `{tenant, repository}` | `{status, permissions[], ownership, message: {en, id}, latencyMs, credentialExpiresAt?}` | all |
| `POST /v1/list` | `{tenant, repository, scopeRoot, folderPath?, pageToken?}` | `{path, items: ObjectMetadata[], nextPageToken?}` | all |
| `POST /v1/ensure-folder` | `{tenant, repository, path}` | `{folderId, path}` | all |
| `PUT /v1/objects` | header `X-MSCD-Put` (base64 JSON `{tenant, repository, scopeRoot, folderPath, fileName, maxBytes, metadataMinimization}`), streamed body | `PutResult`: `externalItemId`, `externalVersionId`, `versionLabel`, `hashAlgorithm: 'sha256'`, `hashValue`, `hashSource: 'computed'`, `sizeBytes`, `detectedMimeType`, `providerCreatedAt`, `webUrl?`, `folderPath`, `encryption?` | managed_vault, m365 (pass-through), google_drive (pass-through) |
| `POST /v1/link` | `{tenant, repository, scopeRoot, externalItemId}` | `ObjectMetadata` (current version pinned) | m365, google_drive |
| `POST /v1/register-client-upload` | `{tenant, repository, scopeRoot, externalItemId, intent: {folderPath, notBefore, correlationCode, creatorEmail}}` | `ObjectMetadata`; rejects items created before `notBefore`, created by another account than `creatorEmail` (Graph `createdBy.user.email`, Drive `owners[].emailAddress` or the creating `lastModifyingUser.emailAddress` of the first revision), or whose name or description lacks `correlationCode` | m365, google_drive |
| `POST /v1/stat` | `{tenant, repository, object: {externalItemId, externalVersionId, hashAlgorithm, hashValue, hashSource, folderPath}}` | `StatResult`: `outcome` (`found`, `not_found`, `forbidden`, `unreachable`, `rate_limited`), `detail?` (`credential_invalid`), `current?: ObjectMetadata`, `pinnedVersionAvailable`, `pinnedHash?`, `folderPath?`, `retryAfterSeconds?` | all |
| `POST /v1/pin-version` | `{tenant, repository, object}` | `{result: 'ok' or 'unsupported'}` | google_drive (`keepForever`); others `unsupported` |
| `POST /v1/open-url` | `{tenant, repository, object}` | `{url}` (the provider URL; the api never returns it directly, it answers with its own short-lived redirect, D.3) | m365, google_drive; vault returns 422 (the api streams vault content itself) |
| `POST /v1/stream` | `{tenant, repository, object}` | decrypted byte stream with `Content-Length` and `X-MSCD-Detected-Type` | managed_vault; pass-through repositories |
| `POST /v1/copy-within` | `{tenant, repository, objects[], targetFolderPath}` | `{items: {externalItemId, newItemId, location}[]}` | all (m365 polls the async copy monitor URL) |
| `POST /v1/rehash` | `{tenant, repository, object}` | `{hashValue, matches}` | managed_vault |
| `POST /v1/anchors` | `{tenant: null, objectKey, body}` (worker only, `CN=mscd-worker`) | `{location}`; writes an audit anchor file to the vault bucket under `audit-anchors/` with S3 object lock when the bucket supports it | managed_vault bucket |

`ObjectMetadata`: `externalItemId`, `externalVersionId`, `versionLabel`, `name`, `mimeType` (provider-reported), `detectedMimeType?`, `sizeBytes`, `hashAlgorithm`, `hashValue`, `hashSource`, `providerCreatedAt`, `providerModifiedAt`, `modifiedBy`, `createdByEmail?`, `folderPath`, `folderId`, `webUrl`.

Adapter calls used against Microsoft Graph v1.0 (base URL configurable, default `https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0`; token endpoint `https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant}/oauth2/v2.0/token` with a certificate client assertion, scope `https://graph.microsoft.com/.default`): `GET /drives/{drive-id}/items/{item-id}`, `GET /drives/{drive-id}/items/{item-id}/versions`, `GET /drives/{drive-id}/items/{item-id}/children`, `GET /drives/{drive-id}/root:/{path}`, `POST /drives/{drive-id}/items/{parent-id}/children` (folder), `PUT /drives/{drive-id}/items/{parent-id}:/{name}:/content` (up to 4 MiB), `POST /drives/{drive-id}/items/{parent-id}:/{name}:/createUploadSession` (larger), `POST /drives/{drive-id}/items/{item-id}/copy` (202 with `Location` monitor). Hash: `file.hashes.quickXorHash`.

Calls used against Google Drive v3 (base `https://www.googleapis.com`, token `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token` with a service account JWT, scope `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/drive`; or a refresh token with `drive.file`): `GET /drive/v3/files/{id}?fields=...&supportsAllDrives=true`, `GET /drive/v3/files?q='<folder>' in parents and trashed=false&corpora=drive&driveId=...&includeItemsFromAllDrives=true&supportsAllDrives=true`, `GET /drive/v3/files/{id}/revisions/{revisionId}`, `PATCH /drive/v3/files/{id}/revisions/{revisionId}` (`keepForever`), `POST /upload/drive/v3/files?uploadType=resumable&supportsAllDrives=true`, `POST /drive/v3/files/{id}/copy`, `POST /drive/v3/files` (folder). Hash: `sha256Checksum`; none for Google-native files (`application/vnd.google-apps.*`).

## E. Worker jobs

### E.1 Queue interface

```ts
// apps/server/src/common/jobs/job-queue.ts (WP-08 creates the interface and an in-memory driver for tests;
// WP-22 adds the pg-boss and BullMQ drivers)
export interface JobEnvelope<P> { orgId: string | null; requestId?: string; requestedBy?: string; payload: P }
export interface SendOptions { singletonKey?: string; startAfterSeconds?: number; retryLimit?: number; priority?: number }
export interface JobQueue {
  send<N extends JobName>(name: N, env: JobEnvelope<JobPayloads[N]>, opts?: SendOptions, trx?: Transaction<DB>): Promise<string | null>;
  schedule(name: JobName, cron: string, env: JobEnvelope<unknown>, tz?: string): Promise<void>;
  work<N extends JobName>(name: N, opts: { concurrency: number },
    handler: (job: { id: string; env: JobEnvelope<JobPayloads[N]>; attempt: number }) => Promise<void>): Promise<void>;
  stats(): Promise<{ name: string; waiting: number; active: number; failed: number; oldestAgeSeconds: number }[]>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}
export const JOB_QUEUE = Symbol('JOB_QUEUE');
```

| Driver | Transactional send | Schedules | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pgboss` (default) | `boss.send(name, data, { singletonKey, startAfter, retryLimit, retryBackoff: true, db: { executeSql: (text, values) => trx.executeQuery(CompiledQuery.raw(text, values)) } })`, so the job commits with the business transaction | `boss.schedule(name, cron, data, { tz })` | Every queue is created with `boss.createQueue(name)` at worker start. Tables in schema `pgboss`, created by `migrate` (C.3) |
| `bullmq` | Inserts into `platform.job_outbox` inside the transaction; an outbox relay (running in `api` and `worker`) moves rows to BullMQ every 500 ms with `jobId = 'ob-' + id` (no duplicates) using `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT 100` | `queue.upsertJobScheduler(name, { pattern: cron, tz }, { data })` | Valkey URL from `jobs.bullmq.url` |

Rules for every handler: idempotent (safe to run twice), declares `scope: 'tenant' | 'installation'` in `@JobHandler`, runs inside `withTenant(orgId, SYSTEM_USER)` for tenant jobs and `withPlatform` for installation jobs, writes audit entries with `actor_kind = 'system'`, logs `jobId`, `orgId`, `attempt`, and updates its `background_task` row when one exists. Handlers are classes decorated with `@JobHandler('name', { concurrency })` and discovered by the worker through Nest's `DiscoveryService`, so modules register handlers without editing a central file. `mscd-server worker --queues a,b` limits a process to selected queues.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `scheduler.tick` (WP-22) | cron | `5 * * * *` UTC | none | `tick:<utc hour>` | Advances `platform.clock_mark.high_water` to `now()` when later (D-15); for each active organisation computes the local hour and enqueues the daily jobs due in that hour (keys below include the local date) | 3 |
| `freshness.recompute` (WP-22) | Commands that change evidence, files, operating criteria or applicability status; `link-health.check` results | 5 second debounce (`startAfterSeconds: 5`) | `{msId?, evidenceIds?, entryIds?}` | `fr:<org>:<ms or all>` | E.3; opens and closes BR-11 and BR-31 tasks (D-29) | 3 |

New matches get a `drift_result` row (holder = record owner, `due_at = today + 30`, BR-48) and a `drift_detected` notification to the holder (digest); open rows that no longer match get `resolved_at` (BR-49). `manual` rules keep their stored rows | 3 |
