Risks get `risk_profile_code` from the stage, owner = primary MS Manager when the sample gives none, and `assessed_at` = today minus 30 days, except the risks listed in the rows of a `stale_risk` drift rule, which get today minus 400 days so `drift.evaluate` finds exactly the seeded rows. Treatments that are ready get `status = 'implemented'`, `implemented_by_user_id` = their owner, and target the first catalogue item or requirement of the system.

### G.8 Mock provider fixtures

`tools/mock-providers/fixtures/demo.json` (written by the export script) contains, per repository:

- `sp` (SharePoint drive `drive-sp-compliance`, root folder `Compliance` with one subfolder per system): one item per seeded storage object of that repository with the same id, version, quickXorHash, folder, modified date and `createdBy` email of the evidence owner; items whose sample integrity is `missing` are absent; `changed` items have a newer current version while the pinned version is still listed; plus the `PICKER_FILES.sp` files under `Compliance / ISMS / Evidence / 2026 / Q3`.
- `od` (OneDrive drive `drive-od-fnugroho`): every request returns 403 (account disabled), so items are `access_denied`.
- `gd` (Shared Drive `drive-gd-bcms`): items and `PICKER_FILES.gd` files; the token endpoint answers `invalid_grant` for the seeded credential (expired delegated token), so checks give `verification_pending` as in the sample.

The mock server loads the fixture at start (`--fixtures demo`) and offers an admin API (`/_admin/*`, J.7) for tests to change state.

### G.9 Seed acceptance

- `GET /ui/org` and `GET /ui/ms/{ms}` for `ndt`, `bsd` and `rmn` as `rina` equal the exported snapshots under the comparison rules of J.4.
- Running every job of E.2 once on the seeded data (with `demo.fixed_today = 2026-09-29` and the mock providers running) leaves the comparison green, including `GET /tasks` counts (stored tasks merge into existing chain items) and the `DRIFT` rows (`drift.evaluate` finds exactly the seeded `stale_risk` rows and resolves none).

## H. Web integration

### H.1 Files

| Path | Owner | Content |
|---|---|---|
| `apps/web/src/data/mscd-data.js` | WP-07 | Sample module. WP-07 moves `T`, `TERA`, `INTEG` and the `LOC` colours and icons into `static.js` and re-exports them from here, so existing imports keep working |
| `apps/web/src/data/static.js` | WP-07 | `T`, `TERA`, `INTEG`, `LOC_VISUALS` (colour and icon per repository kind `sp`, `od`, `gd`, `vault`, `smb`), `ROLE_LABELS`. Imported by both sources; has no sample records |
| `apps/web/src/data/dates.js` | WP-07 | `makeDates(today)` returning `{TODAY_S, P, iso, dd, fmt, addD}` bound to `today` (the helpers of UI 1.19); both sources use it |
| `apps/web/src/data/sample-orgs.js` | WP-07 | `ORGS` moved out of the controller getter |
| `apps/web/src/data/sample-derive.js` | WP-07 | `data`, `chain`, `gateItems`, `phases`, `findX`, `titleOf` helpers and `overviewPhase` moved from the controller, parameterised by `(D, ov, msObj)`; the only change is that `gateItems` returns `type` as a `Pair` (G.5) |
| `apps/web/src/data/types.ts` | WP-07 | The UI 4.2 interfaces (types only) plus `UiRole`, `Permission`, `GateItem.type: Pair` |
| `apps/web/src/data/SampleDataSource.js` | WP-07 | Sample source (H.3) |
| `apps/web/src/data/createDataSource.js` | WP-07 (sample), WP-25 (api) | `async createDataSource()`: `import.meta.env.VITE_DATA_SOURCE === 'api'` loads `./api/ApiDataSource.ts` with a dynamic `import()`, otherwise `./SampleDataSource.js` with a dynamic `import()`, so the API bundle contains no sample records (checked by WP-25 acceptance) |
| `apps/web/src/data/api/ApiClient.ts` | WP-38 | `fetch` wrapper (H.4), shared by the auth pages and the API source |
| `apps/web/src/data/api/ApiDataSource.ts`, `wire.ts` | WP-25 | API source (H.4) |
| `apps/web/src/auth/*.tsx`, `auth.css` | WP-38 | Login, MFA, enrolment, password reset, invitation and guest pages (H.6) |
| `apps/web/src/main.jsx` | WP-38 | Boot sequence (H.7) |
| `apps/web/src/MscdApp.jsx` | WP-07, WP-25 | Controller changes (H.5) |
| `apps/web/vite.config.js` | WP-38 | `/api` proxy for `dev` and `preview` (`VITE_API_TARGET`, default `http://localhost:3000`) and the `@mscd/shared` alias |
| `apps/web/vitest.config.js` | WP-07 | `test: {include: ['test/**/*.test.{js,ts}'], environment: 'node'}` |

The data modules (`mscd-data.js`, `static.js`, `dates.js`, `sample-orgs.js`, `sample-derive.js`, `SampleDataSource.js`) import nothing from React, CSS or the browser, so Node can import them (export script, server parity tests).

### H.2 Interface

The interface is UI 4.2 with these additions:

```ts
export interface DataSource {
  readonly kind: 'sample' | 'api';
  load(orgId?: string): Promise<Snapshot>;                       // api: GET /me, /ui/org, then /ui/ms/{ms} for every system in parallel
  reload(snapshot: Snapshot, msIds: MsId[]): Promise<Snapshot>;  // api: refetch /ui/ms/{ms} for msIds and /ui/org
  listPickerFiles(key: RecordKey, src: string): Promise<PickerListing>;
  // commands of UI 4.2 unchanged: markComplete, verify, assign, remind, remindAll, completeRecordAction,
  // startReview, recordBlocker, linkFile, uploadFile, repositoryAction, pageAction
  switchPersona(role: UiRole): Promise<Snapshot>;                // sample: local; api: POST /auth/demo/persona then load
  switchOrg(orgId: string): Promise<Snapshot>;                   // api: PUT /me/org then load; 409 reauth-required -> login
  logout(): Promise<void>;                                       // sample: no-op
  onUnauthenticated(listener: () => void): () => void;           // api: any 401
}
export interface Snapshot { /* UI 4.2 Snapshot */ me?: MeView; permissions?: { org: Permission[]; ms: Record<MsId, Permission[]> } }
```

### H.3 Sample source (WP-07)

- `load(orgId)` returns the module data plus `today = TODAY_S`, `orgs = ORGS` and `bundles` from `sample-derive.js` for every system of the organisation, computed with the source's own override store.
- Each command applies exactly the override patch the controller applies today (UI 3.2 to 3.6) and returns `{ok: true, changed: [msId]}` with the counts of UI 4.4. Promises resolve in the same microtask turn.
- `switchPersona` and `switchOrg` only return a recomputed snapshot; the controller keeps its current state handling.
- Optional fields of UI 2.14 are filled on the sample objects from the controller's current literals (`ms.gateName`, `ms.gtSuggestions`, `ms.approver` (the per-system approver literal, also copied to each `doc.approver`), `ms.banner.kind`, `ms.audit.prepWindowDays` = 60, `PACKS[ms].status`, `doc.approver`, `doc.path`, `doc.hashAlg`, `e.hash`, `e.lastGoodCheck`, `e.lastCheck`, `REPOS[].adapter`, `REPOS[].ownershipType`, `REPOS[].actions`), and the controller reads each field with the literal as fallback (H.5 step 11), so the view model does not change.

### H.4 API source (WP-25)

- `ApiClient` (WP-38): `fetch` with `credentials: 'same-origin'`, JSON, header `X-CSRF-Token` from the last `Me`, `Accept-Language` from the controller language. A problem response becomes `CommandResult.error = {status, rule, message: [messages.en, messages.id]}` (fallback `[title, title]`). A 401 triggers `onUnauthenticated` listeners; a 409 `reauth-required` is returned with its `methods`.
- Snapshot assembly: `/ui/org` and every `/ui/ms/{ms}` are merged into the UI 4.2 `Snapshot` keys; `LOC` entries get colour and icon from `LOC_VISUALS` keyed by kind; `ChainItem.refKey` and `Activity.docId` are rehydrated to the objects of the same bundle (`it.ref.st`, `a.doc.st` keep working); `refs` is kept as a map from `RecordKey` to `RecordRef`.
- Command mapping:

| Method | Request |
|---|---|
| `markComplete(key)` | kind `req`: `POST /ms/{ms}/applicability/{ref.id}/transitions {to: 'complete'}`; kind `act`: `POST /ms/{ms}/activities/{ref.id}/transitions {to: 'complete'}` |
| `verify(key, to)` | same paths with `{to: 'implemented'}` or `{to: 'verified'}` |
| `assign(key, h, due)` | `POST /ms/{ms}/records/{ref.entityType}/{ref.id}/assignment {kind: <kind of key>, holder: h, dueAt: due}` with `If-Match: ref.etag` |
| `remind(key, h)` | `POST /reminders {ms, ref, recipient: h}` |
| `remindAll(ms)` | `POST /reminders/bulk {ms, scope: 'open_chain_items'}` |
| `completeRecordAction(key)` | kind `task`: `POST /tasks/{id}/complete {}`; kinds `x`, `drift`: `POST /ms/{ms}/records/{entityType}/{id}/complete-action {}`; kind `soa` never reaches the source (H.5 step 13) |
| `startReview(key)` | `POST /ms/{ms}/documents/{id}/transitions {to: 'in_review'}` |
| `recordBlocker(key, gateRef, reason)` | Not called in API mode: the UI has no form yet, so the controller keeps today's toast in both modes (Appendix 1). The source method exists and maps to `POST /ms/{ms}/activities/{id}/transitions {to: 'blocked', blockerGateItemRef, blockerReason}` for the later form |
| `listPickerFiles(key, src)` | `GET /storage/repositories/{ms.loc}/browse?ms=&kind=evidence or document&recordId=<ref.id>`; the result has the `PickerListing` shape with `refs[].repositoryId` |
| `linkFile(key, file)` | evidence: `POST /ms/{ms}/evidence/{id}/files {source: 'link', repository: file.ref.repositoryId, externalItemId, producedAt: today, mode: integ unverifiable ? 'relink' : 'submit'}`; document: `POST /ms/{ms}/documents/{id}/versions {source: 'link', repository, externalItemId}` |
| `uploadFile(key, file)` | `POST /storage/upload-intents`, `PUT` the file to `contentUrl` (streamed, with progress), then the evidence or document command with `source: 'upload'` and `uploadIntentId` |
| `repositoryAction(repoId, action)` | Looks up `REPOS` by `id` (slug) and uses its `ref` (uuid): `test` `POST /admin/storage/repositories/{ref}/test`; `reauthorise` `POST .../{ref}/reauthorize` then follows `redirectUrl`; `moveToLibrary` `POST .../{ref}/move-requests {ms: <selected system>}` |
| `pageAction(ms, action)` | `exportBoard`: `POST /exports {type: 'portfolio_board', format: 'pdf'}`; `exportReadiness`: `POST /exports {type: 'readiness', ms, format: 'pdf'}`; `exportSoa` and `exportDocs`: open the GET export URL; `evidencePack`, `auditorAccess` (the toast text stays; the link is emailed, F.14), `runCheck`, `calendarFeed`, `checkUpdates`, `importBundle` (file input), `verifyPhase`: the endpoints of D.3 |

- After a successful command the source calls `reload(snapshot, result.changed)`; the controller shows the toast after the reload, so the screen and the toast agree.

### H.5 Controller changes

Steps 1 to 4, 7, the loading panel of step 9, 11 (fallbacks) and the `GateItem.type` change are WP-07 (sample mode, no visible change apart from a one-frame loading panel); the rest is WP-25 (API mode).

1. Constructor: `this.ds = props.dataSource`; otherwise `componentDidMount` awaits `createDataSource()`. Until the first snapshot arrives `render()` returns the loading panel of step 9. `componentDidMount` calls `ds.load()` and stores `D` as the snapshot merged with `T`, `TERA`, `INTEG` (from `static.js`), `LOC` visuals, `PICKER_FILES` (sample mode only) and `makeDates(snapshot.today)` (`TODAY_S`, `P`, `iso`, `dd`, `fmt`, `addD`). Every controller read of `D.INTEG`, `D.TODAY_S`, `D.P`, `D.iso`, `D.dd`, `D.fmt` and `D.addD` therefore works in both modes.
2. `ORGS` reads `D.orgs`; `setOrg` calls `ds.switchOrg(id)` and then the current state reset.
3. `data(ms)`, `chain(d)`, `gateItems(d)`, `phases(d)` read `D.bundles[ms.id]` (sample mode computes them in `sample-derive.js`). Gate rows apply `this.li(g.type)`.
4. Every `ovSet` call site becomes the matching command (UI 3.1 list); `state.ov` is removed from the controller.
5. State after every `load`, `reload` of `/ui/org` and `switchOrg` (API mode): `state.org = D.me.org.slug`; `state.ms` stays when it is in `D.orgs[current].ms`, else the first system of that list; `state.role = D.me.uiRoleByMs[state.ms]`, also after every system switch. The `get org()` fallback `'ndt'` and the initial `org`, `ms` and `role` values are used only in sample mode. The persona menu (`roleOptions`) lists `D.ROLES` (demo personas) and, outside demo mode, the current role plus a `Sign out` entry that calls `ds.logout()`.
6. `canVerify` and `canSeePeople`: sample mode keeps today's role checks; API mode reads `D.permissions.ms[ms]` (`applicability.verify` or `activity.verify`, `audit.pending.all`).
7. `openPicker` tests the repository kind `D.LOC[ms.loc][1]` instead of the id; in API mode it awaits `listPickerFiles` and stores the listing in `modal.listing`; `modalVals` and `modalOk` both read `m.listing ?? D.PICKER_FILES[m.src]` (today `modalOk` reads `D.PICKER_FILES` directly).
8. Upload confirm in API mode clicks a hidden `<input type="file">` rendered by `MscdApp.render()` next to `AppView`; the chosen file goes to `uploadFile`.
9. Loading and busy states: before the first snapshot `render()` returns a full-page loading panel (same background and font as the app, text "Loading MSCD"); while a command runs, `state.busy` shows a 3 px progress bar at the top of the viewport (fixed position, outside `AppView`) and further commands queue behind it.
10. Errors: `result.error` shows `li(error.message)` as a toast (the existing toast element); `result.warnings` are appended to the success toast; network failures show "Connection lost. Try again." (`Koneksi terputus. Coba lagi.`).
11. Hard-coded switches of UI 2.14 read data with today's literal as fallback: `gateName` from `ms.gateName`; Golden Thread suggestions from `ms.gtSuggestions`; portfolio attention text, tag and target from `ms.banner.kind` (`connector` to storage, `transition` to the framework screen, `beta` to the framework screen) instead of the system id; `fwScreen` transition and beta flags from `PACKS[ms].status`; document storage facts from `doc.path`, `doc.hashAlg`, `doc.approver`; the fingerprint from `e.hash` (shortened as today) and the last check text from `e.lastGoodCheck` and `e.lastCheck`; storage actions from `REPOS[].actions` instead of the ids `gd` and `od`; vault type text from `REPOS[].adapter`; the planned-event window from `ms.audit.prepWindowDays`.
12. Absent licence and pack data: `fwScreen` renders `-` for every `LICENCE` field and an empty feature list when `D.LICENCE` is `null`, and `-` for a missing `PACKS[ms.id]` (A.4).
13. My Tasks in API mode: a `soa` task's button opens the gate item exactly like a gated `req` task (`go('controls', {tab: {controls: 'gate'}})`), because clearing a SoA gate needs a risk-based justification the UI cannot collect (BR-02). A `task` item's button calls `completeRecordAction`. Sample mode keeps today's behaviour (J.6 tags the affected test).
14. Counts in toasts use `result.count` when present (UI 4.6 step 8).

### H.6 Login and account pages (WP-38)

Port of `project/MSCD Login.dc.html` into React components with the same structure, colours, spacing and animations (`apps/web/src/auth/LoginPage.tsx` and `auth.css`):

- Left brand panel exactly as the design (grid background, animated chain path, headline, three facts). The footer line shows `SystemProfile.version`, the deployment label (`SaaS` or `On-Premise`, with `login.regionLabel` when set), `TLS 1.3` and `MFA enforced` when `login.mfaEnforced`.
- Right panel: SSO buttons from `GET /auth/providers` (in multi-tenant mode after the email field is filled, with `?email=`; icon `microsoft` or `google` as in the design, generic otherwise; hidden when no provider), the "or with email" divider only when both exist, email and password fields (empty, with the design's placeholders), "Keep me signed in on this device for 12 hours" (unchecked by default), Continue button, the second-factor note, the auditor guest note and the "Forgot?" link.
- No Google Fonts link: IBM Plex comes from the existing `@fontsource` packages; the logo comes from `apps/web/public/assets`.
- Pages: `/login` (sign in), `/login/mfa` (6-digit code with automatic submit on the sixth digit, "Use a recovery code" switch), `/login/enrol` (QR code rendered with `qrcode` to SVG, the secret in groups of four, confirm code, then the ten recovery codes with Copy and "I saved them" before continuing; from an active session it first asks for the current code or password), `/forgot-password`, `/reset-password#token=`, `/invite#token=` (set a password or continue with one of the organisation's providers), `/guest#<token>` (F.14: posts the token, removes the fragment, then the emailed code, then enrolment or TOTP; "Send me a code" for returning guests). Errors appear in a red note above the form, in English or Indonesian following `?lang=` or the browser language.
- Error codes from `/login?error=` (`no_account`, `mfa_required_by_policy`, `idp_error`, `expired`, `invitation_invalid`, `sso_required`) map to messages; `reauth-required` from an organisation switch shows the accepted methods.

### H.7 Boot and session handling (WP-38)

- `main.jsx` in API mode: `GET /system/profile`, then `GET /me`. On 401 it renders the auth router; after login it renders `MscdApp` with the API source. In sample mode it renders `MscdApp` immediately (today's behaviour; the sample E2E suite does not change).
- A 401 during use stores the current URL in `sessionStorage`, shows the login page with "Your session has ended. Sign in again." and returns to the stored URL afterwards.
- Display settings in the URL (`drill`, `density`, `layout`, `font`, `lang`, `theme`) keep working; `deploy` in the URL stays a demo override.
- Builds: `npm run build -w @mscd/web` builds sample mode (used by the sample E2E project); `VITE_DATA_SOURCE=api npm run build -w @mscd/web` builds the production bundle used by the `mscd-web` image.

## I. Deployment

### I.1 Deployment profile

The server loads `MSCD_PROFILE_FILE` (YAML, validated by a zod schema at start; the process exits with a list of errors when invalid), then applies environment overrides: `MSCD__<SECTION>__<KEY>=value` (double underscore separates levels, values parsed as YAML scalars), and `*_FILE` variables read secrets from files. The schema is strict: unknown keys are errors (an old profile with `license.trusted_keys` or `packs.trusted_keys` fails at start with "trusted keys are compiled into the build (D-16)"). Release-flavour builds reject `demo.enabled: true`, every `test.*` key, `identity.oidc.allow_insecure_http: true`, `gateway.tls.mode: off` and `kms.provider` other than `file`.

```yaml
# apps/server/profiles/onprem.yaml (saas.yaml, demo.yaml and test.yaml differ as noted)
tenancy:   { mode: single, isolation: database }          # saas: multi, schema
license:   { validation: offline }                        # saas: online (D-32); trusted keys are compiled in (D-16)
updates:   { channel: offline }
packs:     { channel: offline, dir: /app/packs, channel_url: null }
telemetry: { enabled: false, otlp_endpoint: null }
kms:       { provider: file, file: { path: /run/secrets/platform.key, previous_paths: [] } }   # only `file` (D-09)
identity:
  providers: [local, oidc, saml]
  local: { mfa_required: true }
  oidc: { allow_insecure_http: false }                    # demo and test: true
  session: { idle_minutes: 60, absolute_hours: 8, remember_hours: 12, guest_idle_minutes: 30, guest_absolute_hours: 4 }
mail:      { transport: smtp, from: "MSCD <mscd@example.internal>", smtp: { host: smtp, port: 25, secure: false, user: null, password_file: null } }
storage:
  adapters: { enabled: [managed_vault, m365, google_drive] }
  max_upload_bytes: 104857600
  gateway: { url: "https://storage-gateway:8443", token_file: /run/secrets/gateway.token,
             tls: { mode: mtls, ca_file: /run/secrets/ca.crt, cert_file: /run/secrets/client.crt, key_file: /run/secrets/client.key } }
network:   { egress_allowlist: [] }
jobs:      { driver: pgboss, schedules_enabled: true, bullmq: { url: null } }
http:      { port: 3000, public_url: "https://mscd.example.internal", trust_proxy: { cidrs: ["172.31.250.10/32"] } }   # Helm: { cidrs: [<ingress controller pod CIDR>] }
security:  { cookie_secure: true }
database:  { url_file: /run/secrets/db-app.url, pack_installer_url_file: /run/secrets/db-pack.url, pool_max: 10 }
audit:     { anchor_key_file: /run/secrets/audit-anchor.key }       # read by the worker only
observability: { log_level: info, metrics_port: 9464 }
demo:      { enabled: false, acknowledge_insecure: false, fixed_today: null, personas: false, keycloak: null }
test:      { endpoints_enabled: false, template_database: null, admin_database_url_file: null }   # dev flavour only
gateway:                                                  # read only by run mode storage-gateway
  port: 8443
  token_files: [/run/secrets/gateway.token, /run/secrets/gateway.token.previous]   # the second may be absent
  kms: { provider: file, file: { path: /run/secrets/storage.key, previous_paths: [] } }
  tls: { mode: mtls, ca_file: /run/secrets/ca.crt, cert_file: /run/secrets/gateway.crt, key_file: /run/secrets/gateway.key }
  vault: { endpoint: "http://objectstore:8333", region: us-east-1, bucket: mscd-vault, force_path_style: true,
           access_key_file: /run/secrets/s3.access, secret_key_file: /run/secrets/s3.secret }
  providers:
    m365: { graph_base_url: "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0", login_base_url: "https://login.microsoftonline.com" }
    google: { api_base_url: "https://www.googleapis.com", token_url: "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token" }
```

- Shorthand environment variables: `MSCD_DATABASE_URL` (role `mscd_app`), `MSCD_DATABASE_MIGRATOR_URL`, `MSCD_DATABASE_PACK_URL` and `MSCD_DATABASE_ADMIN_URL` (bootstrap only). They win over the profile's `*_file` entries.
- Secret files are read lazily by the module that needs them, and each container gets only its own files (I.3, I.4), so a run mode starts without the secrets of other run modes. `test.yaml` and `demo.yaml` point to `apps/server/test/fixtures/secrets/` (development values, committed, R-11).
- `SystemProfile.deploy` is `onprem` when `license.validation = offline`, else `saas` (a presentation label derived from capabilities; no code branches on it).
- `saas.yaml`: `tenancy.mode: multi`, `license.validation: online`, `packs.channel: managed`, `kms.provider: file` with the KEK paths of the Kubernetes Secrets (D-09), `http.trust_proxy: {cidrs: ["10.0.0.0/8"]}` (the cluster pod range; Helm sets it from `api.trustedProxyCidrs`).
- `demo.yaml` (dev flavour only): `demo.enabled: true`, `acknowledge_insecure: true`, `fixed_today: 2026-09-29`, `personas: true`, `security.cookie_secure: false`, `identity.oidc.allow_insecure_http: true`, gateway `tls.mode: off`, provider base URLs pointing at `mock-providers`, `mail.smtp.host: mailpit`, port 1025, `demo.keycloak: {issuer: "http://keycloak:18088/realms/mscd", client_id: mscd, client_secret_file: /run/secrets/keycloak-client.secret}`.
- `test.yaml` (dev flavour only): as demo plus `jobs.schedules_enabled: false`, `observability.log_level: warn`, `http.trust_proxy: {hops: 0}`; the API E2E stack also sets `test.endpoints_enabled: true`, `test.template_database` and `test.admin_database_url_file` (J.6).
- The gateway refuses non-default provider base URLs unless their host is in `network.egress_allowlist` or demo mode is on.

### I.2 Images

| Image | Dockerfile | Build | Runtime |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cyberlentera/mscd-server` (target `release`, the default) | `deploy/docker/server.Dockerfile` | Every `RUN` with npm mounts `build_ca` (R-12). Stages: `manifests` (all `package.json` files), `deps` (`npm ci`), `build` (`MSCD_BUILD_FLAVOR=release MSCD_TRUSTED_KEYS_DIR=/trusted-keys npm run build -w @mscd/shared -w @mscd/pack-tools -w @mscd/server`, with the named build context `trusted-keys`; then `node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js build-all --sign-key-file /run/secrets/pack_signing_key --out /packs`, with the build secret `pack_signing_key` and no default; the stage fails when the secret is missing or its key id is in `dev-key-ids.json`), `prod-deps` (`npm ci --omit=dev -w @mscd/server -w @mscd/shared`) | `node:22-alpine`, user `10001:0`, read-only root filesystem, `WORKDIR /app`, entrypoint `node apps/server/dist/main.js`, default command `api`. Contains `apps/server/dist`, `apps/server/migrations`, `apps/server/profiles/{onprem,saas}.yaml`, `packages/shared/dist`, `/app/packs/*.pack`, production `node_modules`. No `demo.yaml`, `test.yaml`, seed JSON, `dev-keys` or test fixtures. Labels as the web image. `HEALTHCHECK` none (orchestrators probe `/readyz`) |
| `cyberlentera/mscd-server-demo` (target `demo`) | same file | `MSCD_BUILD_FLAVOR=dev`, trusted keys and pack signing from `packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/` | As release plus `profiles/{demo,test}.yaml` and `apps/server/src/seed/demo/*.json`. Demo overlay, smoke test and API E2E only; never in the offline bundle |
| `cyberlentera/mscd-web` | `deploy/docker/web.Dockerfile` (moved by WP-01) | Adds build argument `VITE_DATA_SOURCE=api` | NGINX config adds the `/api/` proxy (I.2.1) |
| `cyberlentera/mscd-mock-providers` | `deploy/docker/mock-providers.Dockerfile` | `npm run build -w @mscd/mock-providers` (with `build_ca`) | Demo and test only, never in the offline bundle |

**I.2.1 NGINX changes (WP-18).** `site.conf` keeps the GET-only rule for static paths and adds:

```nginx
location /api/ {
    include /tmp/mscd/api-upstream.conf;        # written at start: proxy_pass http://<MSCD_API_UPSTREAM>;
    proxy_http_version 1.1;
    proxy_set_header Host $host;
    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $remote_addr;   # overwrite: the client's own header is never forwarded
    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    proxy_set_header X-Request-Id $request_id;
    proxy_request_buffering off;                 # streamed uploads
    proxy_buffering off;
    client_max_body_size 110m;
    proxy_read_timeout 300s;
    add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
}
```

`40-mscd-runtime.sh` writes `api-upstream.conf` from `MSCD_API_UPSTREAM` (default `api:3000`); when it is set to `none` the location returns 404 (web-only use). When `MSCD_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS` is set (Helm: the ingress controller ranges), it writes `set_real_ip_from` lines plus `real_ip_header X-Forwarded-For; real_ip_recursive on;`, so `$remote_addr` is the client behind the ingress. `nginx.conf` defines `log_format mscd` that logs `$uri` and `$mscd_args_redacted` (a `map` chain replacing the values of `token`, `code`, `state`, `t`, `SAMLResponse`, `RelayState` and `invitation`) instead of `$request`, and uses it for the access log. `/healthz` stays as is.

### I.3 Docker Compose

**Developer stack** `deploy/compose/dev/compose.yml` (WP-02), project name `mscd-dev`, ports from `.env`:

| Service | Image | Ports (host) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `postgres` | `postgres:16.15-alpine` | 55432 | `POSTGRES_PASSWORD` from `.env`; init script `postgres/init/00-roles.sql` runs the C.2 bootstrap with development passwords and database `mscd` |
| `objectstore` | `chrislusf/seaweedfs:4.48` | 58333 | `weed mini -dir=/data -s3.config=/etc/seaweedfs/s3.json -bucket=mscd-vault`; `s3.json` holds the development access key `mscd-dev` and secret `mscd-dev-secret-0123456789` with `Admin`, `Read`, `Write`, `List` |
| `valkey` | `valkey/valkey:8.1-alpine` | 56379 | profile `bullmq` |
| `keycloak` | `keycloak/keycloak:26.7.5` | 58080 | profile `sso`; `start-dev --import-realm --health-enabled=true`; realm file `keycloak/realm-mscd.json` |
| `mailpit` | `axllent/mailpit:v1.31.3` | 51025 (SMTP), 58025 (UI and API) | |

Realm `mscd`: users `rina`, `bagus`, `maya`, `hendra` (fixed user ids of G.3) and `sso.only` with the G.3 emails and password `Mscd-Demo-2026!`, `emailVerified: true`; confidential OIDC client `mscd` (secret `mscd-dev-oidc-secret`, valid redirect URIs `*` because tests use random ports (development realm only), standard flow, PKCE S256 required, direct access grants on for tests); SAML client `mscd-dev-sp` (client id equals the SP entity id, valid redirect URIs `*`, signed assertions, NameID format persistent, `email` attribute mapper).

**On-premise stack** `deploy/compose/docker-compose.yml` (WP-28), project `mscd`. Network `mscd` with subnet `${MSCD_SUBNET:-172.31.250.0/24}`; `web` has the fixed address `172.31.250.10` (F.12).

| Service | Image | Command | Depends on | Secrets (Compose `secrets:`, files under `<install dir>/secrets/`) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `postgres` | `postgres:16.15-alpine` | default | | profile `bundled-db` (on by default through `COMPOSE_PROFILES` in `.env`); volume `pgdata`; init SQL from `config/postgres/`; `postgres-admin.password` |
| `objectstore` | `chrislusf/seaweedfs:4.48` | `weed mini ...` | | profile `bundled-s3`; volume `s3data`; `s3.json` |
| `valkey` | `valkey/valkey:8.1-alpine` | default | | profile `bullmq` |
| `migrate` | `mscd-server` | `migrate` | postgres healthy | `restart: "no"`; `db-migrator.url`, `db-pack.url`; installs packs from `/app/packs` |
| `storage-gateway` | `mscd-server` | `storage-gateway` | | `storage.key`, `s3.access`, `s3.secret`, `gateway.token`, `ca.crt`, `gateway.crt`, `gateway.key`; the only service with an outbound network when cloud adapters are enabled |
| `api` | `mscd-server` | `api` | migrate completed, storage-gateway healthy | `db-app.url`, `platform.key`, `gateway.token`, `ca.crt`, `api.crt` and `api.key` (mounted as `client.crt`, `client.key`) |
| `worker` | `mscd-server` | `worker` | migrate completed, storage-gateway healthy | the api set with `worker.crt` and `worker.key`, plus `db-pack.url`, `audit-anchor.key`, `smtp.password` when configured |
| `web` | `mscd-web` | default | api healthy | none; existing hardening kept; ports 80 and 443 |

All application containers: `read_only: true`, `tmpfs: /tmp`, `cap_drop: [ALL]`, `security_opt: [no-new-privileges:true]`, `init: true`, `user: 10001:0` (web keeps its UID), resource limits from `.env`, JSON log rotation, healthchecks with `wget -q -O /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:<port>/readyz`. Secret files are mode 0600, owned by the run user, and each service lists only its own secrets, so `storage.key` exists only inside `storage-gateway` and `platform.key` never inside it.

**Demo overlay** `deploy/compose/docker-compose.demo.yml` switches `migrate`, `storage-gateway`, `api` and `worker` to the `mscd-server-demo` image, adds `mock-providers`, `mailpit` and `keycloak`, mounts the demo profile, uses the development secrets committed in `deploy/compose/config/demo-secrets/` (development only, gateway `tls.mode: off`, R-11), and runs `mscd-server seed demo` as a one-shot `seed` service after `migrate`. Keycloak has one URL for the browser and the containers: it listens on 18088 inside the container (`KC_HTTP_PORT=18088`, `KC_HOSTNAME=http://keycloak:18088`), is published as `${MSCD_KEYCLOAK_PORT:-18088}:18088`, the api reaches it as `keycloak:18088` on the Compose network, and the smoke browser maps the name `keycloak` to 127.0.0.1 with Chromium's `--host-resolver-rules="MAP keycloak 127.0.0.1"` (J.9). The full-stack smoke test uses this overlay with project `mscd-smoke` and ports 18080, 18443 and 18088.

`install.sh` (WP-28) additionally: generates secrets when missing (`openssl rand` for passwords, the gateway token and the two KEKs; `mscd-server keys generate` inside a one-off container for the anchor key pair; an internal CA and certificates for `storage-gateway`, `api` and `worker` with `openssl req` and `openssl x509`), writes `secrets/db-*.url`, loads the application images and the bundled third-party images from the bundle, runs `docker compose up -d`, waits for `web` and `api` health, creates the organisation (`docker compose run --rm api tenant create --slug <slug> --name <name>`), and prints the URL and the first-admin command (`docker compose run --rm api admin create-user --email ... --tenant-admin`, which prints a one-time invitation link). `upgrade.sh` loads the new images, runs `docker compose run --rm migrate`, then `docker compose up -d` (rolling restart of api, worker, gateway, web).

### I.4 Helm chart `deploy/helm/mscd` (WP-29)

| Template | Content |
|---|---|
| `configmap-profile.yaml` | Profile YAML from `values.profile` |
| `secret-*.yaml` | Only when `secrets.create: true` (CI values); one Secret per component: `mscd-api` (`db-app.url`, `platform.key`, `gateway.token`), `mscd-worker` (the api keys plus `db-pack.url`, `audit-anchor.key`), `mscd-gateway` (`storage.key`, `s3.access`, `s3.secret`, `gateway.token`), `mscd-migrate` (`db-migrator.url`, `db-pack.url`). Production references `existingSecret` names per component in the same split |
| `certs.yaml` | Internal CA and service certificates generated with `genCA` and `genSignedCert`, kept across upgrades with `lookup`; replaced by cert-manager `Certificate` resources when `certManager.enabled` |
| `deployment-web.yaml`, `deployment-api.yaml`, `deployment-worker.yaml`, `deployment-gateway.yaml` | One Deployment per component, each mounting only its own Secret, `securityContext` restricted (non-root, read-only root filesystem, drop all capabilities, `RuntimeDefault` seccomp), probes on `/healthz` and `/readyz`, resources from values, `topologySpreadConstraints` in HA values. Web gets `MSCD_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS` from `ingress.trustedCidrs` |
| `job-migrate.yaml` | Helm hook `pre-install,pre-upgrade`, weight -5, `mscd-server migrate`, `backoffLimit: 1`, `ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 3600`, mounts only `mscd-migrate` |
| `services.yaml`, `ingress.yaml` | Ingress routes `/` and `/api` to web (NGINX proxies `/api`, F.12); api and gateway services are cluster-internal |
| `networkpolicies.yaml` | Default deny; ingress controller to web; web to api (the only ingress of api); api and worker to PostgreSQL, Valkey, gateway; gateway to S3 and to `network.egress_allowlist` CIDRs or all egress on 443 when `gateway.egressAll: true` |
| `pdb.yaml`, `hpa.yaml` | api and worker |
| `serviceaccount.yaml` | `automountServiceAccountToken: false` |
| `tests/test-connection.yaml` | Helm test: GET `/healthz` on web and `/api/v1/system/profile` |
| `values.yaml`, `values-ha.yaml`, `values-ci.yaml` | `values-ci.yaml` enables `testServices` (single-replica PostgreSQL and SeaweedFS Deployments with emptyDir, test only), `secrets.create: true`, demo profile with the `mscd-server-demo` image |
| `ci/validate.sh` | Static checks run by WP-29 here and by CI: `helm lint`, `helm template` for the three values files, kubeconform 0.7.0 in `alpine/k8s:1.34.1` (`-strict -kubernetes-version 1.34.1 -summary`, schema cache in `ci/.schema-cache`), then `ci/assert-render.mjs` (J.1) |
| `ci/k3s-test.sh` | Live install, migrate hook, `helm test` and one upgrade in `rancher/k3s:v1.34.1-k3s1`; nightly CI job only (A.3) |

The existing `deploy/helm/mscd-web` chart is removed by WP-29 (its hardening settings move into the web Deployment).

### I.5 Offline bundle and install guide (WP-33)

- `build-bundle.sh` takes `--version`, `--build-ca <file>` (default `$MSCD_BUILD_CA`), `--trusted-keys-dir <dir>` and `--pack-signing-key <file>` (both required; release keys never come from the repository) and `--sign-key` for `SHA256SUMS`. It builds `mscd-web` (API mode) and the `release` target of `mscd-server`, saves them plus `postgres:16.15-alpine`, `chrislusf/seaweedfs:4.48` and `valkey/valkey:8.1-alpine` into `images/`, packages the `mscd` chart, copies the Compose files, `config/` (without `demo-secrets/`), packs (`/app/packs` exported from the built image), install scripts, the anchor public key and `INSTALL-ONPREM.md`, runs `deploy/scripts/check-release-artefacts.sh` on the result (R-13), writes `SHA256SUMS` (signed when `--sign-key` is given), and an SBOM per image when `syft` is installed (skipped with a warning otherwise).
- `check-release-artefacts.sh <bundle dir or image>`: fails when any file (including inside image layers, read with `docker save` and `tar`) contains a key id from `dev-key-ids.json`, a file whose SHA-256 equals that of any file in the R-11 development secret folders (hashed when the check runs), a `demo.yaml` or `test.yaml` profile, or the seed snapshot.
- In this sandbox and in CI the release inputs are rehearsal keys generated into a temporary directory outside the repository with `mscd-pack keygen` (licence, pack and anchor), so the release path is proven without any development key.
- `docs/INSTALL-ONPREM.md` is rewritten: requirements (8 vCPU, 32 GB, 500 GB for small; Docker 24 or later with Compose v2; or Kubernetes 1.29 or later with Helm 3), bundle verification, Compose install, first admin, licence upload (bound to the installation id shown in the licence screen), identity provider setup (Entra ID OIDC example with `subject_mode: entra`), local sign-in policy, repository connection (SharePoint with `Sites.Selected` and certificate, Google Shared Drive with a service account, per-system root folders), Kubernetes install with Helm values (per-component secrets, trusted proxy ranges), audit chain anchors and what they prove (F.11), upgrades and backups (PostgreSQL dump and object storage copy before every upgrade), troubleshooting with `/readyz` and `mscd-server` diagnostics.

### I.6 Upgrades and migrations

- Migrations are forward-only and compatible with the previous release (expand and contract, ARCHITECTURE 9.3). A column rename takes two releases.
- Compose: `upgrade.sh` stops nothing before `migrate` succeeds; on failure it leaves the old containers running and prints the migrate log.
- Helm: the pre-upgrade hook runs `migrate`; pods then roll. Readiness waits until the schema version matches the image.
- Rollback: images can roll back one release while the schema stays (expand and contract); database restore from backup is the only way to undo a migration.

## J. Testing

### J.1 Layers

| Layer | Tool | Location | Command | Runs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Unit | Vitest | `apps/server/test/unit`, `packages/*/test`, `tools/*/test`, `apps/web/test` | `npm test` | every WP |
| Integration (real PostgreSQL) | Vitest + Testcontainers | `apps/server/test/int/<area>` | `npm run test:int -w @mscd/server -- <path>` | WPs touching the server |
| Contract | Vitest | `apps/server/test/int/contract` | `npm run test:int -w @mscd/server -- test/int/contract` | WP-30 |
| Parity | Vitest | `apps/server/test/int/parity` | same runner | WP-17 and later |
| RLS and tenant isolation | Vitest | `apps/server/test/int/db/rls.test.ts`, `test/int/security/isolation.test.ts` | same runner | WP-06, WP-30 |
| RBAC | Vitest, generated from the matrix and the transition tables | `apps/server/test/int/rbac` | same runner | WP-30 |
| Adapters | Vitest + mock providers + SeaweedFS container | `apps/server/test/int/adapters` | same runner | WP-13, WP-15 |
| Worker | Vitest, both drivers (Valkey container for BullMQ) | `apps/server/test/int/jobs*` | same runner | WP-22, WP-23 |
| OIDC and SAML | Vitest + Keycloak container | `apps/server/test/int/sso` | `MSCD_TEST_SSO=1 npm run test:int -w @mscd/server -- test/int/sso` | WP-16, WP-19 |
| Web E2E, sample | Playwright | `apps/web/e2e` | `CI=1 npm run test:e2e` | web WPs |
| Web E2E, API | Playwright against a seeded server, serial, reset per test | `apps/web/e2e` (project `api`) | `npm run test:e2e:api` | WP-27 and later |
| Full-stack smoke | Playwright + Compose | `tools/smoke` | `npm run test:smoke` | WP-31, WP-33 |
| Helm static | `alpine/k8s:1.34.1` (helm, kubeconform) + node assertions | `deploy/helm/mscd/ci/validate.sh` | `bash deploy/helm/mscd/ci/validate.sh` | WP-29, CI |
| Helm install | k3s in Docker | `deploy/helm/mscd/ci/k3s-test.sh` | nightly CI only (cannot run on this host, A.3) | CI `helm` job |
| Release artefacts | shell | `deploy/scripts/check-release-artefacts.sh` | run by `build-bundle.sh` and CI | WP-33 |
| Text rules | node script | `tools/scripts/check-text.mjs` | `npm run check:text` | every WP that writes docs |

`deploy/helm/mscd/ci/assert-render.mjs` (WP-29) parses the `helm template` output with `yaml` and asserts: the migrate Job carries `helm.sh/hook: pre-install,pre-upgrade` and weight `-5`; every container has `runAsNonRoot: true`, `readOnlyRootFilesystem: true`, `allowPrivilegeEscalation: false`, capabilities `drop: [ALL]` and `seccompProfile.type: RuntimeDefault`; a default-deny NetworkPolicy exists and the api accepts ingress only from web pods; no `Secret` object is rendered unless `secrets.create: true`; the api and worker pods never mount the gateway Secret and the gateway pod never mounts the api Secret; `automountServiceAccountToken` is false.

### J.2 Database test setup

- `apps/server/test/setup/global-db.ts` (Vitest `globalSetup`): first runs `node tools/scripts/ensure-built.mjs` (G.1; the call is added by WP-14 together with the demo template). When `MSCD_TEST_DATABASE_URL` is set it uses that server; otherwise it starts `postgres:16.15-alpine` (or `MSCD_TEST_PG_IMAGE`) through `@testcontainers/postgresql` with label `mscd.test=true` and `TESTCONTAINERS_RYUK_DISABLED=true`. It runs the bootstrap SQL, migrates a template database `mscd_tpl_empty` (platform and ref, no tenants), and, after WP-14, a template `mscd_tpl_demo` (seeded demo). Teardown stops the container.
- Each test file gets its own database with `CREATE DATABASE t_<random> TEMPLATE mscd_tpl_<kind>` (about 100 ms) through the helper `useTestDatabase('empty' | 'demo')`, which returns pools for the migrator, app and pack installer roles and a Nest testing module factory.
- `vitest.int.config.ts` (WP-03): `pool: 'forks'`, `maxWorkers: 2`, `testTimeout: 60000`, `hookTimeout: 120000`, SWC plugin for decorators, alias `@mscd/shared` to source, `globalSetup: ['test/setup/global-db.ts', ...(process.env.MSCD_TEST_SSO === '1' ? ['test/setup/keycloak.ts'] : [])]`. WP-03 creates `test/setup/keycloak.ts` as a stub that exports empty `setup` and `teardown`; WP-16 fills it.
- `test/setup/auth-fakes.ts` (WP-08): overrides `SESSION_RESOLVER` and `PERMISSION_EVALUATOR` in a Nest testing module with a fixed user, organisation and role set, and evaluates permissions from the shared F.6 matrix (scopes and tenancy-specific rows included, own checks left to the services), for WPs whose routes are tested before WP-11 and WP-39 finish; later suites use real sessions.

### J.3 Contract tests

- Every route in the shared route table is mounted (compare `RouteDef.id` list with the routes Nest registered, test profile with test endpoints on); every mounted `/api/v1` route has a `RouteDef`.
- A helper `callRoute(app, def, input, as)` builds requests with a session for a given user, validates the response body against `def.response` (or `Problem` for errors) and fails on extra status codes not in `def.errors`.
- File routes (`def.file`): the response carries the F.13 headers.
- The OpenAPI document generates without error and contains every route except `testOnly` ones; a snapshot of route ids and methods is stored in `test/int/contract/__snapshots__`.

### J.4 Parity tests

- Snapshot parity: for `ndt`, `bsd` and `rmn` as `rina` (role `manager`), `GET /ui/org` and `GET /ui/ms/{ms}` must equal `sample-snapshot.json` after normalisation: `undefined`, `null` and a missing key are equal; `false` equals a missing boolean; `[]` equals a missing array; sample-only fields are removed (`reqs[].prod`, `reqs[].integ`, `reqs[].loc`, `reqs[].due`, `ChainItem.ref`, `Activity.doc`); API-only fields are removed (`refs`, `permissions`, `me`, `evs[].hash`, `evs[].lastGoodCheck`, `evs[].lastCheck`, `docs[].approver`, `docs[].path`, `docs[].hashAlg`, `docs[].usedBy`, `MS[].gateName`, `MS[].gtSuggestions`, `MS[].banner.kind`, `MS[].audit.prepWindowDays`, `PACKS[].status`, `REPOS[].ref`, `REPOS[].adapter`, `REPOS[].ownershipType`, `REPOS[].actions`); `GateItem.type` is compared as a `Pair` on both sides (the sample bundle produces pairs since WP-07); `TITLES` is compared as "every sample entry is present and equal"; `LOC` compares only `[0]`, `[1]`, `[2]` for slugs present in the API.
- Reference numbers (UI 4.7) are asserted explicitly: requirements, evidence, documents, chain items, not-pending items, gate items and critical evidence per system; My Tasks counts `rina` 3, `bagus` 31, `maya` 1, `hendra` 3 through `GET /tasks`, before and after running every job once.
- Command parity: each command of UI section 3 is applied to `SampleDataSource` (imported from `apps/web/src/data`) and to the API on a fresh `mscd_tpl_demo` copy; the touched record, its chain item and the nav counts must match. Known differences are listed in the test with the decision that causes them: document upload (A.4 5.7), auditor verification (5.5), evidence regenerated by the sample after a status change (UI 2.15), verification rejected by BR-01 (E.3), My Tasks `soa` items (H.5 step 13).

### J.5 Isolation, RBAC and security

- RLS (`test/int/db/rls.test.ts`, WP-06): two tenants with identical codes; with `app.org_id` of tenant A, every tenant table of schema B returns zero rows even with an explicit schema-qualified query, and a schema-qualified insert into B fails even when it carries A's id; no `app.org_id` returns zero rows; `mscd_app` cannot `DELETE` or `TRUNCATE` any tenant table, cannot `UPDATE` `audit_trail`, cannot write `ref`, `platform.platform_role`, `platform.pack_install`, `platform.installation` or the `document` of `platform.licence`; `mscd_migrator` without `app.org_id` also sees zero rows (FORCE); org-scoped platform tables show only the session's organisation without platform scope; `assert_member` rejects a non-member in every person column; `secure_tenant_schema` raises for a table without `organization_id`; nobody but the migrator can create objects in `public`, `ext`, `platform`, `ref` or a tenant schema, and the readiness check fails after a test grants `CREATE` on `public` to `PUBLIC`.
- Pool guard (WP-08): a connection returned with a session-level `app.org_id` is destroyed and logged.
- API isolation (WP-30): a user of `tst` gets 404 for every `/ui/ms/{ms}` and command of `ndt`; slugs that exist in both organisations resolve to the session's organisation only; `/admin/users`, `/admin/identity-providers`, `GET /license`, `/admin/audit-trail` and `/admin/storage/*` of organisation A never show rows of organisation B, also for a Tenant Admin of both who is signed in to A; idempotency keys do not replay across organisations.
- Cross-system records (WP-30): for every command with `{id}`, a record of another system of the same organisation gives 404 (RecordResolver); `{entityType}` outside the allow-list gives 404; `POST /reminders` with a record of another system gives 404.
- RBAC (WP-30): for every `RouteDef` with a permission and every role of F.6, a generated test calls the route as a user holding only that role and expects 403 or not-403 per the matrix ("own" grants are tested once with an own record and once with someone else's); every transition row of C.13 is tested with the allowed and one disallowed role; external auditor write routes are 403; reads after `valid_until` or after grant revocation are 403; every external auditor request adds an `auditor.read` entry with the route and record refs; Top Management gets 403 on storage object routes; a 403 writes `authz.denied`.
- Owner scope and projections (WP-30): for every `GET` route, a Domain Owner of ISMS only sees own records and nothing of QMS or ABMS (including `/ui/org` `TITLES`, `CHAINS`, `PEOPLE`, files, packs, chains and exports); an external auditor sees the projection of G.5 and can open only pack copies; `restricted` documents are hidden from a Domain Owner who does not own them.
- Identity (WP-11, WP-16, WP-19, WP-26, WP-39, then WP-30 for the cross-cutting cases): an OIDC token whose verified email matches an existing account does not sign in (`no_account`); an unverified or absent `email_verified` never links or provisions; JIT needs verified domains; an Entra provider rejects a token with another `tid`; a user of two organisations signed in through organisation A's provider gets 409 `reauth-required` on `PUT /me/org` to organisation B, and the same for local sign-in into an organisation with `local_login = 'disabled'`; invitation linking works once and only through the inviting organisation's providers; a provider of organisation A cannot be used to reach organisation B; SAML ACS without the binding cookie fails and a replayed assertion fails; `amr: ['otp']` alone is not MFA and leads to the local TOTP step.
- MFA and passwords (WP-11): ten wrong codes spread over several `mfa_pending` sessions lock the second factor for the user (429 on the next attempt with a correct code), the lock doubles, the user gets an email and `auth.mfa_failed` entries exist; re-enrolment without proof is 401 and with proof revokes other sessions; recovery code use emails the user; password reset revokes every session; local login and reset are refused (no email) for a user whose organisations require SSO.
- Tenant Admin limits (WP-39): disabling or resetting MFA of a user with another active membership is 403; `POST /admin/users` answers the same for new and existing emails; a self-grant of `ms_manager` stays `pending_approval` and needs a second Tenant Admin.
- HTTP (WP-30): CSRF missing or wrong token gives 403; login lockout after 10 failures; MFA replay rejected; session fixation (cookie value changes after login and MFA); `Cache-Control: no-store` and the F.12 CSP on API responses; licence read-only mode blocks writes; a spoofed `X-Forwarded-For` from a non-trusted address does not change `request.ip` (rate-limit key and audit `source_ip`); an invalid `X-Request-Id` is replaced; uploading `text/html` and `image/svg+xml` evidence to the vault and fetching it returns `application/octet-stream`, `Content-Disposition: attachment` and `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox`; log lines of guest, reset, calendar and OIDC callback requests contain no token, code or state value.
- Licence and packs (WP-12): a release-flavour build (build info generated in the test with a temporary rehearsal key directory) rejects a licence and a pack signed with a development key even when the profile or `MSCD__LICENSE__TRUSTED_KEYS` names that key (the profile schema rejects the key); a licence bound to another installation or organisation is rejected; a clock 25 hours behind the high-water mark gives `grace` and `licence.clock_rollback`; `assertFeature` blocks auditor grants, joint links and link health runs when the feature is absent; JIT, invitations and `admin create-user` all hit `LIC-USERS` at the limit; a pack with `../` entries, duplicate names, an extra file, a non-regular entry or more than 200 MB uncompressed is rejected with no row written; an older `pack_version` is rejected; with `tenancy.mode = multi` a Tenant Admin gets 403 on pack import and licence upload.

### J.6 Web E2E in two projects

- `playwright.config.js` keeps the sample project (port 4173, `fullyParallel`). `playwright.api.config.js` (WP-27) uses `workers: 1`, `fullyParallel: false` and `webServer: { command: 'node e2e-api/stack.mjs', url: 'http://localhost:4174/api/v1/system/profile', reuseExistingServer: false }`. `stack.mjs` runs `tools/scripts/ensure-built.mjs`, starts PostgreSQL, SeaweedFS and Mailpit containers (random ports, label `mscd.test=true`; the api's SMTP points at Mailpit and the specs read mail through its API), runs bootstrap, migrate and `seed demo` into database `mscd_e2e_tpl`, creates `mscd_e2e` from it, starts `mock-providers` (4310), `storage-gateway` (TLS off, 4311) and `api` (3174) from `apps/server/dist` with `test.yaml` plus `test.endpoints_enabled: true`, `test.template_database: mscd_e2e_tpl` and the admin URL file, builds the web in API mode and serves it with `vite preview --port 4174` proxying `/api`. No worker runs (jobs stay queued; the E2E suite asserts command results, not job effects). It stops everything on exit.
- State isolation: a test fixture (`e2e-api/fixtures.js`, imported by the API project through `use` options) calls `POST /api/v1/test/reset` before every test and then `POST /api/v1/test/session {handle: 'rina'}` in the test's browser context, so every test starts from the seed state with its own fresh session and `--repeat-each=2` sees the same data (for example "Reminders sent to 8 holders" every time, BR-50 throttling reset). Persona switches in tests go through the demo persona endpoint (the UI persona menu does this in demo mode).
- `@api-only` specs (`apps/web/e2e/api-only/*.spec.js`, skipped in the sample project with `grepInvert: /@api-only/`) cover sign-in with password and TOTP, MFA enrolment (`mfa.tester`), logout, session expiry, guest access (a grant created as `rina`, then the access link and the one-time code read from Mailpit, then TOTP enrolment), the 422 toast for BR-01, the hidden Verify for auditors, the document `in_review` toast, an upload to the Managed Vault, and picker linking from the mock SharePoint. Each sign-in spec uses a different user (`maya`, `hendra`, `mfa.tester`, `sari`) so no user signs in twice within one TOTP step, and the reset before each test clears `last_used_step`.
- Tests that assert behaviour the API deliberately changes carry the tag `@sample-only` with a comment naming the decision and are skipped in the API project (`grepInvert: /@sample-only/`): auditor verification (5.5), auditor sees pending per person (5.6), document upload marks approved (5.7), Domain Owner nav counts over unscoped data (D-04), counts that change because BR-01 rejects a verification, My Tasks "Mark done" on `soa` items (H.5 step 13).
- `display.spec.js` compares font request URLs with the configured `baseURL` (`testInfo.project.use.baseURL`) instead of the literal `http://localhost:4173/`.

### J.7 Adapter tests with mock providers

- `tools/mock-providers` (WP-10) implements: Microsoft identity token endpoint (`POST /{tenant}/oauth2/v2.0/token`, validates the client assertion signature against registered certificates and `aud`), Graph drive items (with `createdBy.user.email`), children, versions, path lookup, folder creation, simple and session uploads (computes quickXorHash), async copy with a monitor URL; Google OAuth token (`jwt-bearer`, `refresh_token` and `authorization_code` grants), Drive files get, list, create (multipart and resumable), copy, revisions get and update. State is in memory; `POST /_admin/reset`, `POST /_admin/fixtures`, and fault switches: `POST /_admin/items/{id}/move`, `/new-version`, `/delete`, `/purge-version`, `/corrupt-hash`, `/_admin/drives/{id}/forbid`, `/_admin/tokens/revoke`, `/_admin/throttle` (next N requests answer 429 with `Retry-After`), `/_admin/latency`. Requests with ids that fail the D.5 patterns are answered 400, so an adapter that skips validation fails its tests.
- The mock follows the documented JSON shapes (field names, `@odata.nextLink`, `nextPageToken`, error envelopes `{error: {code, message}}`), checked by unit tests against recorded example payloads stored in `tools/mock-providers/test/fixtures/` that were copied from the public API documentation.
- Adapter tests (WP-13, WP-15) run every gateway operation of D.5 against the mock and assert the metadata mapping, hash handling, link status precedence (E.4) for every fault switch, rate-limit backoff, root and scope-root enforcement, credential binding (a credential sealed for one config is refused with another drive or root), id validation, zero-transit creator and correlation checks, and that no request leaves for a host outside the configured base URLs.

### J.8 Worker tests

- Each job of E.2 has an integration test that seeds the needed rows, enqueues the job through the `JobQueue`, runs the worker in-process until idle, and asserts rows, audit entries, tasks and notifications. Idempotency: running the same job twice produces the same state and no duplicate rows. Scheduled jobs are tested for two organisations in different time zones with a mocked clock.
- The driver suite runs the transactional-send test on both drivers: a job sent inside a rolled-back transaction never runs; a committed one runs once.
- Notifications are asserted through the Mailpit API (`GET /api/v1/messages`) in a Mailpit container started only by the notification tests.

### J.9 Full-stack smoke

`tools/smoke` (WP-31): `npm run test:smoke` builds the `mscd-server-demo`, `mscd-web` and `mscd-mock-providers` images with the build CA (`--build-ca`, default `$MSCD_BUILD_CA`, R-12) or uses existing ones with `SMOKE_SKIP_BUILD=1`, starts `docker compose -p mscd-smoke -f deploy/compose/docker-compose.yml -f deploy/compose/docker-compose.demo.yml up -d --wait` with ports 18080, 18443 and 18088, then Playwright (Chromium 1194, launch argument `--host-resolver-rules=MAP keycloak 127.0.0.1`) runs: login as `rina` with password and TOTP (`tools/scripts/demo-totp.mjs` logic), portfolio shows four systems, open ISMS controls and verify the reference counts, mark a control complete and see the toast, link a SharePoint file from the mock picker, upload a file to the Managed Vault (QMS) and see SHA-256, run the integrity check, send a reminder and find the email in Mailpit, sign in through Keycloak OIDC as `bagus` (Keycloak login form, then the local TOTP step, F.5), and log out. It finishes with `docker compose down -v`.

### J.10 CI workflow

`.github/workflows/ci.yml` (WP-33): jobs `lint-typecheck-unit`, `integration` (PostgreSQL 15, 16 and 17 matrix through `MSCD_TEST_PG_IMAGE`), `e2e-sample`, `e2e-api`, `images` (build `mscd-web`, the `release` target of `mscd-server` with rehearsal keys and the `demo` target, Trivy scan when available, `check-release-artefacts.sh` on the release image), `smoke` (needs images), `helm-static` (`ci/validate.sh`, every commit), `helm` (k3s install test, nightly), `text-rules`. Node 22, npm cache, Playwright 1.56.1 browsers cached. `MSCD_BUILD_CA` is an optional repository secret written to a file (empty file when unset).

## K. Work packages

### K.1 Waves

A wave starts when every WP of the previous wave is done. Inside a wave at most two WPs run at once; the rest queue in the listed order. WPs in one wave own disjoint paths. Before wave 1 the orchestrator confirms that no other agent is editing `app/`, `deploy/` or `docs/INSTALL-ONPREM.md`, and exports `MSCD_BUILD_CA=/root/.ccr/ca-bundle.crt` for every agent (R-12).

| Wave | Work packages | Lockfile owner | Owners of shared files in this wave |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | WP-01 Monorepo workspaces and web move; WP-02 Developer infrastructure stack | WP-01 | root `package.json`, lockfile, `.gitignore`, `.dockerignore`: WP-01; dev Compose file: WP-02 |
| 2 | WP-03 Server, shared and tool skeleton with the dependency baseline; WP-04 Framework pack sources from the sample | WP-03 | root `package.json`, lockfile, `tsconfig.base.json`, `eslint.config.mjs`, `apps/server/src/app/*.module.ts`, `apps/server/vitest.*.config.ts`: WP-03 |
| 3 | WP-05 Shared API contract; WP-06 Database migrations and schema tests; WP-07 Web DataSource seam (sample mode) | none | `packages/shared/src/index.ts`, `packages/shared/src/api/routes.ts`: WP-05 |
| 4 | WP-08 Platform core services; WP-09 Pack and licence tooling; WP-10 Mock provider servers | none | none |
| 5 | WP-11 Authentication, sessions and MFA; WP-12 Framework engine and licence enforcement; WP-13 Storage gateway and Managed Vault | none | none |
| 6 | WP-39 Authorisation, scoping and membership; WP-14 Demo seed; WP-15 Microsoft 365 and Google Drive adapters; WP-16 OIDC sign-in and identity providers; WP-38 Web auth pages and boot router | none | `apps/server/test/setup/global-db.ts`: WP-14 |
| 7 | WP-17 Read model and parity endpoints; WP-18 Container images and NGINX API proxy; WP-19 SAML sign-in | none | `.dockerignore`: WP-18 |
| 8 | WP-20 Implementation commands and rule engine; WP-21 File commands; WP-22 Job queue, worker and core jobs | none | none |
| 9 | WP-35 Register, document and audit event commands; WP-36 Risk and context registers; WP-23 Link health, integrity and drift jobs; WP-24 Notifications and exports; WP-28 Compose full stack and installer | none | on-premise Compose files: WP-28 |
| 10 | WP-37 Audit registers and cycle derivation; WP-25 Web API data source and controller; WP-26 Evidence packs and auditor access; WP-32 Per-screen read endpoints | none | `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`: WP-25 |
| 11 | WP-27 Web E2E against the API; WP-29 Helm chart; WP-31 Full-stack smoke test; WP-34 Observability and operations | none | none |
| 12 | WP-30 Security, RBAC and contract test suite | none | may edit any `apps/server/src/**` and `packages/shared/src/**` file to fix a defect it proves (alone in the wave) |
| 13 | WP-33 Offline bundle, install guide and CI | none | none |

Common acceptance for every WP (not repeated below): `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` and `npm test` pass once WP-03 is done; `npm run check:text` passes for any document written; the handoff note exists (R-09). Every `docker buildx build` passes `--secret id=build_ca,src="$MSCD_BUILD_CA"` (R-12).

test ! -e app && test -f apps/web/src/MscdApp.jsx

CI=1 npm run test:e2e -w @mscd/web

cp apps/web/src/hover.css /tmp/wp01-hover.css && echo '/* drift */' >> apps/web/src/hover.css && ! npm run gen:views:check -w @mscd/web; cp /tmp/wp01-hover.css apps/web/src/hover.css
docker buildx build --load --secret id=build_ca,src="$MSCD_BUILD_CA" -f deploy/docker/web.Dockerfile --build-context nginx-config=deploy/nginx -t mscd-web:wp01 .
docker run -d --rm --name wp01-web -p 18081:8080 mscd-web:wp01 && sleep 3 && curl -fsS http://localhost:18081/healthz; docker rm -f wp01-web
bash -n deploy/scripts/build-bundle.sh
! grep -nE '(^|[^A-Za-z])app/' deploy/scripts/build-bundle.sh deploy/README.md

grep -q '00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000c1' deploy/compose/dev/keycloak/realm-mscd.json
test "$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://localhost:58333/)" = 403

- Every `test` script passes `--passWithNoTests`. The web `test` script is `vitest run --dir test --passWithNoTests`, so Vitest never collects the Playwright files in `e2e/` (WP-07 adds `vitest.config.js` with the same include). `apps/web/tsconfig.json` includes `src/**/*.ts` and `src/**/*.tsx` with a placeholder `src/data/types.ts` (`export {};`).
- Workspace scripts: server `gen:build-info` (B.4), `prebuild`, `pretypecheck`, `pretest` and `pretest:int` run it; `build` (`tsc -b tsconfig.build.json`), `typecheck`, `test` (`vitest run -c vitest.unit.config.ts`), `test:int` (`vitest run -c vitest.int.config.ts`), `start`, `migrate`, `seed:demo`; shared `build`, `typecheck`, `test`, `openapi`; pack-tools and mock-providers `build`, `typecheck`, `test`; web adds `typecheck` (`tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json`), `test`, `test:e2e:api`, `export:sample`.
- `apps/server`: the B.4 tree with every module folder of B.4 (including `implementation`, `registers`, `risk-registers`, `audit-registers`, `observability`, `test-support`) holding `<name>.module.ts` (empty `@Module({})`) and a three-line `README.md`; `src/app/api.module.ts`, `worker.module.ts`, `gateway.module.ts` import every module of their run mode (D.4), so later WPs never edit them; `src/main.ts` with the commander commands of D-01, each `src/cli/*.ts` exporting `register(program)` with stubs that print "not implemented" and exit 2 until their WP.
- `scripts/gen-build-info.mjs` per B.4 and D-16 (release flavour checks included), writing `src/generated/build-info.ts`.
- `src/config`: zod schema for the full I.1 profile (strict; release-flavour restrictions; `kms.provider` other than `file` rejected with the D-09 message), YAML loader, `MSCD__A__B` overrides, `_FILE` secrets, validation errors listed on exit; `profiles/{saas,onprem,demo,test}.yaml`.
- `src/modules/platform`: Fastify adapter bootstrap helper that registers `@fastify/cookie`, `@fastify/helmet` (F.12 settings), `@fastify/multipart` (limits from the profile) and a raw `application/octet-stream` content-type parser that hands the request stream to the handler without buffering (used by upload content routes); `trustProxy` from `http.trust_proxy` (F.12); pino logging with the F.12 redaction (including the `req.url` query rewriting); request id validation (D.1); `GET /healthz`, `GET /readyz` (database `SELECT 1` and a pluggable list of readiness checks), metrics listener on 9464 with default Node metrics, `GET /api/v1/system/profile` from configuration (local type until WP-05).
- `src/db`: Kysely factory (`pg` pool, date type parser for OID 1082 returning strings), `SqlFileMigrationProvider`, migrator runner for `platform`, `ref` and `tenant` folders (empty folders now), `migrations/bootstrap/roles.sql` and `report-role.sql` (C.2) and the `db bootstrap [--with-report-role]` command.
- `test/setup/global-db.ts` (J.2) with the `empty` template made of bootstrap only; `test/setup/keycloak.ts` stub; `vitest.int.config.ts` with the J.2 `globalSetup` list; `test/int/smoke/db.test.ts`.
- `tools/scripts/check-text.mjs`: fails when any file under the given folders contains U+2014 or U+00B7, printing file and line. `tools/scripts/demo-totp.mjs <handle>`: prints the current TOTP code of a demo user (G.3 secret rule, RFC 6238 in about 30 lines of `node:crypto`).
- `eslint.config.mjs`: `@eslint/js` recommended and `typescript-eslint` recommended for `**/*.ts` and `**/*.tsx`, plus the `.mjs` scripts under `tools/` and `packs/scripts/`; the F.10 restrictions for `apps/server/src/**` (direct query builder use outside `src/db`, `src/modules/tenancy`, `src/seed`; platform scope outside the listed folders; `SET ` without `LOCAL` in string literals). The existing web JavaScript (`apps/web/src/**/*.js`, `*.jsx`, `apps/web/e2e/**`) is not linted in this build. Ignores `dist`, `node_modules`, `packs/sources`, `packs/dist`, `apps/server/src/generated`.

Acceptance:

```bash
npm ci
npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npm test
npm run build -w @mscd/server
node apps/server/dist/main.js --help | grep -E 'api|worker|storage-gateway|migrate|seed'
MSCD_PROFILE_FILE=apps/server/profiles/test.yaml node apps/server/dist/main.js api & sleep 4; curl -fsS http://localhost:3000/healthz; curl -fsS http://localhost:3000/api/v1/system/profile; kill %1
! MSCD_BUILD_FLAVOR=release MSCD_TRUSTED_KEYS_DIR=/nonexistent node apps/server/scripts/gen-build-info.mjs
MSCD__KMS__PROVIDER=cloud_kms MSCD_PROFILE_FILE=apps/server/profiles/test.yaml node apps/server/dist/main.js api 2>&1 | grep -q 'not available in this build'
node tools/scripts/demo-totp.mjs rina | grep -Eq '^[0-9]{6}$'
npm run test:int -w @mscd/server -- test/int/smoke
npm run check:text
CI=1 npm run test:e2e -w @mscd/web
```

#### WP-04 Framework pack sources from the sample

| | |
|---|---|
| Wave, depends on | 2; WP-01 |
| Lockfile owner | no (plain Node scripts, no packages) |
| Owns | `packs/sources/**`, `packs/scripts/**`, `packs/README.md` |

Brief:
- `packs/scripts/extract-from-sample.mjs` imports `apps/web/src/data/mscd-data.js` and writes the six pack source folders of G.7 in the E.8 file layout (CSV with a header row, UTF-8, LF, RFC 4180 quoting; JSON pretty-printed). `manifest.json` has every field except `datasets` (filled by the builder).
- ISMS requirements: the Harmonized Structure entries of the sample QMS list (clauses 4 to 7, 9, 10) plus ISO 27001 clause 8 entries `8.1 Operational planning and control`, `8.2 Information security risk assessment`, `8.3 Information security risk treatment`. Catalogue: `buildReqs(ISMS)` gives code, title, theme and frequency. Other packs: `buildReqs` of their system gives the requirement list.
- Chain, activities, frequencies, labels, cycle requirements (empty derivations), drift rules (G.7 kinds: `risk12` `stale_risk`, `dd` `dd_expired`, `mr` and `csat` `manual`), prep tasks, rules, `lookups.csv`, `audit_activities.csv` and `finding_conditions.csv` per G.7; empty datasets keep their header row. The six ISO 27001 audit activities and six finding conditions are short titles written for this build (never ISO text) and are listed in `packs/README.md`.
- `--check` mode regenerates in memory and exits 1 on any difference with the files on disk.
- `packs/scripts/validate-sources.mjs`: checks every file exists, headers match E.8, codes are unique, references resolve (catalogue to requirement codes where given, chain entity types in the allowed list of C.10, activity phase numbers contiguous, lookup domains `doc_type` and `treatment_option` present), and prints counts.
- `packs/README.md`: format summary and the rule that packs hold short titles only (PRD 3.3).

Acceptance:

```bash
node packs/scripts/extract-from-sample.mjs --check
node packs/scripts/validate-sources.mjs
test "$(tail -n +2 packs/sources/ISO27001-2022/catalogue.csv | wc -l)" -eq 93
test "$(tail -n +2 packs/sources/ISO27001-2022/activities.csv | wc -l)" -eq 69
test "$(tail -n +2 packs/sources/ISO27001-2022/audit_activities.csv | wc -l)" -eq 6
grep -q '"source_kind": "stale_risk"' packs/sources/ISO27001-2022/drift_rules.json
```

#### WP-05 Shared API contract

| | |
|---|---|
| Wave, depends on | 3; WP-03 |
| Owns | `packages/shared/src/**`, `packages/shared/scripts/**`, `packages/shared/test/**` |

Brief:
- `src/enums.ts`: every database enum of C.4 to C.6 and the UI codes of C.11, business, organisation and platform roles, UI roles, permission codes of F.6, problem slugs and rule ids of D.1.
- `src/authz/matrix.ts`: F.6 as data (`{permission: {scope, roles: Role[], own: Role[], orgRoles: OrgRole[], anyMsRoles: Role[], platformRoles: PlatformRole[], tenancy?: 'single' | 'multi'}}`) plus `uiRoleFor(roles)`.
- `src/machines.ts`: the C.13 transition tables as data.
- `src/ui/*.ts`: zod schemas for every UI 4.2 interface (`ManagementSystem` with `audit.prepWindowDays`, `Requirement`, `Document` with `usedBy`, `Evidence` with `lastCheck`, `ChainItem` wire form, `GateItem` with `type: Pair`, `Activity` wire form, `Phase`, `Stage`, `XRecord`, `MsBundle`, `UiOrgSnapshot` with nullable `LICENCE` and the G.5 `REPOS` fields, `UiMsSnapshot`, the `refs` RecordKey kinds of G.5), with the exact keys and status codes.
- `src/api/common.ts` (`RouteDef` with `owner`, `testOnly` and `file` as D.1, `Problem`, `CommandResult`, `Page`, `RecordRef`, `Pair`, `IsoDate`) and one area file per area, each exporting its schemas (D.2) and `routes: RouteDef[]`: `system`, `auth` (login, MFA, password, invitations, OIDC, SAML, guest, identity link), `me` (me, org switch, memberships, preferences, demo persona), `admin-users` (users, identities, organisation policy, role grants and approvals), `admin-idp`, `admin-ms` (management systems and transitions), `ui`, `readmodel`, `implementation` (applicability, activities, phases, operating criteria), `records` (`RECORD_ENTITY_TYPES`, assignment, complete-action, soft delete, task completion), `documents`, `reminders`, `audit-events` (events, prep tasks, cycle items), `risk-registers`, `audit-registers`, `files`, `storage`, `gateway`, `packs`, `license`, `link-health`, `exports` (exports and calendar feed), `notifications`, `evidence-packs`, `auditor-access`, `ops`, `audit-trail`, `test-support`. Every endpoint of D.3, with its `owner`, and every gateway endpoint of D.5 is present. `src/api/routes.ts` concatenates all area arrays; later WPs edit only the area files they own.
- `src/pack/*.ts`: manifest and dataset row schemas (E.8, including `lookups.csv` and `audit_activities.csv`), licence document schema (D-15 binding fields, `trial`) and `canonicalJson()`.
- `scripts/openapi.mjs`: builds OpenAPI 3.1 from the route table with `z.toJSONSchema` (skipping `testOnly` routes), writes `dist/openapi.json`.
- Tests: every schema parses a hand-written valid example and rejects an invalid one; route ids are unique; every route path starts with `/api/v1` (gateway routes with `/v1`); every permission in routes and machines exists in the matrix; every `owner` names an existing WP; every `{entityType}` route uses `RECORD_ENTITY_TYPES`.

Acceptance:

```bash
npm run typecheck -w @mscd/shared && npm test -w @mscd/shared
npm run openapi -w @mscd/shared && node -e "const d=require('./packages/shared/dist/openapi.json'); if(Object.keys(d.paths).length<130) process.exit(1)"
```

|---|---|

The SQL was run on PostgreSQL 15 and 16 when the plan was written; report any error instead of changing semantics.

MSCD_TEST_PG_IMAGE=postgres:17-alpine npm run test:int -w @mscd/server -- test/int/db/migrate.test.ts

#### WP-08 Platform core services

| | |
|---|---|
| Wave, depends on | 4; WP-05, WP-06 |
| Owns | `apps/server/src/common/**`, `apps/server/src/modules/{platform,tenancy,audit,keys}/**`, `apps/server/src/modules/authz/**` and `apps/server/src/modules/license/**` (default providers only; WP-39 and WP-12 take these folders over), `apps/server/src/modules/identity/identity.module.ts` and `apps/server/src/modules/identity/defaults.ts` (WP-11 takes them over), `apps/server/src/db/kysely.ts`, `apps/server/src/db/pool-guard.ts`, `apps/server/test/unit/common/**`, `apps/server/test/int/platform/**`, `apps/server/test/setup/auth-fakes.ts`, `packages/shared/src/api/{system,audit-trail}.ts` |

Brief:
- Problem details filter (D.1 slugs; maps zod errors, `BusinessRuleError(rule, messages, ref)`, `TransitionNotAllowedError`, `ConfirmationRequiredError(affected)`, `ReauthRequiredError(methods)`, `PreconditionFailedError`, Postgres `23505` to 409, `23514` to 422, `23503` from `assert_member` to 422 `NOT-A-MEMBER`).
- Request context with `AsyncLocalStorage` (request id per D.1 validation, user, organisation, system); pino child loggers.
- `@ApiRoute(def)` decorator: mounts method and path from the `RouteDef`, adds the zod validation pipe for params, query and body, sets permission metadata, skips `testOnly` routes unless the profile enables them; response validation in development and test profiles.
- `src/common/tokens.ts` with the injection tokens and interfaces of B.4, and the default providers registered in the owning modules (deny-all `PERMISSION_EVALUATOR`, pass-through `SCOPE_POLICY` that denies own-scoped and external auditor access, `RECORD_RESOLVER` and `MEMBERSHIP_SERVICE` implementing the D.1 and F.7 checks without roles, minimal `ME_ASSEMBLER`, `SESSION_RESOLVER` that authenticates nobody, permissive `LICENCE_GATE` with `assertWritable`, `assertCanAddUser`, `assertCanAddMs`, `assertFeature`, `isAdapterLicensed`); `test/setup/auth-fakes.ts` (J.2).
- Idempotency interceptor (`platform.idempotency_key` keyed by organisation, user and key; request hash per D.1), ETag helpers, `If-Match` check helper.
- Rate limit plugin with the PostgreSQL store (F.3); route-specific limits read from `RouteDef` metadata.
- `Clock` (`today(orgId)` in the organisation time zone, `demo.fixed_today`), ISO date helpers (`addDays`, `diffDays`, `fmtDay`, `fmtDayTime`).
- `TenantResolver` (slug or id to organisation, schema and status, cached 60 seconds, `invalidate(orgId)`), `withTenant` and `withPlatform` with scope (F.10), the pool checkout guard (F.10), and the readiness checks of C.3 (migrations and schema privileges).
- `AuditWriter` for both chains with the F.11 redaction list, `AuditChainVerifier`, anchor verification helper, and the four `/admin/audit-trail` routes (D.3; full entries with `audit_trail.read` in a system, metadata with `audit_trail.metadata.read`; CSV export neutralised per F.15).
- `FileResponder` (F.13) and `SpreadsheetSafe` helpers (F.15) in `src/common`.
- `KeyProvider` file driver, `SecretBox` (F.11), `keys` module for `platform.key`.
- `JobQueue` interface, `JOB_QUEUE` token, in-memory driver, `@JobHandler(name, {concurrency, scope})` decorator and discovery helper (E.1).
- `NotificationWriter.create(trx, {...})` that checks membership, inserts a `notification` row and enqueues `notifications.dispatch`.
- Wire `GET /system/profile` to the shared route definition.
- Tests: problem mapping, request id validation, idempotency across two organisations, pool guard destroys a connection with session-level settings, readiness fails when `PUBLIC` gets `CREATE` on `public`, audit redaction, `FileResponder` headers for `text/html` and `image/svg+xml`, CSV formula neutralisation.

#### WP-09 Pack and licence tooling

| | |
|---|---|
| Wave, depends on | 4; WP-04, WP-05 |
| Owns | `packages/pack-tools/**` (including `dev-keys/` with `dev-key-ids.json`, and `dev-licences/`) |

Brief:
- Library: `buildPack(sourceDir, signingKey)` (validates every dataset with the shared schemas, writes checksums into the manifest, creates a deterministic tar: sorted entries, fixed mtime `2026-01-01T00:00:00Z`, uid and gid 0, gzip level 9, then the `signature` file), `readPack(buffer, limits)` (the E.8 step 1 archive rules: 200 MB decompressed, 500 entries, regular files only, no absolute or `..` paths, unique names, exact file set), `verifyPack(buffer, trustedKeys, {rejectKeyIds})`, `keyId(publicKey)`, `signLicence(doc, key)`, `verifyLicence(doc, trustedKeys, {rejectKeyIds})`, `canonicalJson`, `DEV_KEY_IDS` (from `dev-keys/dev-key-ids.json`).
- CLI `mscd-pack`: `keygen --out <dir> --name <name>` (Ed25519, writes `<name>.key` and `<name>.pub`), `build <sourceDir> --sign-key-file <pem> --out <dir>`, `build-all --sign-key-file <pem> --out packs/dist`, `verify <file> --trusted <pub>...`, `licence sign <json> --key-file <pem>`, `licence verify <json> --trusted <pub>`.
- `dev-keys/`: Ed25519 pairs `pack-signing`, `licence-signing` and `anchor-signing` (with `pack-dev.pub`, `licence-dev.pub` and `anchor-dev.pub` copies in the `<kind>-*.pub` naming that `gen-build-info.mjs` reads), `dev-key-ids.json` with their key ids, and a `README.md` stating they are for development and demo only.
- `dev-licences/LIC-DEMO-NDT.json`, `-BSD`, `-RMN`, `-TST`: signed licences per G.3, bound to the fixed organisation ids.
- Tests: tar safety cases (traversal, absolute path, symlink, duplicate name, extra file, size cap), signature and checksum failures, `rejectKeyIds` refuses a dev-signed licence and pack.

Acceptance:

```bash
npm test -w @mscd/pack-tools && npm run build -w @mscd/pack-tools
node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js build-all --sign-key-file packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/pack-signing.key --out packs/dist
test "$(ls packs/dist/*.pack | wc -l)" -eq 6
for f in packs/dist/*.pack; do node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js verify "$f" --trusted packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/pack-signing.pub || exit 1; done
before=$(sha256sum packs/dist/ISO27001-2022-1.2.0.pack); node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js build-all --sign-key-file packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/pack-signing.key --out packs/dist; test "$before" = "$(sha256sum packs/dist/ISO27001-2022-1.2.0.pack)"
for f in packages/pack-tools/dev-licences/*.json; do node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js licence verify "$f" --trusted packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/licence-signing.pub || exit 1; done
```
