#### WP-15 Microsoft 365 and Google Drive adapters

| | |
|---|---|
| Wave, depends on | 6; WP-10, WP-13 |
| Owns | `apps/server/src/gateway/adapters/{m365,google-drive}/**`, `tools/mock-providers/src/**` (extensions found missing), `apps/server/test/int/adapters/**` |

Brief:
- Every D.5 operation for both adapters with the API calls listed in D.5; Microsoft token through a certificate client assertion (RS256, header `x5t#S256`); Google token through a service account JWT or a refresh token; tokens cached until 5 minutes before expiry.
- Root and scope-root enforcement, id validation and `encodeURIComponent` on every path segment, `URLSearchParams` for queries (D.5); zero-transit verification checks folder, `createdDateTime` after `intent.notBefore`, the creator email and the correlation code; `test-connection` reports `ownership: personal` for OneDrive personal drives (`driveType: personal`) and My Drive, and the credential or certificate expiry when known.
- Pass-through `put`: simple upload up to 4 MiB, upload session or resumable upload above, SHA-256 and type detection while streaming, no temporary file.
- Error mapping: 401 and token failures to `unreachable` with `credential_invalid`, 403 to `forbidden`, 404 to `not_found`, 429 and 503 to `rate_limited` with `Retry-After`, network errors to `unreachable`; retries with exponential backoff for idempotent calls.
- Egress check: requests only to the configured base URLs, and only when the host is allowed (I.1).
- Tests: every operation against the mock, every fault switch mapped to the E.4 status, throttling backoff, an item outside the system's root refused, ids with `/`, `?` or `..` refused before any request, zero-transit items from another account or without the code refused, no request to any other host (intercept with an undici `MockAgent` in strict mode for one test).

Acceptance:

```bash
npm run build -w @mscd/mock-providers && npm run test:int -w @mscd/server -- test/int/adapters
```

| | |
|---|---|

Brief:
- I.2 images: `server.Dockerfile` with the `release` (default) and `demo` targets of D-24 (named build context `trusted-keys`, build secret `pack_signing_key` without default, `build_ca` on every npm step, release content rules of R-13), `mock-providers.Dockerfile`, web image built with `VITE_DATA_SOURCE=api` by default; I.2.1 NGINX changes (`X-Forwarded-For $remote_addr`, real IP from `MSCD_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS`, the redacting `log_format mscd`); F.12 CSP additions.
- `deploy/docker/README.md` with build commands and build arguments (`VERSION`, `REVISION`, targets, `trusted-keys` context, `pack_signing_key` and `build_ca` secrets).
- `deploy/docker/smoke.sh [--build-ca <file>]` that runs the acceptance below and cleans up: network, postgres, db bootstrap, migrate, seed demo, gateway (TLS off), api and web from the demo image; curl `http://localhost:18082/api/v1/system/profile` through the web container returns 200; a request with `X-Forwarded-For: 203.0.113.9` and `?token=secret` leaves no `203.0.113.9` as client and no `secret` in the NGINX access log.

Acceptance:

```bash
K=$(mktemp -d) && node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js keygen --out "$K" --name pack-rehearsal && node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js keygen --out "$K" --name licence-rehearsal && node packages/pack-tools/dist/cli.js keygen --out "$K" --name anchor-rehearsal
docker buildx build --load --secret id=build_ca,src="$MSCD_BUILD_CA" --secret id=pack_signing_key,src="$K/pack-rehearsal.key" --build-context trusted-keys="$K" -f deploy/docker/server.Dockerfile -t mscd-server:wp18 .
docker buildx build --secret id=build_ca,src="$MSCD_BUILD_CA" --secret id=pack_signing_key,src=packages/pack-tools/dev-keys/pack-signing.key --build-context trusted-keys=packages/pack-tools/dev-keys -f deploy/docker/server.Dockerfile . 2>&1 | grep -q 'development key'
docker buildx build --load --secret id=build_ca,src="$MSCD_BUILD_CA" --target demo -f deploy/docker/server.Dockerfile -t mscd-server-demo:wp18 .
docker buildx build --load --secret id=build_ca,src="$MSCD_BUILD_CA" -f deploy/docker/web.Dockerfile --build-context nginx-config=deploy/nginx -t mscd-web:wp18 .
docker run --rm --read-only --user 10001:0 mscd-server:wp18 --help
! docker run --rm --entrypoint sh mscd-server:wp18 -c 'ls /app/apps/server/profiles/demo.yaml'
bash deploy/docker/smoke.sh --build-ca "$MSCD_BUILD_CA"
```

(The rehearsal key names follow the `<kind>-*.pub` rule of B.4 because `keygen` writes `pack-rehearsal.pub`, `licence-rehearsal.pub` and `anchor-rehearsal.pub`.)

Tests with a Mailpit container (subject, language, link), XLSX read back with `exceljs` (a title `=HYPERLINK(...)` stays text), PDF page count with `pdf-lib`, ICS line checks, a revoked token and a user who lost the role get 404, the review alert tiers with a mocked clock.

Acceptance:

```bash
npm run test:int -w @mscd/server -- test/int/notifications test/int/exports
```

## Appendix 1. Deferred items

Each item needs the product owner's sign-off before the release that would otherwise include it (column "Sign-off").

| Item | Reason | Where it would go | Sign-off |
|---|---|---|---|
| React Router, TypeScript views, per-screen hooks, TanStack Query | Parity first; the controller and generated views stay (ARCHITECTURE 14.2 steps 2, 3, 8) | Web refactor after design sign-off | Engineering lead |
| UI forms for the registers and workflows of WP-36 and WP-37, Record blocker, Add control, New document, Add audit event, Connect repository, Settings (UI 5.9) | The design has no forms yet; the commands and lists exist in the API | Web, after design | Product owner |
| Browser file pickers (MSAL OneDrive and SharePoint picker, Google Picker) and browser side of zero-transit uploads | Need real tenants and app registrations; until then link mode browses only the system's root folder with the app credential (Appendix 2, X-05) | Web, after a customer tenant exists | Product owner |
| LDAP sign-in, WebAuthn | D-23; TOTP covers PRD 13 MFA | Identity | Product owner |
| Recursive CTE Golden Thread from `traceability_chain_def`, TS 6.9 per-entity status derivations | D-11, D-12 | Read model | Product owner |
| Framework workbenches beyond the chain tables: ISO 9001 customer feedback and nonconforming output, ISO 22301 BC strategy and plan library with BR-25 (untested plan task), ISO 37001 due diligence register with BR-26 tasks, gifts and hospitality (BR-27), concerns and investigations with field-level encryption (BR-28), `product_service`, `scope` and `org_unit` tables | Pack content work-stream (PRD 15.1); the tables `bc_plan`, `exercise`, `business_associate`, `due_diligence` exist and are seeded | Registers | Product owner |
| BR-05 risk appetite escalation | Needs the A-33 risk profile format with appetite | Risk registers | Product owner |
| BR-18 regression predicates for management review inputs and customer satisfaction (sample `mr` and `csat` rules stay `manual`) | Need the history of review inputs and a customer feedback register | Drift | Product owner |
| Edition Transition Assistant, `transition.assist` job, `transition_task` table, Change Trigger Monitor, Executive Summary View | v2.0 and later scope | Framework engine, Module 3 | Product owner |
| Offering pack template updates to tenants (D-30 copies activity templates; the update offer is not built) | Needs a review screen | Framework engine | Product owner |
| Pack content depth: audit activity templates, finding conditions and document templates beyond the generic ISO 27001 sets of G.7 | Content work-stream with a domain expert (PRD 15.4) | Packs | Product owner |
| `import.run` (Bulk Import, PRD 7.1 P1) | P1 feature without UI | Jobs, registers | Product owner |
| Summary tables and `summary.verify` (TS 9.5) | Live derivation is fast enough at demo scale; needed for the scale targets of PRD 13 | Read model with k6 load tests | Engineering lead |
| Content search and `search.extract` (ARCHITECTURE 8.5) | v2.0 | Gateway, worker | Product owner |
| SMB, S3 and WebDAV adapters | v2.5 scope | Storage gateway | Product owner |
| Vault Transit, cloud KMS and HSM key drivers; Vault KV credential store | One `file` driver in this build (D-09) | Keys | Product owner and security architect |
| Dedicated download origin for file responses | F.13 headers make same-origin serving safe for the allowed types | Web and ingress | Security architect |
| Platform Operator break-glass access (TS 7.2) | Needs a second-operator approval flow | Ops | Product owner |
| `mscd-server export` (full tenant export) and verified SaaS purge | Not needed for the first release | CLI | Product owner |
| Tenant creation through an operator API (SaaS self-service) and the global email-domain directory with DNS verification (ARCHITECTURE 9.4) | CLI only in this build (`tenant create`, `tenant verify-domain`) | Ops | Product owner |
| Audit trail partitioning by month | Volume does not need it yet; expand migration later | Database | Engineering lead |
| cosign signing, SBOM in CI with a KMS key | No signing key infrastructure in the build environment | Release pipeline | Engineering lead |

## Appendix 2. Deviations and questions for the product owner

### 2.1 Deviations from the architecture and the specification

| # | Deviation | Reason | Needed sign-off |
|---|---|---|---|
| X-01 | Owner scoping is an application policy (`ScopePolicy`) on every read path, not an RLS policy (ARCHITECTURE 9.1 step 4; D-04) | Derived counts need the full data of a system; one policy class covers every read path and is tested route by route (J.5) | Security architect |
| X-02 | Sealed repository credentials are stored in `storage_credential` and passed by the caller with every gateway request, instead of the gateway fetching them (TS 4.5, ARCHITECTURE 10.4; D-08). Mitigation: the seal is bound to organisation, repository and configuration hash, the gateway recomputes the binding, logs every use, rotates its token and refuses plain HTTP outside demo mode | Keeps the gateway stateless and without database credentials (ARCHITECTURE 5.3) | Security architect |
| X-03 | SaaS uses the `file` key driver with KEKs from Kubernetes Secrets instead of `cloud_kms` (TS 2.4; D-09) | No cloud KMS in the build environment | Security architect and product owner |
| X-04 | `license.validation: online` means operator-installed signed entitlement documents, not a live registry read (ARCHITECTURE 10.8; D-32) | No licence service exists yet; the code path and enforcement points are shared | Product owner |
| X-05 | Link mode without the delegated browser picker: the api browses the system's root folder of a registered repository with the application credential, limited to users who may submit evidence for that record (ARCHITECTURE 6.1 steps 1 and 2) | Pickers need real tenants (Appendix 1) | Product owner and security architect |
| X-06 | Programme Owners verify and approve (A.4) although TS 6.1 and 6.6 name the MS Manager | A-48 second verifier; sample personas | Product owner |
| X-07 | Licence installation binding can be bypassed by a database administrator who rewrites `platform.installation` | On-premise customers control the database; the signature still prevents forged entitlements | Product owner |

### 2.2 Questions

| # | Question | Default taken in this build |
|---|---|---|
| Q-1 | UI 5.3: should moved and changed files count as "cannot be verified" in the evidence lists? | Yes in lists, no for status downgrades (A.4) |
| Q-2 | UI 5.5 and 5.6: may Internal Auditors verify and see everyone's pending list? | No (TS 7.3 and 7.6) |
| Q-3 | Clause readiness and the four maturity dimensions: what is the formula? | Stored value plus the sample formula (A.4, 5.14) |
| Q-4 | Display summaries (`cert`, `stage`, `phase`, pack label): derive or keep editable? | Stored and editable (D-06) |
| Q-5 | Is the sequential phase lock (D-13) right, or should only critical gates lock? | Sequential display lock, gate enforcement only at critical gates |
| Q-6 | Effects chosen for BR-13, BR-14, BR-15 and BR-17 (A.5); BR-05 and BR-18 predicates deferred | As A.5 |
| Q-7 | Licence model and `max_users` counting (A-39) | Active non-guest members (D-15) |
| Q-8 | Who may see pending per person (TS 7.5, HR sign-off) | MS Manager and Programme Owner; Top Management aggregates only |
| Q-9 | Should the Programme Owner keep verify and approve rights (X-06)? | Yes |
| Q-10 | Should a Tenant Admin be allowed to require SSO for all members (`local_login = 'disabled'`) by default on new SaaS organisations? | No, `allowed` by default |
